See [the exact selector Redheffer action](../0800-0899/828_the_selector_group_has_an_exact_redheffer_action_on_scattering_space_transporting_all_18_checkpoints_without_spectral_refitting.md).

The first full-response analytic audit is mixed but informative. Across 756 upper-half-plane samples of the actual rational-inner response over all eighteen selector elements, every transformed denominator stayed nonzero, every Schur defect passed exact Cholesky positivity, reciprocity held to \(5.3\times10^{-15}\), and 126 real-boundary samples stayed unitary. But the strong undoubled identity \(S_g(z)=\overline{S_g(-\bar z)}\) fails with error about \(1.97\). Real-time consistency survives only in the already established typed eight-channel completion \(\mathbb S_g=\operatorname{diag}(S_{g,+},\overline{S_{g,+}(-\bar z)})\). Selector transport therefore acts first on the positive envelope; the negative sideband is rebuilt by the antiunitary constraint. See [the sampled passivity and sideband-doubling theorem](../0800-0899/829_the_redheffer_orbit_preserves_sampled_schur_passivity_but_requires_explicit_sideband_doubling_for_real_time_reality.md).

The oriented Kantor gate gives a sharp correction. On the exact completed matter TRO \(M_{3,2}\oplus M_{2,3}\oplus M_{2,1}\oplus M_{1,2}\), the raw product \(xy^*z\) passes the first Kantor identity on all \(16^5\) basis cases but fails the second on an explicit quark-rectangle witness. It is not a Kantor triple system, so its 32-dimensional raw order-defect span cannot be promoted to \(\mathfrak g_{\pm2}\). The canonical structurable-linking product \(xy^*z+zy^*x-zx^*y\) passes both identities on all \(16^5\) cases and has a five-dimensional \(K\)-span. However, its outer symmetrization disagrees with the established TRO Jordan triple on 156 of 4096 basis triples. Thus the valid five-graded parent, if useful, is not simply the oriented product whose Jordan shadow was already known. See [the raw-TRO Kantor no-go and structurable replacement](../0800-0899/830_the_raw_furey_tro_product_fails_the_second_kantor_identity_while_its_structurable_linking_product_passes.md).

The five-grading has now been identified without exceptional assumptions. On the full phase-doubled complex TRO it is \(\mathfrak{su}(5,3)\oplus\mathfrak{su}(5,2)\oplus\mathfrak{su}(3,2)\oplus\mathfrak{su}(3,1)\), with grading dimensions \((18,32,50,32,18)\) and total dimension 150. The entrywise-real slice instead gives \(\mathfrak{so}(5,3)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(5,2)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(3,2)\oplus\mathfrak{so}(3,1)\), with \((5,16,23,16,5)\). The lepton rectangle alone closes on \(\mathfrak{so}(3,2)\cong\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb R)\), and its one-dimensional \(K\)-layer is a local orientation area form. But this occurs only after the real/CP slice; the full complex lepton closure is \(\mathfrak{su}(3,2)\), and the contact grading \((1,2,4,2,1)\) is not the \(H_2(\mathbb R)\) TKK grading \((3,4,3)\). See [the classical closure and conditional real-lepton Spin(2,3) theorem](../0800-0899/831_the_structurable_furey_five_grading_is_classical_and_spin23_appears_only_on_the_real_lepton_slice.md).

The established CP pairing does not unlock that conditional coincidence. It exchanges \(M_{2,1}\) with \(M_{1,2}\) by transpose. Their Jordan products are transpose covariant, so the paired graph remains a real Jordan subtriple. Their structurable Kantor products are not: four of eight lepton basis triples fail covariance, and the exchanged five-gradings have degree-two dimensions one and zero. The local area \(K\)-operator is therefore lost on the opposite face and cannot descend to the CP-fixed graph or the global Walsh orientation torsor. This triggers the predeclared stop condition: the oriented Kantor parent is closed as an explanation of the current \(Spin(2,3)\) response. See [the CP/Kantor incompatibility and branch closure theorem](../0800-0899/832_cp_pairing_preserves_the_lepton_jordan_graph_but_not_the_kantor_spin23_slice_closing_the_oriented_parent_gate.md).

The quarantined Freudenthal/\(E_8\) test likewise ends with a bounded negative verdict. The direct linear corner \(H_2(\mathbb R)\hookrightarrow J_3(\mathbb O)\) has identically zero Albert cubic. After adding a homogenizer \(t\) and free scale \(\kappa\), the Freudenthal quartic restricts to \(-4\kappa t^2\det h\), so it correctly lifts the already known Lorentzian determinant. But the restricted symplectic form vanishes, the quartic is even under the orientation reflection, and the complementary idempotent, octonionic direction and normalization are unsourced. The calculation supplies invariant containment without a selector or new constraint; moreover the 56-dimensional quartic system is naturally \(E_7\)-structured, with \(E_8\) only in a larger graded construction. See [the determinant-lift and Freudenthal-parent no-go](../0800-0899/833_the_freudenthal_quartic_can_homogenize_the_h2r_determinant_but_does_not_select_the_embedding_orientation_or_e8_parent.md).
## 17 October 2026 — the octonion associator is odd, but not a canonical selector

The Malcev/octonionic orientation branch has now been tested directly. The associator supplies a genuine ordered witness: in the corpus convention, \([e_1,e_2,e_4]=2e_7\), and outer reversal changes its sign. But it is an imaginary seven-vector. Its real part vanishes, its norm forgets the sign, and a signed projection requires a separately chosen imaginary direction. Meanwhile Furey's completed left-action chains compose associatively, even though collapsing octonion labels before forming the operators retains a nonzero defect. The branch is retained as a conditional frame-dependent diagnostic and closed as a canonical source of the global Walsh/Spin(2,3) orientation bit. ## 17 October 2026 — Jordan–Lie compatibility leaves one dynamical scale free

An explicit (H_2(\mathbb C)) Pauli-coordinate audit now shows that rescaling the Lie bracket by (lambda) can always be compensated by rescaling the double-bracket coefficient by (1/\lambda^2). The Jordan positive cone is independent of this choice, and a homogeneous transverse Ward quotient remains transverse for every scale. Moreover, (H_2(\mathbb R)) itself is Jordan-closed but not closed under the ambient Hermitian commutator; a dynamical claim must name its complex envelope, derivation algebra, TKK algebra or separate Poisson phase space. The missing input is a normalized action, symplectic form, contact term, quantized charge or measured residue. See [the Jordan–Lie scale audit](../0800-0899/835_jordan_lie_compatibility_and_ward_positivity_do_not_fix_the_dynamical_bracket_scale.md).

## 17 October 2026 — the selector orbit is globally passive by lossless feedback factorization

The exact transfer conjugation now factors as (S_g=K_{g^{-1}}\star S\star K_g), with (K_g\) the unitary scattering realization of the (J)-unitary Bogoliubov selector. For all eighteen selectors the fixed feedback blocks have norm (0) or (1/\sqrt5). Since the coupled rational-inner base is strictly Schur throughout the upper half-plane, both feedback inverses exist globally by a uniform Neumann bound. This closes the upper-half-plane pole and passivity gates for the constructed response; its Cayley transform is matrix Herglotz and all spectral weights are positive semidefinite. Explicit sideband doubling remains necessary for real-time reality. See [the global Redheffer theorem](../0800-0899/836_the_selector_redheffer_orbit_is_a_lossless_feedback_cascade_with_global_schur_and_positive_residue_control.md).

## 17 October 2026 — oriented color reaches the transfer carrier, not an ordinary four-amplitude scattering action

The physical-origin audit of the fixed selector networks has a split verdict. The real transfer generator (R) admits an explicit rational intertwiner with one fixed lepton line plus a three-color permutation: (RX=XP_3). This is a genuine Furey/Standard-Model-to-carrier bridge for the color generator. But the handle generator becomes non-monomial in that color basis, and the faithful order-eighteen selector group cannot embed in (S_4). At scattering level (K_R) has infinite order under ordinary matrix multiplication and order three only under Redheffer cascade. A direct four-field unitary intertwiner is therefore impossible; the open source map must land in transfer/network composition after keeping microscopic color and handle factors separate. See [the generator-typing theorem](../0800-0899/837_oriented_color_intertwines_exactly_at_transfer_level_but_not_as_an_ordinary_scattering_or_four_atom_action.md).

## 17 October 2026 — commuting microscopic color and handle exchange force a generator reassignment fork

The exact (R)-to-color intertwiner cannot extend jointly with a microscopic left–right handle flip. Those gauge operations commute, whereas (RH-HR) has full rank four; no surjective response intertwiner can change that relation. Exhaustive classification finds that the only nontrivial order-three selector elements commuting with (H) are the central (Z=(HR)^2) and (Z^2). They have no fixed vectors and trace (-2), unlike the lepton-plus-three-colors quartet with two fixed lines and trace (1). Thus either (R) retains the correct color character while (H) becomes a dressed/background response operation, or (Z) is used as a commuting color-difference quotient that kills lepton and color-sum sources. See [the commuting-generator obstruction](../0800-0899/838_commuting_microscopic_color_and_handle_exchange_cannot_descend_to_R_and_H_and_the_central_repair_loses_the_singlet_lines.md).

## 17 October 2026 — the forbidden linear handle survives as a unique quadratic symplectic relationship

Route A has a precise observable-level resolution. The three conjugate handle reflections (H_i=R^iHR^{-i}) sum to zero, so unbroken color forbids a nonzero linear handle selector. Their quadratic congruence twirl is nevertheless positive, color-covariant and unital relative to an explicit positive invariant order unit. On (Lambda^2V^*) it has characteristic polynomial (lambda^2(\lambda-1)(\lambda+1)^3) and exactly one fixed line, the canonical symplectic form (J_0). The lazy channel ((I+\mathcal F)/2) converges to that line. This turns the earlier obstruction into a relational selector mechanism: the arrows average away, while their quadratic relationship selects (Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)). The microscopic derivation of the three Kraus legs remains open. See [the quadratic twirl theorem](../0800-0899/839_color_twirling_kills_the_linear_handle_but_its_quadratic_relationship_channel_selects_the_unique_symplectic_carrier.md).

## 17 October 2026 — the known neutral TRO exchange is not the missing three-leg relationship multiplet

The first microscopic realization test for the quadratic selector is negative. The established exchanged-TRO operator (S) swaps only neutral endpoint realizations and is the identity on all sixteen named Fock states. It therefore commutes with color and has a one-element conjugacy orbit; its color average is (S), not zero. It continues to supply the Hermitian two-topology (H_2(\mathbb R)) response, but it cannot supply the three distinct (H_i) legs whose quadratic twirl selects the symplectic form. The missing object is now a color-covariant triplet of TRO relationships, likely colored, Higgs-dressed, bilocal or background-dependent. See [the exchanged-TRO comparison](../0800-0899/840_the_established_neutral_exchanged_tro_response_is_a_color_singlet_and_cannot_supply_the_three_quadratic_twirl_legs.md).

## 17 October 2026 — endpoint relabellings cannot be the microscopic twirl legs, even with arbitrary phases

The completed sixteen-arrow Furey incidence has 24 directed-support automorphisms. Exhausting all real signed involutive lifts gives 1,440 candidates and no three-member zero-sum color orbit. Extending to 8,800 exact sixth-root involutive lifts also gives none. The latter search exposes a general support obstruction: a nontrivial three-support orbit always has a singly occupied cell that cannot cancel, while a color-fixed support always contains a color-fixed occupied cell contributing the same nonzero coefficient three times. Thus no unit-phase monomial endpoint map of any order can realize the required linear cancellation. Larger cyclotomic scans are closed; the live microscopic routes are nonmonomial partial isometries, quotient-level cancellation, bilocal triplet--antitriplet kernels, and background-dressed relationships. See [the real signed classification](../0800-0899/841_the_completed_furey_tro_has_no_real_signed_endpoint_exchange_triplet_for_the_quadratic_symplectic_selector.md) and [the arbitrary-phase monomial no-go](../0800-0899/842_no_unit_phase_monomial_map_on_the_completed_furey_endpoints_can_realize_the_zero_sum_color_triplet.md).

## 17 October 2026 — quotient cancellation is feasible at \(C_3\), but full color forces an upstream doubling

An explicit split rank-four quotient now proves that the monomial no-go does not extend to partial response operations. There are maps \(\Pi:\mathbb R^8\to\mathbb R^4\) and \(J:\mathbb R^4\to\mathbb R^8\) with \(\Pi J=I\), intertwining the endpoint color cycle with \(R\). The partial lifts \(S_i=JH_i\Pi\) obey \(\sum_iS_i=0\), \(S_i^2=J\Pi\), and exact quadratic descent on every response matrix. Two of the six source invariant directions survive. But the construction is not selected: the color intertwiner space has real dimension fourteen and the equivariant section freedom dimension eight. See [the split-quotient theorem](../0800-0899/843_a_color_equivariant_split_quotient_can_lift_the_quadratic_selector_but_is_highly_nonunique_and_not_yet_gauge_typed.md).

The full gauge test then imposes a harder boundary. The natural complex \(\mathbf1\oplus\mathbf3\) quartet has no invariant alternating bilinear form and no four-real-dimensional form; the certificate finds zero invariant directions in its six-dimensional alternating-form space. The discrete cycle preserves the transported \(J_0\), but the color Cartan generators do not. Full \(SU(3)_c\) becomes symplectic only on the eight-real-dimensional realification. Hence the credible route is now upstream full-color dynamics followed by a gauge-singlet bilinear or bilocal contraction to the four-real response—not a linear full-color quotient retaining the triplet. See [the full-color symplectic obstruction](../0800-0899/844_the_discrete_color_cycle_quotient_cannot_be_promoted_to_full_su3_on_the_same_real_symplectic_four_carrier.md).

The minimal eight-real repair has now also been classified. Full color leaves exactly two invariant alternating forms: the singlet complex-plane area \(K_s\) and the triplet realification form \(K_t\). Every \(aK_s+bK_t\) with \(ab\ne0\) is symplectic, but color fixes neither their relative coefficient nor the overall scale. The invariant symmetric response space is four-dimensional, with three singlet-plane forms and one triplet metric, and no cross-sector bilinear. Thus the bilocal color trace produces two decoupled response sectors rather than the unique \(J_0\) line. A Furey exchange, Higgs dressing, Ward/UV condition, common stress coupling or Schur elimination must lock the sectors before reduction to \(Sp(4,\mathbb R)\). See [the two-scale full-color theorem](../0800-0899/845_full_color_on_the_minimal_eight_real_carrier_leaves_two_symplectic_scales_and_does_not_select_the_unique_spin23_line.md).

The neutral Furey exchange does not supply that lock. In the explicit quotient it swaps only the killed \(D,E\) directions, so its induced carrier action is the identity. More generally, full-color Schur typing gives \(\operatorname{Hom}_{SU(3)}(\mathbf1,\mathbf3)=\operatorname{Hom}_{SU(3)}(\mathbf3,\mathbf1)=0\): any linear gauge intertwiner is block diagonal and cannot identify singlet and triplet response coefficients. The exchange remains valid within each topology/gauge sector, but the relative symplectic scale must come from composite, spurion-dressed or dynamical input. See [the neutral-exchange locking no-go](../0800-0899/846_the_neutral_furey_exchange_cannot_lock_the_singlet_and_triplet_symplectic_scales.md).

A common neutral mediator also stops one step short. For composite singlet sources the Schur kernel is \(A_{\rm eff}=A-gg^T/m\); with a symmetric bare kernel it selects the line of \(g\), but never derives the projective ratio \(g_t/g_s\). Independent source rescalings carry the entire Schur complement by congruence and can make any nonzero pair of bare couplings appear equal. A true even/odd lock requires an additional composite-sector exchange satisfying \(XAX=A\) and \(Xg=\pm g\). Universal metric coupling canonically defines \(T_s+T_t\) only after action normalization; the response ratio remains the dynamical spectral ratio \(c_t/c_s\). See [the Schur/stress normalization theorem](../0800-0899/847_a_common_schur_or_stress_mediator_transmits_but_does_not_select_the_singlet_triplet_symplectic_ratio.md).

Independently, the quadratic selector is now known to be natural under the complete finite selector group. The three \(H_i\) form a full conjugacy class in the order-18 group, so their congruence average is a central class-sum channel: \(\mathcal F(g^TAg)=g^T\mathcal F(A)g\) for every selector and every bilinear. All 288 spanning identities pass, and the unique \(J_0\) line is globally selector invariant. This aligns the relationship selector with transfer conjugation and the lossless Redheffer orbit at carrier level. It does not license entrywise averaging of nonlinear scattering matrices. See [the full selector class-sum theorem](../0800-0899/848_the_quadratic_handle_twirl_is_a_central_class_sum_for_the_full_order_18_selector.md).

Revisiting the existing stress corpus yields one conditional UV benchmark. For one free conformal complex singlet and one otherwise identical complex color triplet, equal per-component normalization gives \(c_t/c_s=3\). Yet raw total-stress couplings have ratio one, canonically whitened amplitudes ratio \(\sqrt3\), and direct spectral weights ratio three. The stress-to-symplectic functor must decide which object maps to \(b/a\). All free conformal contributions also remain on one projected spin-two Jordan ray, and interactions conserve only total stress. Thus \(3:1\) is a consistency limit, not the missing lock. See [the conditional free-UV stress benchmark](../0800-0899/849_free_uv_color_multiplicity_conditionally_gives_a_three_to_one_stress_weight_but_not_the_symplectic_pencil_ratio.md).

The linear stress-to-symplectic functor has now been completely classified under independent source rescalings. Every natural linear map has \(a=\alpha C_{ss}\), \(b=\beta C_{tt}\); the mixed stress entry cannot contribute because it carries the wrong scaling character. Positivity requires \(\alpha,\beta\ge0\) but leaves \(\beta/\alpha\) free. The free kernel \(\operatorname{diag}(1,3)\) therefore maps to ratio \(3\) under a total-weight convention and ratio \(1\) under a per-component convention, both naturally. See [the linear functor classification](../0800-0899/850_rescaling_naturality_classifies_linear_stress_to_symplectic_maps_but_leaves_one_relative_normalization_free.md).

Nonlinearity alone does not help. The scale-free quotient of a positive two-sector kernel is its correlation \(\rho=C_{st}/\sqrt{C_{ss}C_{tt}}\), so \(b/a\) may be any positive function \(f(\rho)\). An exact exchange of the two composite singlet responses would force \(f=f^{-1}\) and hence the equal line, but that symmetry is not supplied by low-energy color or the neutral endpoint exchange. See [the normalized cross-stress theorem](../0800-0899/851_normalized_cross_stress_leaves_functional_freedom_unless_an_exact_composite_sector_exchange_forces_the_equal_line.md).

A larger symmetry supplies precisely the missing relation. On the realified fundamental \(\mathbf4_{\mathbb R}\) of Pati–Salam color–lepton \(SU(4)\), the invariant alternating space is one-dimensional. Restriction to \(SU(3)\) gives \(K_s+K_t\), so one leptoquark generator already forces \(a=b\). This is equal **per-component** normalization, fully compatible with total triplet weight three. It resolves the earlier multiplicity objection without claiming a triplet-to-line bijection. But the completed Furey ternary product fails the required leptoquark derivations, so \(SU(4)\) is a conditional enlarged gauge/response envelope, not an intrinsic consequence of the tested TRO. See [the conditional Pati–Salam lock](../0800-0899/852_pati_salam_su4_collapses_the_full_color_symplectic_pencil_to_the_equal_component_line_but_is_not_a_furey_tro_symmetry.md).

The protection is ultraviolet, not automatically infrared. After \(SU(4)\to SU(3)\times U(1)_{B-L}\), the invariant alternating space returns to \(\mathbb RK_s\oplus\mathbb RK_t\); the splitting \(K_s-K_t\) is symmetry allowed. A discrete transitive exchange of lepton and color coordinates would retain \(a=b\), but it moves the distinguished \(B-L\) charge and cannot survive as an ordinary low-energy internal symmetry. Pati–Salam therefore supplies a matching condition whose threshold and RG stability must be calculated, unless a spurionic or emergent response duality survives. See [the symmetry-breaking stability theorem](../0800-0899/853_su4_breaking_to_su3_times_b_minus_l_reopens_the_two_scale_pencil_unless_a_transitive_discrete_response_symmetry_survives.md).

The RG and threshold problem now has exact algebraic gates. For \(d(a,b)^T/dt=\Gamma(a,b)^T\), the equal line is invariant exactly when the two row sums of \(\Gamma\) agree. A threshold matrix preserves it exactly when its row sums agree. Full composite exchange makes \(\Gamma=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}u&v\\v&u\end{smallmatrix}\right)\) and is sufficient, but a weaker row-sum Ward identity would also work. Spurionic exchange between two vacua protects one chosen vacuum only if the vacuum is fixed or the observable identifies the orbit. See [the RG/matching row-sum theorem](../0800-0899/854_the_pati_salam_equal_line_is_rg_invariant_exactly_when_beta_and_threshold_row_sums_match.md).

The four-real reduction also has a sharp answer. No linear \(SU(3)\)-singlet quotient of \((\mathbf1\oplus\mathbf3)_{\mathbb R}\) can have rank above two. The minimal replacement is bilocal: two covariant legs produce \((\bar w_sv_s,w_t^\dagger v_t)\in\mathbb C^2\), whose realification has the canonical \(J_0\). Exact \(SU(4)\) preserves only the sum of these contractions; the two-component response appears after the \(1+3\) breaking. See [the linear no-go and bilocal carrier theorem](../0800-0899/855_no_linear_full_color_singlet_four_quotient_exists_but_the_minimal_bilocal_response_is_c2_with_canonical_j0.md).

Most importantly, the order-18 selector is now correctly typed on that bilocal \(\mathbb C^2\). The handle \(H\) is the passive complex exchange \((u,z)\leftrightarrow(z,u)\), while \(R\) is a canonical Bogoliubov transformation mixing \(z\) with \(\bar z\). All eighteen elements satisfy the two-mode canonical identities and the conversion is a group homomorphism. Because amplitudes and conjugates are gauge singlets, the action never reintroduces color charge. This joins the microscopic color contraction to the transfer/Redheffer programme at response level, while leaving dynamical generation of the exact coefficients open. See [the bilocal Bogoliubov typing theorem](../0800-0899/856_the_minimal_bilocal_c2_carrier_supports_the_full_selector_as_typed_two_mode_bogoliubov_transformations.md).

The physical second-leg candidates have now been separated by fixed-frequency image dimension. The neutral exchanged copy, constrained negative-frequency sideband, a gauge-covariant one-body propagator and a colorless Higgs alone each give at most two real response directions. Only an independent covariant source/boundary leg—or a dynamical degree of freedom equivalent to one—makes the bilocal map surjective onto \(\mathbb C^2\). Sideband reality and topology exchange remain necessary elsewhere but cannot be double-counted as the independent leg. See [the second-leg classification](../0800-0899/857_only_an_independent_covariant_second_leg_supplies_the_full_bilocal_c2_carrier_at_fixed_frequency.md).

The exact selector is also dynamically healthy in finite dimensions. Explicit symplectic logarithms generate \(R\) and \(H\) from positive-semidefinite quadratic Hamiltonians. \(R\) is a \(2\pi/3\) elliptic pulse on the second bilocal mode; \(H\) is a \(\pi\) pulse on the antisymmetric mode. Their quadratic spectra are nonnegative and the pulses generate all eighteen elements. The remaining problem is not stability but the physical origin and quantization of two timed controls. A Berry/Floquet/boundary holonomy is now a credible precisely stated branch. See [the positive-pulse construction](../0800-0899/858_positive_quadratic_hamiltonian_pulses_generate_the_exact_bilocal_selector_without_finite_dimensional_instability.md).

The three-handle channel has a smaller environment than its visible label set suggests. After whitening the common invariant metric, its three Kraus involutions sum to zero. The apparent three-label dilation therefore has Kraus rank two: its \(C_3\)-singlet environmental direction vanishes and only the real standard doublet survives. This gives an exact response-level realization of “one carrier, two faces, three relationships.” It does not yet identify the two faces with microscopic matter or code degrees of freedom. See [the minimal relationship-environment theorem](../0800-0899/859_the_three_handle_class_sum_has_a_minimal_two_dimensional_relational_environment.md).

Prakash and Ramya's Standard-Model code now meets this result at a precise boundary. Their theorem supplies an integral cubic anomaly code; the handle class sum supplies a quadratic response channel. The best bridge is not the word “three,” but a canonical two-dimensional \(C_3\) deformation or syndrome space of the punctured five-bit/Furey code whose sewing recovers the integral symplectic carrier and whose induced channel intertwines the handle twirl. Until that construction exists, the two layers are complementary rather than identical. See [the code/Kraus-layer synthesis](../0800-0899/860_the_standard_model_triorthogonal_code_and_the_two_kraus_relationship_plane_are_complementary_layers_not_yet_one_structure.md).

The first representation census shows that this doublet is not selected by the code alone. The fifteen punctured coordinates contain four real color doublets; gauge neutrality removes one but leaves three, and logical neutrality still leaves all three. Their equivariant commutant is \(M_3(\mathbb C)\), so no preferred copy exists from color, the stabilizers and the Euclidean metric. In characteristic three the constrained space contains three \(J_3\) unipotent blocks and no direct \(J_2\), whereas the active handle carrier reduces to \(J_2\). A typed Furey, Higgs, response or integral-filtration operator must therefore implement the missing \(\mathbf2^{\oplus3}\to\mathbf2\) selection. See [the punctured-code multiplicity theorem](../0800-0899/861_the_punctured_generation_code_contains_three_gauge_compatible_color_doublets_and_does_not_select_the_unique_handle_environment.md).

The positive pulse realization also fails to protect the exact finite group by itself. Positive timing rescalings preserve Hamiltonian positivity while breaking \(R^3=I\) and \(H^2=I\) linearly; generic perturbed phases have infinite order. The order-three and order-two pulse areas are isolated near their nominal values. A physical selector therefore needs a quantized holonomy, symmetry-protected Floquet spectrum, discrete boundary sewing or exact feedback return condition. See [the pulse-area protection no-go](../0800-0899/862_positive_quadratic_dynamics_does_not_protect_the_selector_pulse_areas_or_its_finite_order.md).

The established Furey incidence Laplacian supplies the first nontrivial repair of the code multiplicity obstruction. Orthogonally compressing it to the six-real-dimensional gauge-neutral color-standard space gives spectrum \((2,2,5,5,5,5)\). The simple low doublet has the explicit projector \(E_{\rm inc}=(4/3)Q_{\rm std}P_{d^c}Q_{\rm std}\). This is robust across the two existing incidence contractions and matches the representation type of the minimal handle environment up to the unitary freedom intrinsic to Kraus labels. The result remains conditional on the coordinate/Hilbert–Schmidt metric and does not yet derive the carrier Kraus operators. See [the incidence-selected code doublet](../0800-0899/863_the_furey_incidence_laplacian_conditionally_selects_one_of_the_three_code_color_doublets.md).

Saturating the integral lattice in that selected rational plane produces a stronger bridge. Color acts on it by \(C=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}1&3\\-1&-2\end{smallmatrix}\right)\), which is unimodularly conjugate to the active handle block \(R_{\rm act}=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&-1\\1&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right)\). Modulo three both become one \(J_2(1)\) block. The previous three-\(J_3\) obstruction is therefore resolved only when incidence selection, lattice saturation and prime reduction are performed in that order. The remaining problem is to supply the complementary rank-two mode, the symplectic sewing and \(H\). See [the integral incidence/handle conjugacy](../0800-0899/864_the_incidence_selected_code_doublet_is_integrally_conjugate_to_the_active_handle_c3_module.md).

The conditional rank-four sewing is now classified completely. For a primitive color-fixed rank-two complement, every color-invariant alternating form is \(J(a,d)=\operatorname{diag}(a\varepsilon,d\varepsilon)\); cross blocks vanish because \(\det(C-I)=3\). Unimodularity forces \(a,d=\pm1\), and the transported handle exchange fixes their relative sign, leaving only overall orientation. The resulting integral generators preserve \(J\) and form the exact order-18 group. This proves rigidity after the complement and exchange are supplied, but does not derive either from matter. See [the conditional integral symplectic completion](../0800-0899/865_a_fixed_rank_two_complement_and_handle_exchange_complete_the_incidence_mode_to_a_unique_unimodular_symplectic_selector.md).

The completed generation does contain a canonical primitive color-fixed complement: the \((\nu^c,\nu)\) coordinate plane. Adjoining it to the incidence-selected lattice is primitive and gives exactly the required \(I_2\oplus C\) color action. Yet \(\nu\) has \(Y=-1/2\) and \(2T_{3L}=1\), so the raw pair is not an unbroken-gauge singlet response. Moreover, Dirac exchange sends the selected active plane to a transverse doublet, while neutral topology exchange is identity on field labels. The complement exists at support level; a Higgs/bilocal/boundary dressing must still create the physical complement and \(H\). See [the neutrino-complement and exchange audit](../0800-0899/866_the_neutrino_puncture_pair_supplies_a_primitive_fixed_complement_but_neither_known_furey_exchange_supplies_the_handle.md).

The incidence selector has a quantitative but limited stability margin. Its complex multiplicity gap is three, so arbitrary self-adjoint corrections with operator norm below \(3/2\) cannot close the low/high separation. Yet any nonzero off-block correction rotates the selected line and destroys its exact rational/integral coordinates. Unequal up/down Higgs-dressed Gram weights have a nonzero commutator with \(E_{\rm inc}\), whereas equal weights are scalar. The integral bridge therefore needs a Ward, RG or topological projector-protection theorem; the gap alone gives only an approximate low doublet. See [the incidence-projector stability theorem](../0800-0899/867_the_incidence_selected_doublet_is_gap_stable_but_not_exactly_protected_against_yukawa_response_mixing.md).

The existing Furey ribbon homology does not provide that protection. Sector aggregation sends the selected lattice to zero because its coefficients sum to zero independently in every colored orbit. The ordinary six-arrow cycles \(c_C,c_D\) are color-singlet data at a coarser resolution. A topological repair must retain a three-sheet color cover or \(C_3\) local coefficients. See [the ordinary-cycle no-go](../0800-0899/868_ordinary_furey_cycle_homology_annihilates_the_incidence_selected_color_plane_and_cannot_protect_it.md).

That refinement has an exact algebraic target: the selected active lattice is the augmentation ideal \(\ker(\mathbb Z[C_3]\to\mathbb Z)\). Its color matrix is the active handle block; singlet plus augmentation has index three in the permutation lattice; and modulo three it becomes one \(J_2\). This explains the projector denominator, the saturation step and the qutrit shadow at once. The augmentation ideal canonically protects the module type, while Furey incidence still selects its embedding across the four colored sectors. See [the augmentation-ideal theorem](../0800-0899/869_the_incidence_selected_active_lattice_is_the_c3_augmentation_ideal_and_its_index_three_nonsplitting_explains_the_qutrit_shadow.md).

The full color-resolved graph now reproduces the code kernel exactly. With augmentation coefficients on \(u,d,u^c,d^c\), boundary closure at the resolved \(O_2\) vertex is \(u+d-u^c-d^c=0\). Its primitive integral kernel is \(I^3\), rank six, and equals the gauge-neutral color-standard part of the punctured code. Thus topology protects the three-doublet envelope, while the incidence Laplacian performs the remaining \(I^3\to I\) selection. See [the augmentation-cycle/code-kernel theorem](../0800-0899/870_the_color_resolved_furey_augmentation_cycle_kernel_is_exactly_the_three_doublet_gauge_neutral_code_space.md).

On that exact \(I^3\) kernel, the fifteen-field incidence selector collapses to a three-by-three multiplicity operator tensored with the identity on color. Its simple eigenvector is the primitive relation \((u,d,u^c)=(-w,-w,w)\), hence \(d^c=-3w\). The selection is therefore an exact matter-sector ratio, not merely a numerical plane in field coordinates. Its spectral derivation still uses the Hilbert--Schmidt metric. See [the incidence multiplicity reduction](../0800-0899/871_on_the_color_resolved_I3_kernel_the_incidence_selector_is_a_rank_one_multiplicity_operator.md).

The same copy has a minimal metric-free integral presentation as the cocycle kernel of \((u-d,u+u^c)\). The corresponding two-by-three matrix has Smith invariants \((1,1)\), so the selected \(I\) is primitive and the quotient is torsion free; tensoring makes the construction exactly color equivariant. This does not manufacture a physical topology, but it turns that open problem into a concrete search for two face-flatness equations or Ward identities. See [the minimal two-face cochain completion](../0800-0899/872_the_incidence_copy_is_the_primitive_cocycle_kernel_of_a_minimal_two_face_local_coefficient_completion.md).

Testing the actual planar Yukawa faces gives a sharp partial no-go. The up triangle projects to the required row \(u+u^c\), but the down triangle projects to \(d+d^c\). On the incidence copy \(d^c=3d\), every unit-sign orientation \(\pm d\pm d^c\) evaluates to \(\pm2d\) or \(\pm4d\), never zero. The missing constraint must therefore be a weak-component seam \(u-d\), or a nonprimitive winding-three relation \(3d-d^c\). See [the Yukawa-face obstruction](../0800-0899/873_the_existing_yukawa_faces_do_not_select_the_incidence_copy_and_the_missing_face_must_compare_weak_components_or_carry_winding_three.md).

The winding-three relation is canonical on the color augmentation ideal: \((1-g)(1-g^{-1})=2-g-g^{-1}=3\,1_I\). Hence a color-Laplacian face \(\Delta_{C_3}d-d^c\), together with the up face, has exactly the incidence copy as its integral kernel. Its row lattice has index two in the primitive selector lattice; saturating it recovers \(u-d=(F_u-F_{d,\Delta})/2\). This isolates a concrete possible interface with the independently unresolved ribbon/Pfaffian orientation sign, without yet identifying the two. See [the color-Laplacian factor-three theorem](../0800-0899/874_the_c3_color_laplacian_canonically_generates_the_factor_three_selector_but_leaves_an_index_two_orientation_defect.md).

The Hodge audit sharpens the scope. The \(C_3\)-invariant symmetric metric on \(I\) is unique up to scale, with primitive \(A_2\) Gram matrix, and the adjoint difference satisfies \(D^\dagger D=3I\). But \(D^\dagger Dd=d^c\) is a constitutive equation, not a consequence of \(\delta^2=0\); topology supplies the operator, not its coupling to \(d^c\). Orientation reversal leaves the Laplacian unchanged, so any Pfaffian repair must act on its first-order factorization or the saturated half-row. See [the color-Hodge claim-boundary theorem](../0800-0899/875_the_color_laplacian_factor_three_is_canonical_hodge_data_but_the_selector_equation_and_index_two_saturation_are_additional_physics.md).

The multiplicity operator itself supplies a still cleaner explanation. After switching \(u^c\mapsto-u^c\), it is exactly \(2I+L_{K_3}\). The primitive rows \(u-d\) and \(u+u^c\) are two spanning-tree edges of this signed triangle, and the selected mode synchronizes \((u,d,-u^c)=(w,w,w)\). Cycle closure then gives \(d^c=3w\) simply by summing the three locked nodes. Arbitrary positive edge reweighting preserves the exact line; the general Ward criterion is equality of signed row sums. See [the signed-\(K_3\) synchronization theorem](../0800-0899/876_the_incidence_selector_is_a_signed_k3_synchronization_laplacian_and_its_factor_three_is_the_sum_of_three_locked_matter_nodes.md).

The signed graph now has an upstream ternary derivation. Cycle closure gives the parameter Gram \(H=I+vv^T\), while the established Furey triple-incidence weights give \(K=5I+vv^T\); hence \(H^{-1}K=5I-vv^T\). More generally, diagonal weights \((a,b,c,e)\) preserve the selected line exactly iff the three primary weights satisfy \(a=b=c\); the derived \(d^c\) weight may vary freely. This converts the earlier Yukawa instability into a precise Ward condition and shows that triple incidence plus topology—not spectral reverse engineering—forces the signed triangle. See [the triple-incidence/cycle-closure derivation](../0800-0899/877_equal_furey_triple_incidence_weights_and_cycle_closure_force_the_signed_k3_selector_and_isolate_the_exact_yukawa_obstruction.md).

A fully contracted tensor construction now removes the most serious basis-dependence concern. The Hilbert--Schmidt frame norm of the Furey triple with the candidate in its distinguished middle slot gives sector weights \((u,d,u^c,d^c)=(14,14,14,4)\). Its compression is \((13/2)I+(5/2)L_{K_3}\), with the identical integral projector and a gap \(15/2\). The fully polarized and first-slot frames do not preserve the necessary equality, so the result specifically validates the relational middle \(X\) in \(A\widetilde X B\). See [the basis-independent middle-slot frame theorem](../0800-0899/878_the_basis_independent_middle_slot_frame_of_the_furey_triple_selects_the_same_signed_k3_active_module.md).

The three signed multiplicity edges and the three whitened handle involutions are moreover the same equilateral zero-sum frame: their Gram matrices differ only by a factor two, so \(e_i\mapsto O_i/\sqrt2\) is an isometry after cyclic labels are aligned. Both carry the standard real \(C_3\) module. This supplies an exact frame-level source-to-Kraus intertwiner, but with a crucial typing boundary: the matter-edge cycle permutes \((u,d,-u^c)\), selector conjugation cycles the handles, and microscopic color acts separately inside \(I\). See [the triangular-frame intertwiner and typing theorem](../0800-0899/879_the_signed_multiplicity_edges_and_the_three_handle_kraus_operators_are_the_same_zero_sum_triangle_as_abstract_frames_but_their_c3_actions_are_differently_typed.md).

At effective Gaussian level the frame bridge upgrades to an exact channel identity. The signed-triangle mismatch covariance is the standard-plane projector \(P_{\rm std}\); because the three whitened handles sum to zero, its cross-term superoperator equals the diagonal handle class sum. Factoring \(P_{\rm std}\) yields exactly the two previously derived minimal Kraus operators. The outstanding microscopic gates are now isolated: contract the color augmentation factor to a gauge singlet without retaining a four-dimensional bath, and derive the cyclic label alignment. See [the Gaussian mismatch-to-handle channel theorem](../0800-0899/880_isotropic_gaussian_reduction_of_the_signed_multiplicity_mismatch_plane_produces_the_exact_three_handle_class_sum_channel.md).

The color contraction can preserve Kraus rank, but only in the correct bilocal form. There is no invariant linear functional on the augmentation ideal, while its invariant symmetric form is unique. A factorized quadratic singlet contraction therefore duplicates each of the two relational Kraus operators only by scalar color weights; the four redundant labels still have Choi rank two and give the same normalized channel. Nonfactorizing colored response vertices remain a falsifier. See [the quadratic color-contraction theorem](../0800-0899/881_gauge_singlet_color_contraction_is_necessarily_quadratic_and_does_not_enlarge_the_two_kraus_environment_when_the_response_vertex_factorizes.md).

The required factorization is now proved for the canonical quadratic frame itself. On the full six-real color-standard kernel, both the physical Gram and middle-frame response Gram split as multiplicity matrices tensored with the unique \(A_2\) color metric; the shifted mismatch form is \((5/2)S L_{K_3}S\otimes G_{A_2}\). Thus color/multiplicity separation is not an ansatz at response-Gram level. What remains is the stronger amplitude-level alignment, which is defined only up to an auxiliary partial isometry. See [the exact middle-frame tensor-factorization theorem](../0800-0899/882_the_canonical_middle_slot_frame_factorizes_exactly_into_signed_multiplicity_response_times_the_a2_color_metric.md).

Even the amplitude existence question now closes. A minimal product factor \(L_{\rm mult}\otimes L_{\rm color}\) exists, and every other minimal factor with the same Gram differs from it by an \(O(4)\) environment rotation. Thus apparent multiplicity--color entanglement in an unobserved bath is Stinespring gauge. The Gram cannot, however, select a named tensor decomposition or align its multiplicity factor with the handle frame; those remain physical typing data. See [the minimal-amplitude gauge classification](../0800-0899/883_every_minimal_amplitude_of_the_middle_frame_mismatch_gram_is_environment_gauge_equivalent_to_a_multiplicity_times_color_factorization.md).

Environment typing reduces that ambiguity in an exact hierarchy. Physical color alone leaves a \(U(2)\) orthogonal centralizer; adding the independent multiplicity cycle leaves \(U(1)\times U(1)\). A compatible real reflection reversing both cycles removes all continuous freedom and leaves exactly four joint-plane sign choices. This gives the ribbon/Pfaffian branch a specific target action, while retaining the crucial caveat that no existing physical reflection has yet been shown to implement it. See [the environment-commutant reduction theorem](../0800-0899/884_color_and_multiplicity_cycles_reduce_environment_gauge_to_two_phases_and_a_joint_real_reflection_reduces_it_to_four_signs.md).

Auditing the known Furey involutions closes the naive realization negatively. Neutral survivor exchange is gauge-linear and preserves named matter labels, so it reverses neither environment cycle. Furey/CAR conjugation supplies the color reflection but not the multiplicity reflection and carries additional chirality/charge typing. The required joint real structure must therefore be composite: known color conjugation times a still-missing multiplicity/ribbon reversal. See [the known-real-structure typing no-go](../0800-0899/885_no_known_single_furey_exchange_supplies_the_joint_environment_reflection_color_conjugation_needs_an_independent_multiplicity_reflection.md).

The missing factor is now found at the unbroken response level. The weak Weyl swap \(u\leftrightarrow d\), with \(u^c\) fixed, reverses the signed multiplicity cycle while preserving its synchronized line, closure equation, Laplacian and canonical middle-frame Grams. Its fundamental \(SU(2)\) lift squares to the central \(-I\), but its action on weights and quadratic observables is an involution. A chosen Higgs vacuum is not fixed, so this is an exact pre-EWSB symmetry or a Higgs-covariant spurionic relation, not an unbroken infrared symmetry. See [the weak-Weyl multiplicity-reflection theorem](../0800-0899/886_the_weak_weyl_reflection_supplies_the_missing_multiplicity_cycle_reversal_before_higgs_vacuum_selection.md).

Prakash and Ramya’s new Standard-Model quantum-code construction independently anchors this conclusion. One completed generation is the sixteen even-parity weights of the \(Spin(10)\) spinor; cubic anomaly cancellation is integral triorthogonality; puncturing \(\nu^c\) gives a \([[15,1,3]]_p\) code for every prime. Its \(S_5\) automorphism group contains the weak-coordinate transposition \(q_4\leftrightarrow q_5\), and its doubling construction makes \(2T_{3L}\) the chiral linking row. The paper also imposes a major Golay boundary: ternary Golay doubling is a valid code construction but has no direct all-positive integral anomaly-free lift. See [the Standard-Model-code synthesis](../0800-0899/887_prakash_ramya_standard_model_code_places_the_project_cubic_form_weak_reflection_and_golay_boundary_on_one_exact_lattice.md).

The representation-level Furey/code intertwiner is now explicit and exact. The completed corpus module is the even five-mode Fock space, and \(q_i=1-2N_i\) reproduces every published \(Spin(10)\) weight and every Standard Model charge; the change of basis is only a permutation. Pulling back the anomaly cubic gives zero on the complete generation and the rank-one tensor \(-a^{\otimes3}\), \(a=(1,1,1,1,1)\), after puncturing \(\nu^c\). The remaining algebra question is product-level: whether the division-algebra source multiplication intertwines the five-oscillator Clifford structure. See [the exact completed-module intertwiner](../0800-0899/888_the_completed_furey_five_mode_module_is_exactly_the_spin10_triorthogonal_weight_system_and_the_punctured_anomaly_is_rank_one.md).

The 25 minimum code logicals do not contain the Furey signed triangle, closing that direct identification negatively. Yet each Furey support \((d_c^c,u_c,d_c,u_c^c)\) is an exact equal-sign \(Spin(10)\)-neutral quartet, and its incidence signs \((+,-,-,+)\) are exactly weak-center parity: negative on the weak doublet and positive on the singlets. The response relation is therefore naturally a center-twisted local-coefficient boundary, not a charge equation. See [the minimum-logical census and weak-center twist](../0800-0899/889_the_furey_incidence_quartet_is_a_weak_center_twist_of_an_exact_spin10_neutral_relation_not_one_of_the_25_minimum_code_logicals.md).

That first-order weak data resolves the old index-two selector defect. For the weak Weyl swap \(W\), \((1-W)u=u-d\) is the primitive seam, while \((1-W)^T(1-W)u=2(u-d)\). The enriched-face matrix therefore has Smith invariants \((1,2)\) precisely because quadratic response squared away the oriented flux; retaining the Weyl coboundary restores \((1,1)\). The algebraic orientation problem is closed. The physical frontier is to derive an amplitude, Higgs boundary, Pfaffian line or holonomy that retains \(1-W\) before tracing to its Gram. See [the first-order Weyl saturation theorem](../0800-0899/890_the_first_order_weak_weyl_coboundary_exactly_saturates_the_index_two_selector_defect_lost_by_quadratic_response.md).

The Higgs ray supplies that primitive response operator canonically. Its complementary projectors \(P_h,P_{\widetilde h}\) define a phase-independent, \(SU(2)\)-covariant population swap \(\Phi_h\), and \((1-\Phi_h)P_{\widetilde h}=P_{\widetilde h}-P_h\) becomes \(u-d\) in unitary gauge. Coherent unitary lifts retain one relative \(S^1\) phase; imposing determinant one forces every lift to square to the weak center \(-I\). Thus the population selector and central square are fixed, while a genuine Hopf-phase torsor remains at amplitude level. This is a kinematic Higgs construction, not yet a derived effective-action term. See [the Higgs-ray/Hopf-lift theorem](../0800-0899/891_the_higgs_ray_canonically_supplies_the_population_weyl_coboundary_while_its_coherent_su2_lift_retains_one_hopf_phase.md).

The remaining phase has a complete conditional classification. In the Higgs frame every coherent \(SU(2)\) lift is \(U(\alpha)=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&e^{i\alpha}\\-e^{-i\alpha}&0\end{smallmatrix}\right)\). Even reality and skew/Pfaffian typing both force \(\alpha=0,\pi\), hence \(U=\pm\varepsilon\); odd reality instead gives the symmetric class \(\pm i\sigma_x\). Thus a genuine Pfaffian line reduces the Hopf circle to one orientation sign. The condition is not yet derived, and full three-generation CKM violation prevents invoking exact CP without qualification. See [the Pfaffian phase-lock theorem](../0800-0899/892_pfaffian_skewness_or_even_real_structure_collapses_the_higgs_hopf_phase_to_the_two_weak_symplectic_signs.md).

Sewing that weak Pfaffian to the already-derived color metric gives \(\Omega=\varepsilon\otimes G_{A_2}\). Real color whitening sends it exactly to the canonical \(J_0\), providing the cleanest current Standard-Model-typed route to \(Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)\). Integrally, \(\operatorname{Pf}\Omega=-3\) and \(\det\Omega=9\); the form is unimodularly congruent to the earlier equal-area \(A_4\) form. The group typing does not yet match: the natural product color/reflection action leaves two index-three completions, neither the previously unique color-average line. Real carrier sewing closes; arithmetic equivariance remains a sharply localized obstruction. See [the weak-Pfaffian/color-metric sewing theorem](../0800-0899/893_the_weak_pfaffian_times_the_a2_color_metric_is_exactly_j0_over_the_reals_but_does_not_select_the_known_integral_completion_equivariantly.md).

The representation audit corrects the word “carrier” in that result. The product bath has color character \(I\oplus I\), trace \(-2\), and no fixed vector; the established carrier has \(I\oplus\mathbf1^{\oplus2}\), trace \(1\), and a fixed plane. Their characteristic polynomials differ, so no invertible real or rational color intertwiner exists. The exact \(J_0\) congruence is a form theorem for the pre-contraction mismatch bath, not yet a typed-carrier theorem. The correct route is quadratic color contraction to the rank-two relationship plane, followed by adjoining the separate neutral fixed pair. See [the color-character typing correction](../0800-0899/894_the_product_pfaffian_color_space_is_a_symplectic_bath_not_the_typed_carrier_because_its_color_character_has_no_fixed_plane.md).

That corrected route now closes at normalized-response and integral-lattice level. The invariant normalized trace \(\kappa_G(T)=\frac12\operatorname{tr}(G^{-1}T)\) sends \(\varepsilon\otimes G_{A_2}\) to the primitive relationship area \(\varepsilon\), independently of the color-metric scale. The integral incidence conjugator reverses it to \(-\varepsilon\). Sewing the ordered primitive neutrino complement \((\nu^c,\nu)\), whose determinant generator is \(\varepsilon\), gives exactly \(J_{\rm code}=\varepsilon\oplus(-\varepsilon)\), the order-18 selector form and canonical \(J_0\) after basis change. Physical equality of spectral residues and generation of the handle remain open. See [the normalized contraction and neutrino-sewing theorem](../0800-0899/895_normalized_color_contraction_produces_the_primitive_relationship_area_and_the_ordered_neutrino_pair_completes_the_exact_selector_lattice.md).

Prakash–Ramya doubling now narrows the latter gap. Its two Dirac-paired faces contain independent Weyl amplitudes, and invariant responses such as \(m_\ell=e^ce\) and \(m_q=\sum_i u_i^cu_i\) are jointly surjective onto \(\mathbb C^2\). This gives a concrete Standard-Model source for the two independent bilocal legs required by the four-real carrier. But the doubling Weyl involution preserves the lepton and color sectors separately, so face reversal acts within the two response channels and cannot induce the selector handle \(H:(m_\ell,m_q)\mapsto(m_q,m_\ell)\). The highest-value next calculation is therefore the off-diagonal response kernel of the relevant \(SO(10)\) vector-mediator channel. See [the Dirac-doubling carrier/handle boundary](../0800-0899/896_prakash_ramya_dirac_doubling_supplies_independent_bilocal_legs_but_not_the_selector_handle.md).

That vector-mediator calculation is now exact at weight and two-mode kernel level. The \(+2e_4\) channel contains \(\nu^c\nu\) and all three \(u_i^cu_i\) pairs, while \(-2e_4\) contains \(e^ce\) and all three \(d_i^cd_i\) pairs. A nonzero vector-\(\mathbf{10}\) Yukawa coupling therefore forces a common-mediator lepton–color cross term after Schur elimination. In raw integral sum coordinates its direction is \((1,1)\), and a relative-\(\pi\) rank-one pulse yields \(-H\). In orthonormal free-field coordinates the color sum contributes \(\sqrt3\), giving direction \((1,\sqrt3)\) and a different Householder reflection. Exact handle generation is now reduced to two measurable gates: equality of canonical response residues and protection of the relative \(\pi\) phase. See [the vector-mediator kernel and handle criterion](../0800-0899/897_the_so10_vector_mediator_forces_a_lepton_color_cross_kernel_but_generates_the_handle_only_after_a_normalization_and_phase_gate.md).

Unexpectedly, that \(\sqrt3\) obstruction is exactly productive for the order-three branch. If the weak-conjugate mediator channel is transported with the opposite Pfaffian orientation, the two canonical normals are \(g_\pm=(1,\pm\sqrt3)\). Their relative-\(\pi\) Householder reflections multiply to the Euclidean \(120^\circ\) rotation. The explicit orbit-basis conjugator \(P\) satisfies \(CP=PA\), with \(A=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&-1\\1&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right)\), and \(P^TP=G_{A_2}/2\). Thus the paired mediator geometry reproduces the exact integral active selector block and its color metric. The result is conditional on deriving the opposite weak sign and protecting both \(\pi\) phases; with equal orientation the product is identity. See [the paired-mediator Coxeter theorem](../0800-0899/898_color_multiplicity_and_an_oriented_weak_sign_turn_the_paired_so10_mediator_reflections_into_the_exact_a2_coxeter_generator.md).

The explicit five-mode Clebsch calculation now closes the minimal sign hypothesis negatively. Constructing the symmetric \(16\,16\to10\) map from wedge, contraction and the invariant top-form pairing gives all 40 allowed coefficients. For the two neutral weak components the down/electron coefficient vector is exactly the overall negative of the up/neutrino vector; after a common oriented color contraction their two canonical directions are projectively identical. Ordinary weak Pfaffian orientation therefore makes the two mediator reflections coincide, so their product is identity rather than \(C_3\). The conditional \(A_2\) theorem survives, but it now requires an additional color-relative local coefficient, a nonminimal mediator channel or a boundary twist. See [the explicit Clebsch sign no-go](../0800-0899/899_the_explicit_spin10_16_16_10_clebsch_map_gives_only_an_overall_weak_sign_so_the_minimal_paired_mediator_c3_route_closes_negatively.md).

The known weak-center incidence twist cannot be that additional coefficient. It is \(-1\) on every weak doublet and \(+1\) on every weak singlet, so each Dirac bilinear \(f^cf\) has character \(-1\), including neutrino, electron, up and down channels. After any color contraction its two-sector action is \(-I_2\), not \(D=\operatorname{diag}(1,-1)\). This preserves its exact role in explaining the Furey \((+,-,-,+)\) incidence signs while closing its use as a lepton/color discriminator. See [the weak-center sector-sign no-go](../0900-0999/900_the_weak_center_incidence_twist_acts_as_overall_minus_on_every_dirac_response_and_cannot_supply_the_missing_lepton_color_sign.md).

Nor can any ordinary unbroken internal gauge transformation supply \(D\) after the response operators are completed. Color acts contragrediently on a triplet and dual triplet, fixing their contraction; additive electromagnetic and \(B-L\) charges cancel; and Higgs completion cancels the weak-center sign of the bare fermion bilinear. The induced action on the completed lepton/color response pair is therefore \(I_2\). A viable \(D\) must survive as extra local-coefficient data, compare different vacua spurionically, arise from inequivalent mediator interference, or be a boundary/holonomy operation. See [the unbroken-gauge response-sign no-go](../0900-0999/901_no_unbroken_internal_gauge_transformation_can_supply_the_missing_sign_on_completed_lepton_and_color_dirac_responses.md).

The corpus already contains precisely such extra response data. On the ordered Higgs-dressed atoms \((u,\nu,d,e)\), the established Furey-handle and Higgs-channel gradings are \(h=(+,-,-,+)\) and \(\chi=(+,+,-,-)\). Their product \(h\chi=(+,-,+,-)\) is quark/lepton parity. Normalized aggregation to \((L,Q)=((\nu+e)/\sqrt2,(u+d)/\sqrt2)\) sends it to \(-D\), projectively the missing sign. The channel translation simultaneously becomes the exchange \(X\), and the two anticommute as a real Pauli pair; Walsh duality exchanges their sign/exchange roles. Applying \(h\chi\) to one minimal-\(\mathbf{10}\) mediator face produces the second \(A_2\) root normal and reopens document 898 algebraically. The open physics is now the one-face insertion and \(\pi\)-phase protection, not existence of the sign. See [the handle-times-Higgs grading theorem](../0900-0999/902_the_product_of_furey_handle_and_higgs_channel_gradings_is_exactly_the_missing_lepton_color_sign_and_conditionally_reopens_the_a2_route.md).

The carrier audit imposes one essential qualification. On the real aggregate plane, both \(D\) and \(X\) have determinant \(-1\) and reverse the area; only their paired Coxeter product is symplectic. Complexifying them makes each operation symplectic on four quadratures, but also doubles the \(C_3\) action, giving trace \(-2\) and no fixed plane rather than the typed selector's trace \(1\) and fixed rank two. Thus \(h\chi\) remains a credible source of the active \(R\) block, while the final handle \(H\) must still come from the separately adjoined neutral plane and orientation-reversing sewing. See [the active-plane/complex-carrier typing theorem](../0900-0999/903_the_hchi_sign_can_source_the_real_active_c3_plane_but_its_exchange_dual_is_not_the_selector_handle_on_that_same_plane.md).

The existing rational-inner response now provides an exact analytic realization of the two active reflections. At frequency \(\omega_0\), positive pole scales \(\gamma_n=(1+\sqrt2)\omega_0\) and \(\gamma_t=(\sqrt2-1)\omega_0\) give eigenphases \(+i\) and \(-i\), so the response is \(-i\) times the required Householder reflection. Two same-frequency faces therefore yield \(-C_3\), of order six. Real-time sideband symmetry gives the opposite face the conjugate phase, and the oriented cascade \(D S(-\omega_0)D\,S(+\omega_0)\) cancels the common phase and equals the exact \(A_2\) Coxeter element. The remaining physical gate is a coherent sideband-converting boundary/Floquet process; the silver-ratio pole scales are exact construction data, not predicted masses. See [the sideband-reversing rational-inner Coxeter cycle](../0900-0999/904_a_sideband_reversing_rational_inner_boundary_cycle_realizes_the_exact_a2_coxeter_element_with_positive_pole_scales.md).

Gauge superselection fixes where that sideband conversion may occur. On a charged microscopic Nambu pair the exchange carries anomalous charge \(\pm2Q\) and fails to commute with continuous gauge phase unless a charge-two spurion is present. The Higgs-dressed and color-contracted Dirac response modes are neutral, so their sideband exchange is gauge compatible. The correct ordering is therefore matter \(\to\) Higgs/color completion \(\to\) neutral composite response \(\to\) frequency-converting boundary. The constrained negative sideband is not an additional particle or independent bilocal leg. See [the neutral-response sideband typing theorem](../0900-0999/905_the_sideband_reversing_a2_cycle_is_gauge_compatible_only_downstream_of_higgs_dressing_and_color_contraction.md).

Canonical typing now distinguishes the exact theorem from a device claim. The exchange matrix reverses the bosonic Nambu metric, \(X^T\eta_BX=-\eta_B\), so it cannot be a passive or finite stable one-mode bosonic Gaussian evolution. It is exact as the antiunitary real-time sewing \(XK\): a boundary condition relating the two analytic faces of one real response. One might attempt an enlarged neutral annihilation/idler realization, but it still needs an explicit source of the real-structure reduction. Thus the active \(C_3\) result stands first as a boundary-monodromy theorem. See [the sideband implementation classification](../0900-0999/906_the_sideband_exchange_in_the_a2_cycle_is_an_antiunitary_boundary_sewing_not_a_single_mode_passive_bosonic_evolution.md).

That prospective dilation has now been audited and narrowed. A complex-linear unitary with a fixed ancilla cannot equal conjugation because it obeys \(T(iz)=iT(z)\), while \(K(iz)=-iK(z)\); quantum mechanically, transposition has a negative Choi eigenvalue and is not completely positive. Two neutral modes permit a unitary swap only after a real source or boundary has already supplied \((z,\bar z)\). The highest-value target is therefore a local boundary action imposing the real structure, or a supplied-port scattering geometry—not a passive idler claimed to manufacture \(K\). Approximate phase conjugation remains possible only with its added noise or conditioning made explicit. See [the antiunitary-dilation no-go](../0900-0999/907_a_neutral_idler_swap_routes_but_cannot_generate_the_antiunitary_sideband_sewing.md).

That local target now has an exact effective model. Two real neutral response fields on oppositely oriented half-spaces may be sewn by \(q_2=Dq_1\) and \(p_2=Dp_1\). Orthogonality of \(D\) cancels the variational boundary term and preserves quadratic flux; Fourier reality then gives \(q_2(-\omega)=D\overline{q_1(+\omega)}\), precisely the required antiunitary sideband twist. Combined with the rational-inner faces it yields the exact \(A_2\) Coxeter element. The remaining high-value problem is no longer existence of a local interface, but deriving these neutral response fields and the \(D=h\chi\) matching condition from microscopic Standard-Model/Furey data. See [the local real-interface construction](../0900-0999/908_a_local_real_interface_supplies_the_antiunitary_sideband_twist_and_closes_the_boundary_monodromy_model.md).

Protection separates into three layers. The orientation-reversing component of the interface is topological because \(\pi_0(O(2))\cong\mathbb Z_2\), but its reflection axis moves continuously; determinant parity alone does not preserve the exact order-three angle. Requiring an integral \(A_2\) isometry reduces the circle to the six reflections of \(O(G_{A_2},\mathbb Z)\cong D_6\). Of these, two give the identity mediator product and four give order three. The transported $h\chi$ sign is exactly the integral reflection \(B=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}1&-1\\0&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right)\), selecting the named Coxeter action. Topology protects the component, arithmetic protects a finite menu, and the Furey/Higgs grading chooses the element. See [the topological limit](../0900-0999/909_topology_protects_the_orientation_reversing_interface_component_but_not_the_exact_a2_coxeter_angle.md) and [the integral normalizer census](../0900-0999/910_the_integral_a2_lattice_discretizes_the_interface_axis_but_hchi_is_still_needed_to_select_the_order_three_reflection.md).

Quadratic radiative stability has an exact conditional answer. On the aggregate plane, $D=h\chi$ invariance removes lepton--color mixing but still permits unequal diagonal residues. The compressed channel exchange $X$ interchanges those residues. Since $DX=-XD$, the joint commutant is only the scalars: every symmetric invariant counterterm is $kI$. If both operations are true defect symmetries, they protect the canonical $(1,\sqrt3)$ mediator direction and hence the $A_2$ angle. The unresolved gate is physical, not algebraic: $X$ is a response duality rather than an established unbroken gauge symmetry, so its Ward action on the defect generating functional must still be derived. See [the crossed-commutant protection theorem](../0900-0999/911_crossed_hchi_and_channel_exchange_symmetry_rigidify_the_active_response_metric_and_protect_the_a2_angle.md).

The independent neutral-plane branch has now advanced at effective-boundary level. The known full handle $H$ is symplectic on the primitive fixed-plus-active carrier, so its graph is automatically Lagrangian in the doubled boundary space $V^-\oplus V$; the graph lattice is primitive because its upper block is $I_4$. Thus $H$ defines an exact local canonical sewing without being misidentified as an unbroken internal gauge transformation. Averaging over the order-eighteen selector also gives a positive energy metric. See [the primitive Lagrangian handle defect](../0900-0999/912_the_full_selector_handle_is_a_primitive_lagrangian_graph_defect_with_an_invariant_positive_energy_metric.md).

That positive form is unique up to scale. The simultaneous $R/H$-invariant symmetric-form space is one-dimensional, generated by a primitive positive integral metric $G_0$ of determinant nine. Conditional on exact selector Ward symmetry, this fixes the neutral/active residue ratio and closes the normalization freedom left by the lattice sewing. Together $J$ and $G_0$ determine a compatible complex structure $\mathcal I=\sqrt3G_0^{-1}J$. Its full selector centralizer is exactly $\mathbb R[I,\mathcal I]\cong\mathbb C$, and the symplectic centralizer is only $U(1)$. The finite selector therefore fixes a compact complex frame inside—not equal to—the full noncompact $Sp(4,\mathbb R)$ carrier group. See [the unique invariant metric](../0900-0999/913_the_full_selector_fixes_a_unique_positive_response_metric_and_a_compatible_complex_structure.md) and [the exact centralizer classification](../0900-0999/914_the_full_selector_centralizer_is_complex_scalar_and_its_commuting_symplectic_dynamics_is_only_u1.md).

The unique complex structure is now typed against the Jordan/Furey constructions. On the active plane its integral generator is exactly $\mathcal I_{0,\rm act}=-(I+2A)$, the Eisenstein orientation of the order-three cycle; the handle forces $\mathcal I_{0,\nu}=X\mathcal I_{0,\rm act}X$ on the neutral plane. A real symmetric middle propagator or positive frame cannot square to $-3I$, so neither can equal this orientation directly. The correct chain is symmetric Jordan propagator $\to$ positive response metric $G_0$, alternating sewing $\to J$, and only then $\mathcal I_0=3G_0^{-1}J$. The neutral Furey Peirce multiplication-by-$i$ has the same orbit type, but a source-native intertwiner preserving the metric, determinant area, Higgs dressing and handle transport remains open. See [the middle-element/orientation typing theorem](../0900-0999/915_the_selector_complex_structure_is_the_eisenstein_orientation_of_the_active_cycle_not_a_symmetric_jordan_middle_element.md).

The neutral Peirce intertwiner is now completely classified. Complex-structure matching alone gives $S(v)=(v,-I_\nu v)$ and a normalized $U(1)$ torsor. Requiring the source conjugation and the integral selector reflection to intertwine collapses the circle to $S=\pm S_0$, with $S_0=P^{-1}/\sqrt2$. The same matrix $P$ that conjugates the Euclidean mediator cycle to the integral active $A_2$ lattice therefore identifies the neutral Peirce complex/conjugation pair with the selector neutral block. This removes arbitrary $GL(2)$ matching, conditional on a microscopic identification of the real structures. See [the Peirce/selector intertwiner classification](../0900-0999/916_the_neutral_peirce_to_selector_intertwiner_is_a_u1_torsor_collapsing_to_two_signs_when_real_structure_is_preserved.md).

The minimal two mediator faces generate $C_3$ but their projector sum is $\operatorname{diag}(2,6)$, so they do not by themselves protect the response metric. Closing the normals under their own Coxeter action produces the third root $(-2,0)$; the three equal-weight projectors sum to $6I$. This completed orbit is exactly the same zero-sum $A_2$ frame as the signed multiplicity edges and whitened handle Kraus operators: their Gram matrices and cyclic actions coincide after the fixed $\sqrt2$ normalization. Thus “two faces, three relationships” is now an exact tight-frame theorem. The pinned dependency is a typed Feshbach/Jordan map identifying mediator monodromy with the existing handle class-sum channel. See [the orbit-completion theorem](../0900-0999/917_the_two_mediator_faces_generate_c3_but_only_the_completed_three_root_a2_orbit_protects_the_response_metric.md) and [the three-way frame intertwiner](../0900-0999/918_the_mediator_root_orbit_multiplicity_edges_and_handle_kraus_operators_are_one_three_way_a2_frame.md).

The third relationship is now generated by the Jordan/Feshbach mechanism itself. Since $g_++g_-=(2,0)=-g_0$, the quadratic self-energy satisfies $\Sigma(g_++g_-)=g_0g_0^T$; subtracting the two primitive self-energies yields the symmetrized cross term, exactly the Jordan polarization. Hence no third elementary mediator is required. The phase audit is essential: the equal-norm root needs in-phase coupling coherence $\theta_c=0$, whereas the Householder pulse uses a $\pi$ eigenchannel difference and the two sidebands carry opposite common phases. These are three distinct variables. The third projector may be reconstructed operationally from controlled quadratic evaluations or protected by a full class-sum Ward symmetry; an autonomous single device still needs a phase-referenced common Feshbach node. See [the coherent Jordan generation theorem](../0900-0999/919_the_third_a2_relationship_is_the_coherent_two_face_feshbach_self_energy_and_its_cross_term_is_the_jordan_polarization.md) and [the three-phase ledger](../0900-0999/920_the_third_relationship_requires_in_phase_coupling_coherence_not_the_pi_pulse_or_opposite_sideband_common_phase.md).

The completed root frame now supplies the exact handle channel, not only an isomorphic label plane. Its synthesis matrix obeys $M^TM=6I_2$ and $MM^T=6P_{\rm std}$, so an isotropic mediator-plane Gaussian pushes forward to the precise covariance whose coupling to the whitened handles is the central class-sum superoperator. On the canonical Furey middle-frame route the color gate is already closed: the raw covariance factorizes as $P_{\rm std}\otimes G_{A_2}$, the unique quadratic color trace returns $P_{\rm std}$, and redundant color Kraus labels retain Choi rank two. The remaining frame obstruction is physical cyclic label identification, plus autonomous inclusion/equal weighting of the complete orbit. See [the mediator-Gaussian handle-channel theorem](../0900-0999/921_the_completed_mediator_root_gaussian_pushes_forward_to_the_exact_handle_class_sum_channel.md) and [the canonical color-gate closure](../0900-0999/922_for_the_canonical_middle_frame_the_color_contraction_gate_is_closed_and_only_physical_cyclic_typing_remains.md).

That label obstruction is now finite and explicitly classified. The three equilateral frames admit exactly six dihedral alignments. Pairing the unique pure-lepton coherent root with the base handle attached to the neutrino complement fixes the cyclic offset and leaves two orientations; the weak/Pfaffian ordering of the up/neutrino and down/electron faces selects one. The resulting map is $(g_+,g_0,g_-)\mapsto(O_2,O_0,O_1)$, with the reversed weak orientation giving the sole alternative. No continuous Kraus or frame rotation remains. The open physics is reduced to deriving those two named anchors in one microscopic boundary action. See [the dihedral alignment theorem](../0900-0999/923_a_neutrino_lepton_anchor_and_one_weak_orientation_bit_uniquely_align_the_three_a2_frames.md).

The separate full-handle dynamics also closes at causal effective-transfer level. Because $H$ is an involutive isometry of the unique selector metric $G_0$, the degree-zero transfer $S(z)=H$ is entire, real-time compatible and lossless. Its $G_0$-self-adjointness makes the power-normalized matrix symmetric and orthogonal, so it is a reciprocal instantaneous feedthrough with two $+1$ and two $-1$ ports. Unlike the active $C_3$ cycle, it needs no pole tuning, sideband conversion or auxiliary mode. The remaining issue is microscopic boundary selection and derivation of the physical $G_0$ power metric. See [the constant rational-inner handle realization](../0900-0999/924_the_full_handle_graph_is_a_constant_reciprocal_rational_inner_feedthrough_in_the_unique_selector_metric.md).

The two effective pieces now form one explicit four-channel lossless network. The dispersive block $R(\omega)=I_2\oplus T^{-1}[DS(-\omega)DS(+\omega)]T$ and the constant handle $H$ both preserve the unique power metric $G_0$ on the real-frequency axis. At $\omega_0$, they are exactly the integral selector generators and generate the order-eighteen group. Away from that point the network remains lossless but $R(\omega)^3\ne I$: the finite selector is a checkpoint monodromy, not a broadband internal symmetry. See [the complete checkpoint network](../0900-0999/925_the_dispersive_active_cycle_and_static_handle_form_one_lossless_network_realizing_the_order18_selector_only_at_the_checkpoint.md).

That checkpoint is nevertheless much flatter than generic tuning. Reciprocal pole placement $\gamma_n\gamma_t=\omega_0^2$ cancels the first derivative of the relative eigenphase, while the silver quotient fixes its stationary value to $-\pi$. Hence the generator drifts as $O(\epsilon^2)$. The exact cascade trace law $\operatorname{tr}C=\tfrac12+\tfrac32\cos\Delta$ supplies a second stationarity, so $\|R^3-I\|=O(\epsilon^4)$. This is double stationary-point robustness, not topology: pole-product drift, loss and microscopic selection of $\omega_0$ remain open. See [the reciprocal-pole detuning theorem](../0900-0999/926_the_reciprocal_silver_pole_pair_makes_the_selector_checkpoint_first_order_detuning_protected_but_not_broadband.md).

In the enlarged frequency/pole space, that silver checkpoint is the vertex of an exact locking curve rather than an isolated solution. With $y=\log(\sqrt{\gamma_n\gamma_t}/\omega)$ and $s=\frac12\log(\gamma_n/\gamma_t)$, the two-face cascade has exact order three iff $\sinh s=\cosh y$. Its local form is $s-s_0=y^2/(2\sqrt2)+O(y^4)$. Common pole-center/frequency mismatch is therefore tangent and first-order protected, while pole-separation error is normal and first-order dangerous. A Ward identity need only preserve this relation—not necessarily predict an absolute mass scale—but no microscopic functional currently does so. See [the selector-locking manifold](../0900-0999/927_the_silver_checkpoint_is_the_vertex_of_an_exact_selector_locking_manifold_with_protected_tangent_and_unprotected_normal.md).

Strict passive loss supplies a hard boundary rather than another direction on that manifold. Splitting each inner zero/pole radius by a nonnegative loss parameter makes the cycle determinant a product of factors strictly below one. Since $C^3=I$ requires $(\det C)^3=1$, no frequency or pole-ratio retuning can restore exact finite order in the reduced lossy two-channel block. Exact selector action may still live in a larger lossless dilation or a decoherence-free subspace, but tracing environmental ports destroys it on the observed carrier. See [the passive-loss determinant no-go](../0900-0999/928_strict_passive_loss_destroys_exact_finite_order_in_the_reduced_selector_network.md).

The coherent-dilation escape hatch is nonempty. A two-channel contraction is a compression of an order-three unitary exactly when its unique operator barycentric coordinates $P_0,P_1,P_2$ at $1,\zeta,\zeta^2$ are positive. All 81 tested points in the passive-loss quadrant satisfy this criterion. At equal loss $0.05$, all three $P_k$ have rank two, giving an explicit minimal six-port Naimark network with $U^3=I_6$ and $V^*UV=C$. The reduced block still fails finite order because its subspace is not invariant. This closes active-cycle dilation existence at one strict-loss witness but leaves the neutral-plane/handle lift—and hence the enlarged order-eighteen group—open. See [the six-port order-three dilation](../0900-0999/929_the_lossy_active_cycle_has_an_exact_six_port_order_three_naimark_dilation_but_the_handle_lift_is_not_automatic.md).

That handle lift now closes exactly. The three positive effects are realized respectively by a trivial/sign pair and the two conjugate centrally twisted standard representations of $C_3\times S_3$. Giving every active effect range an orthogonal neutral handle partner produces a twelve-dimensional unitary representation with $\widetilde R^3=\widetilde H^2=I$ and exhaustive group order eighteen. An explicit isometry compresses it to $I_2\oplus C_{\rm loss}$ and the exact physical handle swap; indeed the enlarged handle intertwines the embedded four-carrier without leakage. At the lossless rank pattern $(0,1,1)$ the construction collapses back to four dimensions. This is a finite checkpoint representation theorem, not yet a local twelve-port transfer or matter interpretation. See [the exact order-eighteen coherent completion](../0900-0999/930_the_lossy_four_channel_selector_is_the_compression_of_an_exact_twelve_port_order18_unitary_representation.md).

The enlarged checkpoint also passes the causal-transfer gate without adding dynamics. In the character-adapted port basis, both $\widetilde R$ and $\widetilde H$ are symmetric unitary matrices, so the constant transfers $S_R(z)=\widetilde R$ and $S_H(z)=\widetilde H$ are entire reciprocal rational inner. Pairing each with its conjugate sideband gives an exact $\Xi K$ real-time completion, still of group order eighteen. This closes checkpoint realization only. A broadband dilation of $C(\omega)$ would require one fixed isometry and an analytic rational-inner spectral factor; pointwise Naimark factors generally vary with frequency and do not prove that stronger statement. See [the degree-zero twelve-port checkpoint network](../0900-0999/931_the_enlarged_order18_checkpoint_is_a_constant_reciprocal_inner_network_but_not_yet_a_dilation_of_the_full_dispersive_law.md).

The three environmental sectors now meet the earlier $A_2$ triangle at exactly the right categorical level. The mediator roots live in the zero-sum subspace of a three-label permutation module; the three-point Fourier transform sends its projector to $\operatorname{diag}(0,1,1)$, the two nontrivial $C_3$ characters. This exactly matches the support of the lossless dilation. Strict loss activates the formerly absent trivial-character sector. But the positive effects are not the root tight frame: their rank patterns are respectively $(1,1,1)$, $(0,1,1)$ and $(2,2,2)$ for roots, lossless spectral effects and strict-loss effects. The bridge is orbit/character Fourier duality, not equality of POVMs or particle content. See [the Fourier-dual label theorem](../0900-0999/932_the_dilation_sectors_are_fourier_dual_to_the_a2_root_labels_but_are_not_the_same_three_effect_frame.md).

That Fourier bridge now has an exact minimal network.
