On $\mathbb C^3_{\rm label}\otimes\mathbb C^2$, propagation is a nearest-neighbor cyclic shift in the orbit basis and $\operatorname{diag}(1,\zeta,\zeta^2)\otimes I_2$ in the character basis. Factoring the response effects as $P_k=B_k^*B_k$ gives a fixed observed-port embedding whose two descriptions compress to the same lossy cycle. The orbit couplings obey $\sum_jW_j=\sqrt3B_0$: the lossless port is exactly zero-sum, while strict loss is precisely activation of the common label mode. The $B_k$ are still reconstructed from response data rather than derived from Furey dynamics. See [the orbit-local character-diagonal boundary network](../0900-0999/933_a_minimal_three_label_boundary_network_is_orbit_local_character_diagonal_and_activates_the_common_mode_exactly_with_loss.md).

The cyclic update itself has a sharp implementation dichotomy. Its autonomous Hermitian logarithm is $\frac{2\pi}{3\sqrt3}i$ times the oriented triangle adjacency; complex conjugation reverses both the flux and cycle, and no real-symmetric reciprocal autonomous generator can produce the nonsymmetric directed shift. Alternatively, two local symmetric orthogonal swaps generate the cycle as an ordered word, and reversing their order reverses its orientation. This is the exact three-label form of “one twist” versus “two faces”: direction lives either in a chiral coefficient or in temporal/boundary ordering. See [the local shift realization theorem](../0900-0999/934_the_three_label_shift_requires_an_oriented_autonomous_twist_or_two_ordered_reciprocal_faces.md).

The two-swap branch is now exactly intertwined with the mediator construction. With $Q=M/\sqrt6$, restriction to the zero-sum plane gives $Q^TS_{01}Q=-H_{g_-}$, $Q^TS_{12}Q=-H_{g_+}$ and $Q^TS_{02}Q=-H_{g_0}$. Hence the two central minus signs cancel and $Q^T(S_{01}S_{12})Q=H_{g_-}H_{g_+}$, while reversed order gives the inverse cycle. The third local swap is the face associated with the coherently Jordan-generated third root. This closes the algebraic diagram from label-local faces to Standard-Model-motivated mediator reflections; only the physical clock/traversal mechanism remains. See [the exact swap/Householder intertwiner](../0900-0999/935_the_two_local_label_swaps_are_exactly_the_two_mediator_householder_faces_on_the_zero_sum_plane_up_to_canceling_signs.md).

Each swap now has a minimal positive local action. The pulse Hamiltonian $K_{ab}=\frac\pi2(I-S_{ab})$ is a rank-one edge Laplacian and satisfies $e^{-iK_{ab}}=S_{ab}$. On the active plane the two primitive pulses restrict to $\frac\pi2(I+H_{g_-})$ and $\frac\pi2(I+H_{g_+})$; their ordered unit pulses give the exact Coxeter cycle without negative energy or non-Hermitian dynamics. The remaining control data are explicit: integrated area $\pi$, pulse order and inter-pulse coherence. See [the minimal positive edge-pulse action](../0900-0999/936_two_positive_rank_one_edge_pulses_are_the_minimal_local_action_for_the_two_face_selector_cycle.md).

An orientation audit then imposes a necessary correction. The $h\chi$ reflection is exactly $S_{02}$ on label space, so it conjugates $K_{01}\leftrightarrow K_{12}$ and $T\leftrightarrow T^{-1}$. It selects the named lepton/color reflection axis but cannot, while unbroken, select clockwise over counterclockwise traversal. The orientation observable $(T-T^{-1})/2$ is $h\chi$-odd. A second odd spurion—plausibly the weak/Pfaffian sign—or spontaneous/boundary orientation is required. See [the $h\chi$ pulse-order no-go](../0900-0999/937_hchi_exchanges_the_two_primitive_pulses_and_reverses_the_cycle_so_it_cannot_alone_select_orientation.md).

The Pfaffian candidate has now passed the exact parity test but not the dynamical one. Since $D\varepsilon D^T=-\varepsilon$ and $D_{\rm label}\Omega D_{\rm label}=-\Omega$, the weak Pfaffian coefficient $\eta_{\rm Pf}$ and cycle orientation are the same odd sign representation. The Hermitian term $\eta_{\rm Pf}i\Omega$ is therefore invariant and, at a tuned coefficient, its two signs generate $T$ and $T^{-1}$. Nothing yet forces the coefficient to be nonzero, selects a Pfaffian vacuum, or quantizes its magnitude. The earlier frame alignment must consequently be read as conditional on a chosen/spontaneously selected sign. See [the Pfaffian-to-cycle pairing audit](../0900-0999/938_the_weak_pfaffian_sign_has_exactly_the_right_odd_parity_to_pair_with_cycle_orientation_but_the_coupling_is_not_yet_derived.md).

The pointwise lossy-dilation branch is now global rather than census-based. Writing the two attenuations as $p=e^{-\ell_n}$ and $q=e^{-\ell_t}$ reduces positivity of all three $C_3$ barycentric effects to five scalar polynomials on the unit square. Their exact Bernstein expansions over $\mathbb Q(\sqrt2)$ have no negative coefficients: the four diagonal-bound numerators have bidegree $(8,8)$ and the determinant numerator has bidegree $(16,16)$. The sole determinant zero occurs at the lossless corner. Thus every nonzero passive attenuation gives three rank-two effects and a minimal six-port order-three dilation; the twelve-port full-selector extension applies pointwise throughout the passive quadrant. This still does not construct a single broadband analytic dilation with fixed ports. See [the global passive-loss dilation theorem](../0900-0999/939_the_full_passive_silver_loss_family_has_positive_c3_barycentric_effects_and_exact_six_port_dilations.md).

Prakash and Ramya's qutrit gate now gives the sharpest arithmetic candidate for protecting the still-tuned selector angle. Their anomaly-protected transversal gate is $U=\operatorname{diag}(1,\zeta_9,\zeta_9^{-1})$, so $U^3=\operatorname{diag}(1,\omega,\omega^{-1})$ has exactly the order-three character spectrum used by the boundary dilation. Logical negation reverses it, and $\langle U^3,X\rangle\cong S_3$. This could supply the selector's noncentral $S_3$ factor if a typed logical-to-response intertwiner exists. Equal group order does not close the bridge: $\langle U,X\rangle\cong D_9$ has order eighteen but is not the established $C_3\times S_3$ selector, as witnessed by its order-nine elements and trivial center. See [the qutrit anomaly-phase/selector comparison](../0900-0999/940_the_prakash_ramya_qutrit_anomaly_gate_contains_a_protected_c3_phase_but_its_order18_extension_is_not_the_selector_group.md).

The required character-to-response map now exists canonically at observable level. The unique UCP map $\Phi:\mathbb C^3\to M_2(\mathbb C)$ defined by $\Phi(E_k)=P_k$ sends the anomaly-protected cubed phase exactly to the passive selector cycle and obeys $D\Phi(a)D=\Phi(Xa)$. At zero loss it descends to a *-representation of the two-character quotient and even transports the ninth-root lift. Under strict loss it becomes nonmultiplicative, with Kadison defect $I-C^*C$, so the protected group law resides in the coherent Stinespring/Naimark carrier rather than its dissipative compression. This closes structural existence and reflection covariance while leaving source-dynamical derivation of the map open. See [the qutrit-character UCP intertwiner](../0900-0999/941_the_qutrit_character_algebra_maps_canonically_to_the_selector_povm_but_transports_the_protected_group_law_only_losslessly.md).

The lossless ninth-root lift now has a complete full-carrier classification. If $t=2\cos(2\pi/9)$ and $C$ is the active integral Coxeter block, then $Q=(C+tI)/(t^2-1)$ satisfies $Q^3=C$ and $Q^9=I$. The four-carrier root $W=I_2\oplus Q$ is symplectic; handle conjugation transports it to the other face, and the closure is $(C_9\times C_9)\rtimes C_2$ of order 162. Cubing the two phase factors gives $(C_3\times C_3)\rtimes C_2\cong C_3\times S_3$, exactly the established selector. This is an exact ancestry theorem over the real cubic field, not over the primitive lattice: a rational order-nine matrix would require the degree-six factor $\Phi_9$, impossible in dimension four. See [the cyclotomic ninth-root extension](../0900-0999/942_the_order18_selector_is_the_cubed_shadow_of_a_cyclotomic_order162_ninth_root_extension_but_the_root_is_not_integral.md).

Restriction of scalars makes the arithmetic boundary even sharper. The two-dimensional ninth-root module over the real cubic field becomes a six-dimensional integral rational module with cubed character multiplicities $(0,3,3)$; the full four-dimensional field-valued carrier becomes twelve-dimensional with $(6,3,3)$. These dimensions match the strict-loss active and full Naimark carriers, but the latter have multiplicities $(2,2,2)$ and $(4,4,4)$. Hence no invertible intertwiner identifies them. Cyclotomic restriction gives three copies of the lossless nontrivial-character plane, whereas passive loss genuinely activates the trivial/common sector. Any unification must be a noninvertible channel with added environment structure. See [the restriction-of-scalars character obstruction](../0900-0999/943_restriction_of_scalars_explains_the_six_and_twelve_dimensional_cyclotomic_sizes_but_not_the_lossy_dilation_characters.md).

That minimal channel now exists and independently recovers the programme's two-face environment. A covariant Stinespring embedding from source multiplicities $(0,3,3)$ to target $(2,2,2)$ needs environment dimension at least two. Reflection stability leaves $2\chi_0$ or $\chi_1\oplus\chi_2$; only the nontrivial pair can absorb source charge while populating the target common character. It is therefore uniquely selected by common-mode activation. The resulting twelve-dimensional tensor representation has multiplicities $(4,4,4)$, exactly the full selector dilation, and an explicit reflection-equivariant isometry sends the maximally mixed source to $I_6/6$. This closes the representation-level code/environment bridge anticipated in documents 859–860 while leaving equality of microscopic Kraus amplitudes open. See [the minimal $A_2$-environment channel](../0900-0999/944_a_unique_minimal_a2_doublet_environment_embeds_the_cyclotomic_source_into_the_balanced_selector_dilation.md).

The channel-to-carrier compression now closes algebraically. The six-dimensional character channel is a $3+3$ dephasing channel after an output permutation. The four-dimensional handle class-sum channel becomes a $2+2$ dephasing channel after a real Kraus Hadamard rotation, exposure of its canonical complex structure, and complex spectral decomposition. Selecting a reflection-paired $2+2$ subspace in the character channel gives an exact simultaneous Kraus factorization. More importantly, the initially free choice is canonically removed by the cyclotomic Galois action: each multiplicity triple is the permutation module of the three embeddings above a fixed cubed character, and its unique trace-zero $A_2$ summand is the required two-plane. The discarded conjugate pair is the two Galois trace lines. What remains is to derive trace-mode decoupling physically. See [the complexified channel factorization](../0900-0999/945_the_character_channel_factors_to_the_handle_class_sum_after_complexification_but_the_four_carrier_compression_is_not_canonical.md) and [the Galois trace-zero selection theorem](../0900-0999/946_galois_covariance_canonically_selects_the_trace_zero_four_plane_and_removes_the_handle_factorization_grassmannian.md).

That trace pair is not optional if the primitive ninth root is retained. Its three embedding eigenvalues are $\zeta_9(1,\omega,\omega^2)$, whose Galois Fourier transform is $\zeta_9$ times the cyclic shift among trace singlet and the two trace-zero characters. The ninth-root operation therefore leaks into and out of the discarded line with unit strength; the trace-zero plane is not invariant. Cubing makes the shift identity and leaves $\omega I$, so the four-carrier retains the exact $C_3$ shadow while necessarily losing $C_9$. This gives a concrete order-reduction mechanism and a new dynamical target: the eliminated coherent trace mode should reappear as the passive common-mode environment if the two branches are one system. See [the ninth-root trace-mode traversal theorem](../0900-0999/947_the_primitive_ninth_root_traverses_the_galois_trace_mode_so_the_trace_zero_carrier_retains_only_its_cubic_shadow.md).

## 17 October 2026 update — the lossy response face now has a fixed broadband conservative dilation

The broadband question has a positive answer for **one analytic response face**.  For

\[
r(\omega)=\frac{\omega-i\gamma e^{-\ell}}{\omega+i\gamma e^{\ell}},
\qquad \ell\geq0,
\]

set \(\kappa_{\rm obs}=\gamma\cosh\ell\) and \(\kappa_{\rm env}=\gamma\sinh\ell\).  A symmetric two-port rational-inner resonance has observed reflection coefficient exactly \(r\); two ordered copies with independent environment ports have observed coefficient \(r^2\).  Taking the normal/tangent direct sum and applying the fixed 60-degree mixing therefore gives a frequency-independent six-port dilation of the complete lossy face.  The result supplies an exact positive-rate systems meaning for attenuation and replaces pointwise frequency-dependent completion by one fixed port architecture.

The remaining frontier is now narrower: sew the positive- and negative-frequency faces through the established antiunitary real structure, determine whether the two ordered environment stages are the cyclotomic Galois trace pair, and recover the pointwise character Naimark basis from this broadband stage basis.  No claim of a single complex-linear causal dilation of the complete sideband-reversing cycle is yet made.  See [the fixed six-port broadband face dilation](../0900-0999/948_each_lossy_response_face_has_a_fixed_six_port_rational_inner_dilation_with_two_positive_environment_rates.md).

That sewing has now been completed at the conservative effective-network level.  Sharing the observed pair while giving the two traversals fresh four-port environments produces a fixed ten-port unitary cascade whose observed corner is exactly \(D S(-\omega)D S(+\omega)\).  At the lossless silver checkpoint it reduces to the exact order-three Coxeter action.  Real-time reality means that the negative sideband is constrained data, not an independent matter copy; the remaining questions are global port minimality, environment/trace-mode typing, and addition of the neutral handle.  See [the ten-port two-face construction](../0900-0999/949_the_two_face_sideband_cycle_has_a_fixed_ten_port_conservative_dilation_while_reality_prevents_double_counting.md).

Direct spectral factorization improves the port count. The square of each scalar factor has a two-port inner completion, so a generic strict-loss normal/tangent face is minimally four-port by its rank-two defect. Two such faces with fresh environments give the same active sideband cycle on six ports. The earlier six-/ten-port constructions remain exact stage-resolved models, but are not input-output minimal. Whether the composite cycle itself admits a direct four-port completion is now the sharp minimality question. See [the minimal spectral completion](../0900-0999/950_spectral_factorization_compresses_each_squared_loss_channel_to_two_ports_and_the_full_two_face_cycle_to_six.md).

The direct same-half-plane version of that question is now closed negatively. Writing the opposite face as \(S(-z)\) moves its poles into the upper half-plane. Strict loss leaves them uncancelled already in the determinant; losslessly, the noncommuting grading and unequal silver scales leave them in matrix entries. No enlargement can make a prescribed nonanalytic compression analytic. The correct frontier is minimal conservative realization in a two-oriented-chart, real-interface or Floquet category. See [the sideband analyticity no-go](../0900-0999/951_the_two_face_sideband_cycle_is_boundary_monodromy_not_one_same_half_plane_rational_inner_transfer.md).

Realifying the orientation-changing interface now places it directly beside the full selector handle. The two are conjugate over \(\mathbb Q\) and \(\mathbb R\), with an explicit integer intertwiner of determinant \(-4\). They are not conjugate over \(GL(4,\mathbb Z)\): the Smith invariants of \(T-I\) and \(H-I\) are respectively \((2,2)\) and \((1,1)\). Nor do their natural symplectic typings agree: the sideband twist reverses the Fourier form while the handle preserves the selector form. The exact missing object is therefore an index-four saturation/quotient that also changes the pairing. See [the handle/chart-exchange boundary](../0900-0999/952_the_realified_sideband_twist_is_rationally_conjugate_to_the_selector_handle_but_not_in_its_natural_symplectic_typing.md).

That saturation now exists explicitly. Passing to \(L'=\mathbb Z^4+\frac12(e_1+e_2)+\frac12(e_3+e_4)\) makes the handle intertwiner unimodular and the pulled-back alternating form primitive. Existence is therefore closed. Uniqueness is not: all 35 exponent-two index-four overlattices are twist invariant, and 15 are symplectically integral and unimodular. The frontier has become a finite selection problem among fifteen Lagrangian planes, with neutral-neutrino, Jordan, Galois and boundary-locality branches as discriminants. See [the saturation census](../0900-0999/953_an_index_four_saturation_turns_the_realified_twist_into_the_primitive_selector_handle_but_fifteen_symplectic_saturations_compete.md).

A conditional selector now reduces that finite ambiguity completely. Fourier-complex stability leaves seven planes; quadrature-product locality leaves three; requiring the quotient to activate both normal and tangent eigenchannels leaves only \(W_P=\langle e_1+e_2,e_3+e_4\rangle\). This is exactly the known saturation and mirrors the earlier unique common-mode-activating \(A_2\) environment. The remaining burden is no longer combinatorial: derive product locality and both-channel participation, or construct the equivariant environment-to-quotient map. See [the common-mode saturation selector](../0900-0999/954_fourier_locality_and_both_channel_common_mode_reduce_the_fifteen_handle_saturations_to_the_unique_p_induced_plane.md).

The direct environment-to-quotient map is now excluded. Selector \(C_3\) is integral on the saturated carrier but does not preserve the original sideband lattice, so it cannot act on \(L'/L\). It cycles three distinct index-four sublattices whose mod-two planes have zero common intersection and full combined span. The correct bridge target is therefore the three-placement orbit—an induced representation or groupoid object—not one fixed quotient. See [the three-sublattice arithmetic holonomy](../0900-0999/955_the_selector_c3_cycles_three_index_four_sublattices_so_the_common_mode_saturation_quotient_is_not_itself_the_a2_environment.md).

That induced bridge now closes at representation level. The active \(C_3\) cycles the three placements and the handle fixes one while swapping two, giving the full \(S_3\) permutation module. Its canonical trace-zero plane is the real \(A_2\) standard representation, complexifying to \(\chi_1\oplus\chi_2\). This exactly matches both the minimal common-mode environment and the Galois trace-zero doublet. The open work is dynamical: assign physical amplitudes to placements and derive the loss-dependent Kraus coefficients. See [the trace-zero placement theorem](../0900-0999/956_the_trace_zero_amplitudes_of_the_three_sublattice_holonomy_are_exactly_the_a2_environment_and_galois_doublet.md).

Schur's lemma now localizes the needed dynamics. An unbroken \(S_3\)-equivariant map between observed and placement \(A_2\) doublets is scalar and cannot split normal from tangent response. A single face may preserve only its reflection \(C_2\); that commutant has two projectors and permits distinct silver poles and losses. The missing microscopic datum is therefore necessarily a face/reflection spurion. Two conjugate broken faces restore \(C_3\) only in their ordered monodromy. See [the reflection-spurion necessity theorem](../0900-0999/957_the_a2_placement_environment_requires_a_face_reflection_spurion_to_generate_distinct_silver_response_channels.md).

The strongest spurion candidates now coincide at projector level. The placement reflection is unimodularly conjugate to the Higgs population swap, making the Higgs ray the strongest primitive integral source. The \(h\chi\) aggregate grading is the same reflection in its diagonal/Walsh eigenbasis, but the basis change has index two and cannot be unimodular. Thus Higgs swap and Furey–Higgs sign are two typed descriptions of one face reflection, separated by the same primitive seam found earlier. See [the Higgs–hχ reflection intertwiner](../0900-0999/958_the_placement_face_reflection_is_integrally_the_higgs_population_swap_while_hchi_is_its_index_two_walsh_eigenbasis.md).

At coherent level the Pfaffian and scattering uses separate cleanly. The weak alternating lift is \(\varepsilon\), while the reciprocal silver face is the symmetric odd-real lift \(-iX\). They induce the same population swap but are not the same tensor. Their exact relation \(-iX=i\varepsilon D\) shows that the \(h\chi\) grading and central sideband phase convert one role into the other; conjugation reverses that phase on the opposite face. See [the Pfaffian-to-silver coherent-lift theorem](../0900-0999/959_hchi_and_the_sideband_phase_convert_the_pfaffian_higgs_lift_into_the_reciprocal_silver_scattering_lift.md).

The silver numbers now have an exact response-theoretic meaning. For a squared scalar Blaschke factor the Cayley reactance is \(q(r)=2r/(1-r^2)\). Requiring the two reflection eigenchannels to have the involutive values \((-1,+1)\) gives, uniquely among positive ratios, \(r_n=1+\sqrt2\) and \(r_t=\sqrt2-1\). At matrix level the face reactance is \(K=X=-\varepsilon D\), and its unit Cayley transform is \(-iX=i\varepsilon D\). Thus the reciprocal silver scattering lift is precisely the on-shell Cayley image of the graded Pfaffian generator. The result derives the dimensionless ratios conditional on \(K^2=I\); it does not yet explain why the checkpoint is reached, protect it dynamically, or fix a physical scale. See [the silver unit-reactance theorem](../0900-0999/960_the_silver_ratios_are_exactly_the_unit_cayley_reactances_of_the_graded_pfaffian_face_generator.md).

Strict loss is now part of the same Cayley geometry. For \(f=((\omega-ia)/(\omega+ib))^2\), \(0<a<b\), the reactance \(q=-i(1-f)/(1+f)\) lies strictly in the lower half-plane. Moreover, \(-\operatorname{Im}q\) and the Schur defect \(1-|f|^2\) have the identical positive numerator \((b^2-a^2)(2\omega^2+a^2+b^2)\). This is exactly the polynomial whose spectral square root supplies the minimal environment coupling. Loss therefore deforms the real silver reactance into a dissipative effective generator, rather than adding an independent mechanism. The theorem does not yet identify that environment with the Galois trace pair, the placement doublet, or a Furey/Jordan field. See [the dissipative Cayley theorem](../0900-0999/961_strict_loss_is_a_lower_half_plane_cayley_deformation_whose_dissipative_part_is_the_spectral_environment_defect.md).

The environment ontology now has a hard boundary. Every dissipative reactance \(Q=A-iB\), \(B\geq0\), is the Schur complement of a minimal rank-\(B\) outgoing-port block, but no ordinary elimination of a finite invertible Hermitian auxiliary block can produce its anti-Hermitian part. A conservative realization must retain propagating ports or a continuum; a finite closed hidden multiplet is not enough. This recovers the defect-rank port count and sharply constrains any Furey/Jordan interpretation of the environment. See [the outgoing-port Schur theorem](../0900-0999/962_the_dissipative_cayley_generator_has_a_minimal_outgoing_port_schur_realization_but_no_closed_finite_hermitian_elimination.md).

The minimal outgoing pair can now be typed canonically at representation level. Requiring both the face reflection and nontrivial transport through the three face placements excludes the reducible \(1\oplus\mathrm{sgn}\) representation and selects the real \(A_2\) doublet. Its positive coupling is \(G_0=\sqrt{\beta_n}P_++\sqrt{\beta_t}P_-\), and the three conjugate loss forms obey \(\sum_{k=0}^2C^kB_0C^{-k}=\tfrac32(\beta_n+\beta_t)I\). Local face anisotropy therefore cancels exactly in the complete three-placement orbit. This matches the placement, Galois and minimal channel environments as representations, not yet as microscopic fields. See [the outgoing-port \(A_2\) theorem](../0900-0999/963_the_minimal_outgoing_port_pair_is_canonically_the_a2_doublet_and_its_three_face_loss_orbit_is_globally_isotropic.md).

A first Jordan-cubic realization now exists. In the rank-three Euclidean Jordan algebra \(\mathbb R^3\) with norm \(N=x_0x_1x_2\), the positive cone metric \(\operatorname{Hess}[-\log N]\), restricted at \((u,v,v)\) to the trace-zero \(A_2\) plane, has reflection eigenvalues \(((2a+b)/3,b)\). It realizes a prescribed density \((\beta_+,\beta_-)\) exactly when \(3\beta_+>\beta_-\). Cyclically moving the distinguished coordinate produces the three face forms and their isotropic sum. The raw Hessian of \(N\) is not positive; the logarithmic barrier is essential. This is a toy existence theorem and sharp channel test, not yet a derivation of the frequency trajectory or an exceptional/Furey lift. See [the cubic-barrier face metric](../0900-0999/964_a_rank_three_jordan_cubic_barrier_generates_the_a2_face_loss_metric_subject_to_a_sharp_channel_inequality.md).

The actual silver loss law now tests that toy chart sharply. Tangent-even assignment fails globally for every loss by its high-frequency limit. Normal-even assignment covers all real frequencies only for \(\ell\geq\ell_*\approx0.4779176415\), corresponding to \(e^{-\ell_*}\approx0.62007\). Below threshold—including weak loss—the cubic positivity inequality fails in a finite band near the tangent pole. This pins one overly simple model: a single fixed diagonal cone chart cannot represent the complete weakly lossy silver response. Multi-chart, non-diagonal Peirce, bounded-window and complexified-cone branches remain open. See [the global chart threshold](../0900-0999/965_the_silver_loss_law_fits_one_global_diagonal_jordan_cone_chart_only_above_a_finite_attenuation_threshold.md).

The minimal multi-chart repair is exact. The normal-even domain \(3\beta_n>\beta_t\) and tangent-even domain \(3\beta_t>\beta_n\) cover every positive two-channel density, overlapping when \(1/3<\beta_n/\beta_t<3\). Their reflection embeddings are \(F\) and \(-F\), related not by an \(S_3\) Weyl element but by the invariant quarter-turn \(J\): \(J^2=-I\), \(JFJ^{-1}=-F\), and \(JC=CJ\). The weak-loss silver trajectory therefore has a complete two-chart Jordan atlas whose transition is exactly the coherent Hopf/complex structure already found in the Pfaffian and sideband branches. A common microscopic connection is not yet derived. See [the two-chart Hopf atlas](../0900-0999/966_two_jordan_cone_charts_cover_every_positive_a2_loss_density_and_their_transition_is_the_hopf_complex_structure.md).

The atlas itself does not yet generate the central phase. Coordinate consistency requires the return transition to be \(J^{-1}\), so an ordinary chart round trip gives \(J^{-1}J=I\). The nontrivial central holonomy arises only when the two physical faces both apply the same oriented map, \(JJ=-I\). The “one twist” is therefore genuine connection data rather than an artifact of using two coordinate patches. The Pfaffian sign remains the leading orientation-odd selector between same-oriented and inverse-oriented sewing, but its dynamical coupling is still open. See [the atlas/holonomy separation theorem](../0900-0999/967_the_two_chart_jordan_atlas_has_trivial_coordinate_holonomy_while_the_central_minus_identity_requires_an_oriented_two_face_connection.md).

The minimal two-face connection now separates two binary data exactly. Writing the face maps as \(U_j=J^{\eta_j}\), equal signs give \(-I\) and opposite signs give \(I\). Simultaneously reversing both signs reverses the orientation of both quarter-turns but leaves \(-I\) unchanged. Hence the global Pfaffian sign can select the two inverse orientations while the relative face sign selects the central holonomy sector. A single coherent sign shared by both faces has exactly the desired algebra; whether antiunitary sideband sewing aligns or anti-aligns those signs is now the decisive open calculation. See [the two-face \(Z_4\) connection](../0900-0999/968_the_minimal_two_face_z4_connection_separates_global_orientation_from_relative_central_holonomy.md).

That sideband calculation now closes algebraically. Bare \(\Xi=XK\) reverses both \(\varepsilon\) and \(-iX\), but the \(h\chi\) grading \(D\) reverses them again. The full interface is \(\Gamma=D\Xi=DXK=\varepsilon K\); it preserves the Pfaffian quarter-turn and coherent silver lift, aligns the two face signs, and satisfies \(\Gamma^2=-I\). Thus the adopted graded interface lies in the nontrivial \(Z_4\) sector and is a quaternionic/Kramers-type antiunitary rather than a plain real involution. Its Standard-Model derivation and forward-time implementation remain open. See [the graded sideband alignment theorem](../0900-0999/969_the_graded_sideband_interface_preserves_pfaffian_alignment_and_is_a_quaternionic_antiunitary_with_square_minus_identity.md).

Realifying \(\Gamma\) on the four-carrier produces the sought Lorentz split inside \(Spin(2,3)\). It is an anti-symplectic complex structure, and its adjoint involution decomposes \(\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb R)\) into a six-dimensional fixed algebra and four-dimensional odd complement. The fixed algebra is \(\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb C)_\mathbb R\cong\mathfrak{so}(1,3)\); the complement has invariant signature \((3,1)\) and is the Lorentz vector/AdS-translation sector. This gives the TKK-produced ambient algebra a physically motivated internal selector. It remains to prove that the actual face, handle and mediator generators span all ten directions. See [the Lorentz symmetric-pair theorem](../0900-0999/970_the_graded_quaternionic_interface_selects_the_lorentz_subalgebra_inside_sp4_and_the_four_remaining_generators_form_a_minkowski_vector.md).

The ten-dimensional generation gate now closes conditionally and minimally. On the TKK four-carrier, the two diagonal face translations, their one off-diagonal relational translation, and the canonical grade-reversing handle have commutator closure exactly \(\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb R)\). Ablation is decisive: the translations alone are abelian of dimension three; the two diagonal faces plus handle give two uncoupled \(\mathfrak{sl}_2\) channels of dimension six; the off-diagonal relationship raises the closure to ten. The only remaining seam is to identify the saturated physical handle with the canonical TKK inversion in the same symplectic typing. See [the physical generator-closure theorem](../0900-0999/971_two_face_translations_their_off_diagonal_relationship_and_one_grade_reversing_handle_generate_all_of_sp4.md).

That seam requires a coherent lift, not direct conjugacy. Primitive selector handle \(H\) squares to \(+I\), while TKK inversion \(W\) squares to \(-I\), so they cannot be conjugate. Splitting into the two handle eigenspaces and assigning opposite complex orientations gives a commuting \(Z\) with \(Z^2=-I\); the lift \(L=ZH\) is symplectic, squares to \(-I\), and an explicit symplectic basis sends it to \(-W\). The Lie-closure theorem therefore survives with a sharper physical gate: identify this \(Z\) with the independently derived Hopf/Pfaffian coherent structure. See [the coherent handle-lift theorem](../0900-0999/972_the_primitive_selector_handle_cannot_equal_tkk_inversion_but_its_coherent_eigenspace_lift_is_symplectically_conjugate_to_it.md).

That identification now closes at typed-operator level. In the handle eigenbasis, \(Z'=\operatorname{diag}(\varepsilon,-\varepsilon)\), exactly the real-linear part of the graded Pfaffian/sideband interface. It is anti-symplectic for the Fourier form but symplectic for the handle-eigenspace form; transporting by the explicit eigenbasis gives the \(Z\) used in \(L=ZH\). No second Hopf structure is needed. The remaining conditional input is the physical selection of the saturated pairing, not the algebraic identity of the lift. See [the Pfaffian/handle typed-identity theorem](../0900-0999/973_the_coherent_handle_lift_complex_structure_is_the_graded_pfaffian_sideband_operator_after_the_required_pairing_transport.md).

Transporting the three Jordan translations through that same map verifies the full ten-dimensional closure directly in the selector basis. Arithmetic adds a productive obstruction: normal and tangent generators are half-integral but share the same mod-two residue, while the relationship and coherent handle are integral. The operator extension is therefore index two, not four, and cannot by itself explain the earlier carrier saturation. One additional independent carrier-level half-direction must come from pairing, quadrature or chart transport. See [the selector-basis closure and saturation mismatch](../0900-0999/974_the_transported_selector_generators_close_to_sp4_but_their_single_half_integral_residue_does_not_explain_the_index_four_carrier_saturation.md).

The complete fifteen-plane census now exposes and resolves that seam. No index-four symplectic saturation is preserved by all four primitive generators: the unique plane compatible with both face translations differs from the unique plane compatible with relationship plus coherent handle. But the face translations are intrinsically half-integral. Replacing them by their minimal integral representatives $2T_n,2T_t$ makes the previously selected common-mode plane $W_P=\langle e_1+e_2,e_3+e_4\rangle$ the unique saturation preserved by the full normalized set. This supplies an independent integrality selection of $W_P$, while moving the open question to the physical origin of the factor two. See [the integral-generator saturation theorem](../0900-0999/975_primitive_face_integrality_obstructs_every_index_four_saturation_but_doubled_faces_uniquely_select_the_common_mode_carrier.md).

That unique carrier does not yet carry the full Chevalley order. In a unimodular standard symplectic basis, the bracket-closed order generated by $2T_n,2T_t,T_r,L$ has exact index $512=2^9$ in $\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb Z)$. Its reduction has dimension five modulo two but dimension ten modulo three and five. The real and rational $Spin(2,3)$ closure is unchanged; the remaining arithmetic defect is purely dyadic. The next branch is therefore not a generic root-length adjustment but a choice among parahoric boundary structure, missing primitive generators, or completion by the three saturation placements. See [the dyadic integral-order theorem](../0900-0999/976_the_common_mode_carrier_supports_a_full_rational_sp4_but_its_generated_integral_lie_order_has_dyadic_index_512.md).

The mod-two image is not unstructured. It preserves exactly one proper nonzero subspace, a Lagrangian plane, and has neither invariant lines nor common fixed vectors. Its induced diagonal image is the full three-dimensional $\mathfrak{sl}(2,\mathbb F_2)$, with a two-dimensional abelian off-diagonal kernel. This identifies the dyadic memory as a unique-Lagrangian boundary extension. The next decisive test is whether the three saturation placements supply conjugate Lagrangians whose combined image restores all of $\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb F_2)$. See [the mod-two boundary theorem](../0900-0999/977_the_dyadic_lie_image_is_a_unique_lagrangian_boundary_extension_of_sl2f2_by_a_two_dimensional_abelian_radical.md).

That decisive test succeeds more strongly than required. One placement generates the index-$512$ boundary order, while adjoining any one distinct $C_3$ conjugate produces Hermite diagonal $(1,\ldots,1)$ and hence the complete $\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb Z)$ lattice. The third placement is redundant for Lie generation but remains necessary for the canonical three-point $S_3$ permutation module and its trace-zero $A_2$ environment. Arithmetic completion therefore comes from the relationship between placements: a single boundary is proper, while two transverse boundaries recover the full integral carrier. See [the placement-completion theorem](../0900-0999/978_two_distinct_c3_related_placements_complete_the_dyadic_boundary_order_to_the_full_chevalley_sp4z_lattice.md).

The second placement can now be reduced to its exact operational minimum. No single generator—or single integral mixture of its four generators—completes the first order; all fifteen nonzero mod-two parity classes stop at dimension at most nine. Two independently available generators suffice precisely when they are the conjugate coherent handle and any one conjugate Jordan translation. Translation-translation pairs remain index two, and the mixed sum $L'+T'$ also remains index two. Integral completion therefore requires composability, not merely the presence of both components in one observable. See [the minimal conjugate-pair theorem](../0900-0999/979_arithmetic_completion_requires_two_independent_conjugate_operations_one_handle_and_one_translation.md).

A pairing audit now prevents an over-compressed synthesis. The graded operator produces the Lorentz $6+4$ symmetric pair only as an anti-symplectic operator for the Fourier form. Under the handle/selector pairing required for coherent TKK closure, the same abstract operator is symplectic and its adjoint decomposition is $4+6$, with fixed algebra $\mathfrak u(1,1)$. Thus Lorentz selection and coherent handle lift are exact roles in different pairing charts, not simultaneous properties on one fixed symplectic carrier. The next central gate is an explicit physical pairing-switching morphism or bi-symplectic/groupoid formulation. See [the two-pairing obstruction theorem](../0900-0999/980_the_lorentz_6_plus_4_split_and_the_coherent_handle_lift_are_two_pairing_dependent_roles_not_one_fixed_symplectic_typing.md).

The two forms themselves now provide such a mathematical bridge. Their relative recursion $R=\Omega^{-1}J$ is an integral involution anticommuting with the graded complex structure $G$; together with $K=GR$ they form a split-quaternionic triple. The $R$ eigenspaces are two oppositely oriented symplectic planes exchanged by $G$, and the two constant inverse bivectors form a compatible Poisson pair. Integrally, the sum of the two eigenlattices has index four and quotient $C_2^2$, reproducing the exact Smith type of the earlier saturation seam. Literal equality with the selected lattice is not yet proved, so the next gate is the typed lattice intertwiner. See [the bi-symplectic recursion theorem](../0900-0999/981_the_fourier_and_handle_forms_define_a_split_quaternionic_bisymplectic_recursion_with_an_index_four_eigenlattice_seam.md).

The typed lattice comparison closes that gate negatively but informatively. The symplectic dual of the recursion eigenlattice transports to the admissible plane $W_E=\{0,7,11,12\}$, while the common-mode carrier is $W_P=\{0,3,12,15\}$. The recursion plane preserves the coherent handle but none of the three primitive translations. Pairing recursion therefore derives the index-four $C_2^2$ saturation class, not the physical member. Relationship and both-channel/common-mode data remain genuinely necessary to select $W_P$. See [the recursion-versus-common-mode saturation theorem](../0900-0999/982_the_bisymplectic_dual_saturation_is_admissible_but_not_the_common_mode_carrier_so_recursion_alone_does_not_select_physics.md).

The two planes nevertheless differ by the smallest possible relational operation. Among all fifteen primitive symplectic transvections, exactly the shears along $e_3$ and $e_4$ map $W_E$ to $W_P$. Both lie in the imaginary-quadrature block, so the finite geometry selects the correct block while leaving a twofold channel choice. This one rank-one nilpotent shear selects the carrier but does not complete the integral Lie algebra; that still requires the independent conjugate handle-plus-translation pair. See [the minimal transvection theorem](../0900-0999/983_a_single_imaginary_quadrature_symplectic_transvection_maps_the_recursion_saturation_to_the_common_mode_carrier.md).

Transport back to the canonical TKK carrier identifies the two shears exactly. The $e_3$ option is the tangent $E_{22}$ channel and the $e_4$ option the normal $E_{11}$ channel. In either case the nilpotent is $N_i=C_i+H_i-T_i=\operatorname{Ad}_{\exp(T_i)}C_i$: a translation-dressed special-conformal generator containing the channel translation, its opposite-grade dual and dilation. Neither is the off-diagonal relationship direction. The remaining twofold choice is therefore precisely the physical normal/tangent asymmetry already visible in silver loss, Higgs reflection and Jordan chart parity. See [the dressed TKK transvection theorem](../0900-0999/984_the_carrier_selecting_shear_is_a_translation_dressed_special_conformal_generator_in_one_peirce_channel.md).

The discrete spurions now classify that ambiguity rather than prematurely resolving it. Higgs population swap exchanges $N_{11}$ and $N_{22}$; $h\chi$ labels the channels and reverses the integral sideband shear orientation while leaving its mod-two action; the graded Pfaffian exchanges channel and conformal orientation. Only unequal normal/tangent response coefficients break the exchange. Under the additional demand of one global diagonal Jordan chart, strong loss $\ell\geq\ell_*$ conditionally selects the normal shear; weak loss provably requires both charts and admits no global binary choice. See [the carrier-shear spurion theorem](../0900-0999/985_higgs_exchanges_the_two_carrier_shears_hchi_orients_them_and_only_response_anisotropy_can_select_a_channel.md).

The diagonal cubic barrier cannot supply that shear dynamically. Its Hessian metric is Euclidean in logarithmic coordinates, its curvature and holonomy vanish, and dilation is its only channelwise projective-$\mathfrak{sl}_2$ Killing direction. The carrier nilpotent appears only in the TKK prolongation, where its flow is Möbius-parabolic with vector field $-(x-1)^2\partial_x$ and double fixed point at one. This isolates the exact “next rung”: cubic norm to positive metric to dilation geometry, then conformal/TKK completion to relational shear. The unit fixed point is adjacent to the unit-reactance silver theorem, but their coordinate equality remains an explicit open map. See [the barrier-versus-TKK theorem](../0900-0999/986_the_diagonal_cubic_barrier_is_flat_in_log_coordinates_and_cannot_generate_the_carrier_shear_without_tkk_prolongation.md).

That coordinate map now exists in a sharply delimited class. In the normal/tangent eigenbasis the silver reactance is $K=\operatorname{diag}(-1,+1)=-D$, where $D$ is the same $h\chi$ channel grading. The folded projective coordinate $\Xi=-DK$ is therefore exactly $I$: $x_n=-q_n=1$ and $x_t=q_t=1$, the joint double fixed point of the two channel TKK shears. This fold is unique among diagonal involutive sign corrections and covariant when Higgs exchange transports both $D$ and $K$. Strict passive loss sends the two folded coordinates into opposite half-planes, so the equality is a lossless checkpoint bridge rather than a one-chart dissipative unification. The dynamical selection of $K=-D$ remains open. See [the graded-reactance/TKK bridge](../0900-0999/987_the_hchi_graded_reactance_fold_identifies_the_silver_checkpoint_with_the_joint_tkk_unit_fixed_point.md).

The minimal effective-action burden now splits into two independent mechanisms. The symmetric constraint $K^2=I$ leaves a full circle of trace-zero reflections, so involutivity alone cannot select the silver orientation. On that locus the covariant spurion scalar $\operatorname{tr}(DK)$ has the unique minimum $K=-D$. An auxiliary-multiplier action realizes this as an exact constrained saddle, but only as a template: upstream dynamics must still generate both the involution protection and the sign of the $h\chi$ coupling. This prevents the coordinate identity from being mistaken for a microscopic explanation. See [the minimal constrained-action classification](../0900-0999/988_the_minimal_silver_response_action_needs_both_involutive_protection_and_an_oriented_hchi_spurion.md).

The Furey branch now consolidates those two generic ingredients algebraically. On $(u,\nu,d,e)$ the commuting handle and Higgs gradings have product $p=h\chi$ with $p^2=I$; normalized lepton–colour compression gives exactly $p_{LQ}=\operatorname{diag}(-1,+1)=-D$. A physical response may still be $K(k)=k p_{LQ}$, so the grading fixes direction and relative sign but not amplitude. Its Cayley relative phase is $-4\arctan k$; the exact positive-$k$ $A_2$ condition forces $k=1$ uniquely and recovers the silver ratios. The remaining non-circular task is to derive both proportionality to $p_{LQ}$ and the finite-monodromy condition from a microscopic mediator/boundary action. See [the Furey-grading/Coxeter normalization theorem](../0900-0999/989_the_furey_higgs_product_grading_is_the_silver_reactance_direction_and_a2_monodromy_fixes_its_unit_amplitude.md).

The arithmetic protection option proposed earlier is now proved. The integral normalizer of $G_{A_2}$ has twelve elements and is dihedral; all six orientation-reversing elements are genuine square-one reflections. The transported mediator faces are two of them, with product the integral order-three Coxeter matrix. Therefore exact preservation of the placement $A_2$ lattice quantizes the projective face eigenvalue ratio to $-1$, independently imposing $4\arctan k=\pi$ and hence $k=1$. This avoids circular normalization only if physical boundary sewing is shown to preserve the integral lattice, rather than merely its real span. See [the integral-boundary quantization theorem](../0900-0999/990_exact_a2_lattice_preservation_quantizes_the_boundary_reflection_and_independently_forces_unit_silver_reactance.md).

The placement branch already contains the required integral arrows. In the trace-zero placement basis its handle is $F=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}1&0\\1&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right)$ and its cycle is $A=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}0&-1\\1&-1\end{smallmatrix}\right)$. The two transported mediator faces are exactly $F_-=F$ and $F_+=FA$, so their ordered cascade is identically $F(FA)=A$. They generate the six-element Weyl subgroup of the twelve-element normalizer; the central-minus coset remains coherent-lift data. The open gate is no longer matrix matching but construction of a physical functor from placement objects and arrows to mediator configurations and scattering maps. See [the placement-groupoid/mediator-arrow theorem](../0900-0999/991_the_two_mediator_faces_are_exactly_adjacent_reflection_arrows_in_the_three_placement_groupoid.md).

The coherent lift of that functor is now classified. Replacing the real reflection $F$ by its response lift $X=iF$ gives $X^2=-I$ and generates with $A$ a twelve-element dicyclic group $\operatorname{Dic}_3$, not the twelve-element real lattice normalizer. Sideband-reversing composition gives $(+iF)(-iFA)=A$, while same-sideband composition gives $(-iF)(-iFA)=(-I)A$ of order six. Thus the analytic response is a projective placement-groupoid representation whose $C_2$ cocycle records the central sideband/Pfaffian sign. A microscopic interface must realize this projective functor, not merely the real Weyl matrices. See [the coherent dicyclic-lift theorem](../0900-0999/992_the_coherent_two_face_response_is_a_dicyclic_central_lift_of_the_placement_weyl_groupoid.md).

The multiplier is explicitly the nontrivial parity-square cocycle $c(g,h)=\epsilon(g)\epsilon(h)$: a central $-I$ appears exactly when two reflection arrows compose. Exhausting all normalized $C_2$ one-cochains proves that this cocycle is not a coboundary, so no global phase convention splits the coherent extension. Reversing exactly one face cancels the sign on the selected Coxeter path; reversing neither or both leaves the order-six lift. The physical target is therefore a projective functor plus a local, orientation-sensitive one-cochain—not a globally ordinary $S_3$ amplitude action. See [the cocycle non-splitting theorem](../0900-0999/993_the_dicyclic_placement_lift_has_a_nontrivial_parity_square_cocycle_that_only_local_sideband_reversal_can_cancel.md).

Extending that local cancellation over all three placement paths produces an odd-cycle obstruction. With reflections $R_i=FA^i$ and static reversal bits $b_i$, each edge has central exponent $e_i=1+b_i+b_{i+1}$. The three requirements $e_i=0$ are inconsistent; every assignment has total flux one and leaves at least one edge in the order-six sector. A global Pfaffian sign changes all $b_i$ and leaves the edge sectors unchanged. The obstruction vanishes on the canonical six-cycle double cover, or can be addressed by a path-dependent connection or compensating idler/inflow cocycle. This pins a genuine global dependency instead of forcing a static sign story. See [the placement-triangle frustration theorem](../0900-0999/994_the_three_placement_triangle_has_a_z2_sideband_frustration_and_requires_path_dependence_or_a_double_cover.md).

The double-cover branch is unique up to gauge. Writing a lifted object as $(i,s)\in\mathbb Z_3\times\mathbb Z_2$, the connected cover is $T(i,s)=(i+1,s+1)$; hence $T^3$ is the deck flip and $T^6=I$. All four odd-flux voltage assignments are gauge equivalent to this six-cycle, while even flux gives two disconnected triangles. The sheet label cancels every lifted edge and is typed most naturally as sideband/orientation parity. CAR identification remains possible only on neutral or self-conjugate sectors, and the six vertices must not be counted as six matter generations. See [the canonical coherent-cover theorem](../0900-0999/995_the_unique_connected_double_cover_of_the_placement_triangle_is_the_six_cycle_with_sideband_as_deck_parity.md).

The established graded antiunitary supplies a coherent lift of that deck flip. Realifying $\Gamma=\varepsilon K$ gives $\Gamma_{\mathbb R}^2=-I_4$. If each placement step applies one such interface, $\mathcal T=C_{\rm pl}\otimes\Gamma_{\mathbb R}$ has order twelve, with $\mathcal T^6=-I$ and projective order six. Forgetting the sheet recovers order three. The dicyclic center, quaternionic square, deck-lift square and sixth power of transport are therefore the same central operator. This exact $C_{12}\to C_6\to C_3$ hierarchy is conditional only at the local assignment of one $\Gamma$ to each placement edge. See [the nested transport theorem](../0900-0999/996_the_graded_sideband_antiunitary_lifts_the_six_cycle_to_a_nested_c12_to_c6_to_c3_transport.md).

The edgewise insertion now has an explicit local effective-field construction. With the real matching matrix $\varepsilon=DX$, the conditions $q_{n+1}=\varepsilon q_n$ and $p_{n+1}=\varepsilon p_n$ cancel the boundary variation and preserve flux because $\varepsilon^T\varepsilon=I$. Fourier reality converts that real sewing into the antiunitary $\Gamma=\varepsilon K$. Its six-edge holonomy is $-I$, so the field is antiperiodic on the canonical six-cycle and periodic on the twelve-step cover. This closes local effective existence without evading the no-unitary-conjugation theorem; the remaining frontier is the microscopic origin of the real field and its $DX$ boundary term. See [document 997](../0900-0999/997_a_local_conservative_real_interface_network_realizes_the_nested_c12_transport_as_antiperiodic_six_cycle_holonomy.md).

The passive-idler alternative is now sharply excluded at the lossless checkpoint. If a unitary dilation has unitary observed compression $A=\varepsilon$, unitarity forces the observed subspace to reduce it: the full edge is necessarily $\varepsilon\oplus V$. Six such edges therefore restrict to $\varepsilon^6=-I$ regardless of environment dimension or edge-dependent environment dynamics. Loss ports can realize strict-loss contractivity and carry the $A_2$ representation, but they cannot cancel the coherent central sign without changing the observed edge map or the physical equivalence relation. The live closures are antiperiodic/projective six-step transport or the twelve-step cover. See [document 998](../0900-0999/998_a_passive_idler_dilation_cannot_cancel_the_lossless_placement_cocycle_without_changing_the_observed_edge_map.md).

The projective six-step branch now closes exactly on even observables. A common central $-I$ is nontrivial on the four-channel Furey amplitude carrier but cancels on every diagonal quadratic tensor, Hermitian bilinear, symmetric/antisymmetric pairing, Gram, current and stress constructed from equally transformed legs. On the complete 78-dimensional symmetric square of the twelve-real coherent transport, the induced action has exact order six rather than twelve. Thus the antiperiodic carrier and periodic Jordan relationship are consistent without an idler. What remains open is microscopic typing: the response sign is not yet identified with fermion parity, a spin center or a Standard-Model gauge transformation, and fixed Higgs or unequal-character legs can still detect it. See [document 999](../0900-0999/999_the_central_sign_is_invisible_on_even_furey_bilinears_and_reduces_coherent_c12_to_observable_c6.md).

The microscopic-center audit now rules out the most direct identification. Each gauge-invariant Higgs-dressed Yukawa composite contains two fermions and one weak-doublet Higgs: it is even under fermion parity, the Lorentz spin center and the weak center; quark channels are also color-center neutral. The common response $-I$ therefore cannot act directly on these completed bilinears as any familiar microscopic center. Meanwhile the relative Furey–Higgs grading $p=(+,-,+,-)$ is noncentral and must remain distinct. The coherent sign has to live on an upstream square root—an individual carrier leg, neutral Nambu/Majorana amplitude, Pfaffian line or emergent projective bundle—whose quadratic image is the order-six response. See [document 1000](../1000-1099/1000_all_four_higgs_dressed_furey_dirac_composites_are_even_under_the_known_microscopic_centers_so_the_coherent_minus_sign_must_live_on_a_response_root.md).

The response-root candidates no longer tie. The unique gauge-neutral Furey complex plane—the neutral neutrino Peirce line—tensored with the real lepton–colour doublet gives exactly the required four-real carrier. In standard block ordering, $\Gamma_{\mathbb R}=K_\nu\otimes(DX)=\operatorname{diag}(\varepsilon,-\varepsilon)$ and squares to $-I_4$. Writing the root as a $2\times2$ real matrix $\Psi$ gives the canonical Jordan map $Q=\Psi^T\Psi\in H_2(\mathbb R)_+$, covariance $Q(\Gamma\Psi)=\varepsilon Q\varepsilon^T$, and central descent $Q(-\Psi)=Q(\Psi)$. This is dimension-minimal and exact; the remaining burden is the source-level Furey/Higgs intertwiner and boundary action. See [document 1001](../1000-1099/1001_the_neutral_furey_peirce_line_times_the_lepton_color_doublet_is_the_minimal_four_real_response_root_and_its_gram_is_the_jordan_carrier.md).

The response-channel part of that intertwiner now closes. The normalized map $A:(u,\nu,d,e)\mapsto(L,Q)$ obeys $Ap=(-D)A$ and $AF_C=XA$, so the source antiunitary $F_CpK$ descends exactly to $\varepsilon K$. Both square to minus identity, and the aggregate projection commutes with $p$ and $F_C$, making the passage reducing rather than lossy. Under symmetric within-sector aggregation and positive exchange covariance, $A$ is unique up to the common sign already killed by the Gram map. The only remaining source seam is identifying the aggregate complex phase with the neutral $D\leftrightarrow E$ Peirce line and deriving $F_CpK$ dynamically. See [document 1002](../1000-1099/1002_the_four_furey_response_channels_have_an_exact_reducing_intertwiner_to_the_neutral_peirce_lepton_color_root.md).

Broken-phase stability qualifies that uniqueness. Dropping within-sector equality yields the exact circle $A(a,b)$ with lepton coordinate $a\nu+be$ and quark coordinate $bu+ad$; every point still intertwines $p,F_C$ and $F_CpK$. A fixed symmetric aggregate survives diagonal running only when $r_u=r_d$ and $r_\nu=r_e$, while exact $F_C$ covariance requires the crossed equalities $r_u=r_e$ and $r_\nu=r_d$; together they force all four rates equal. A moving weighted root can absorb cross-related reweighting, but generic infrared running leaks into antisymmetric modes. The exact root is therefore naturally unbroken-scale, matched-boundary or spurion-covariant—not automatically a fixed low-energy particle subspace. See [document 1003](../1000-1099/1003_the_exact_furey_response_root_has_a_weighted_circle_of_covariant_aggregates_but_a_fixed_symmetric_root_requires_unbroken_channel_degeneracy.md).

The weighted circle itself carries the central topology. With $a=\cos\theta$, $b=\sin\theta$, its projector satisfies $P(\theta+\pi)=P(\theta)$ while the normalized root frame satisfies $A(\theta+\pi)=-A(\theta)$. The canonical connection is locally flat, $A\,dA^T=0$; the minus sign is a global transition holonomy of the horizontal double cover, not local Berry curvature. This reproduces the root/relationship period doubling independently. However, strictly positive response weights occupy only a contractible quadrant, so a physical sign requires phase/sign winding, patching or an independent endpoint identification. See [document 1004](../1000-1099/1004_the_weighted_furey_root_is_a_flat_horizontal_double_cover_of_its_projector_circle_with_central_minus_identity_holonomy.md).

Complex relative phase removes that positivity obstruction without changing magnitudes. Taking $a=e^{-i\phi/2}/\sqrt2$ and $b=e^{i\phi/2}/\sqrt2$ gives a horizontal Hopf lift with projector period $2\pi$, frame period $4\pi$, and one-loop holonomy $-I$. But conjugation sends $A(\phi)$ to $A(-\phi)$, so the graded antiunitary is a Real-bundle involution covering base reflection rather than a fiberwise operator at generic phase. This supplies a nonvanishing central loop at fixed positive magnitudes while moving the open gate to a physical CP/Higgs phase cycle and an explicit map to placement transport. See [document 1005](../1000-1099/1005_a_complex_relative_phase_realizes_the_weighted_root_hopf_loop_at_fixed_positive_magnitudes_but_turns_the_antiunitary_into_a_real_bundle_involution.md).

The base-loop map now exists on six canonical marked phases. Sampling $\phi_n=n\pi/3$ and sending $n$ to $(n\bmod3,n\bmod2)$ reproduces exactly the placement advance plus sheet flip. Conjugation $n\mapsto-n$ becomes placement-cycle inversion with sheet parity fixed. The phase half-turn $n\mapsto n+3$ is the deck flip, whose coherent coefficient lift is $\operatorname{diag}(-i,+i)$ and squares to $-I$. This realizes the full $C_{12}\to C_6\to C_3$ tower in one marked Hopf phase orbit. The remaining gate is dynamical marking: a connected phase circle cannot map nontrivially and continuously to six discrete vertices. See [document 1006](../1000-1099/1006_the_six_marked_hopf_phases_map_equivariantly_to_the_placement_cover_and_the_phase_half_turn_is_the_coherent_deck_lift.md).

The symmetry part of that dynamical-marking gate now closes. Placement advance requires Fourier weight divisible by three, the deck half-turn requires even weight, and Real/CP reflection removes the sine sector. Their first common nonconstant invariant is therefore $\cos6\phi$. A leading potential $V=-\kappa\cos6\phi$ with $\kappa>0$ selects exactly the six marks $\phi_n=n\pi/3$ and gaps their phase fluctuations; $\kappa<0$ selects the interlaced shifted hexagon. This is an exact invariant-theory result, not yet a microscopic potential: the existence, sign and scale of $\kappa$, and even the dynamical status of $\phi$, remain open. See [document 1007](../1000-1099/1007_placement_deck_and_real_symmetry_force_cos6phi_as_the_minimal_phase_locking_term_and_select_the_six_marked_hopf_phases_conditionally.md).

The Jordan cubic now supplies a sharply typed candidate for that coefficient. On a complex weight-one root, a homogeneous cubic carries phase $e^{i3\phi}$: it is placement invariant but deck odd. Its linear real part would produce the forbidden third harmonic, whereas the first deck-even angular observable is $N^2+\bar N^2\propto\cos6\phi$; $N\bar N$ is only radial. This makes the sixth harmonic a square of a cubic orientation amplitude. The construction fails for an ordinary real Jordan determinant, so the next decisive task is to find—or exclude—a complexified, Pfaffian-line or Furey cubic with the required coefficient-line character. See [document 1008](../1000-1099/1008_a_deck_odd_complex_jordan_cubic_generates_the_minimal_sixth_harmonic_but_the_real_determinant_alone_cannot.md).

Corpus reconciliation finds that such a complex cubic is not wholly hypothetical: the oriented exceptional complement already has the unique holomorphic invariant $\det Z$. But the current Hopf root is a half-angle frame, $a=e^{-i\phi/2}/\sqrt2$, $b=e^{i\phi/2}/\sqrt2$. No primitive holomorphic cubic in $a,b$ has phase weight three. The target is instead the projective-ratio cubic $(b/a)^3$, with global normalized representative $b^3\bar a^3=e^{i3\phi}/8$. It is a bicubic of total frame degree six, and its even square is degree twelve. The decisive gate is now an equivariant map from the neutral Furey root—or two paired cubic face sectors—into the exceptional complement determinant line. See [document 1009](../1000-1099/1009_the_oriented_exceptional_determinant_has_the_right_character_only_after_a_projective_ratio_embedding_and_is_bicubic_on_the_hopf_root.md).

The apparent chart dependence of that projective cubic now disappears globally. On the normalized Hopf spinor, $w=2b\bar a$ is the gauge-invariant equatorial coordinate of $\mathbb{CP}^1\cong S^2$, namely $w=\sin\theta e^{i\phi}$. Hence $w^3$ is a global smooth deck-odd cubic relationship, and $V=-\kappa\operatorname{Re}(w^6)=-\kappa\sin^6\theta\cos6\phi$ has exactly six global equatorial minima for either sign. For positive $\kappa$ its Hessian there is $\operatorname{diag}(6\kappa,36\kappa)$, so the same term locks both equal magnitudes and phase. It necessarily vanishes at the two pure-face poles, leaving a precise unwinding channel unless dynamics excludes them. See [document 1010](../1000-1099/1010_the_bicubic_orientation_is_a_global_hopf_map_function_whose_even_square_selects_six_equatorial_roots_and_has_forced_polar_zeros.md).

The Jordan positive-cone branch supplies an exact conditional polar barrier. The coherent Hopf projector itself is rank one and has zero determinant everywhere, so it cannot be used. But the diagonal face-population relationship $R=\operatorname{diag}(|a|^2,|b|^2)$ has $4\det R=\sin^2\theta$. Adding $-\mu\log(4\det R)$ to the Hopf-cubic potential preserves exactly the six minima, raises the polar barrier to infinity and changes the Hessian to $\operatorname{diag}(2\mu+6\kappa,36\kappa)$. The remaining consistency gate is severe: derive the population Jordan variable or diagonal conditional expectation without destroying the coherent phase $w$ needed by the cubic. See [document 1011](../1000-1099/1011_the_face_population_jordan_determinant_supplies_a_polar_barrier_but_the_coherent_rank_one_projector_cannot.md).

That population/coherence compatibility gate now closes at completely-positive effective level. The phase-damping channel $\mathcal E_s(P)=\frac{1+s}{2}P+\frac{1-s}{2}DPD$ produces one mixed Jordan matrix with determinant $(1-s^2)|a|^2|b|^2$ and surviving coordinate $w_s=2s b\bar a$. Exactly $0<s<1$ supports both a positive determinant barrier and a nonzero cubic phase. The sixfold gap is suppressed by $s^6$, with Hessian $\operatorname{diag}(2\mu+6\kappa s^6,36\kappa s^6)$. The endpoints are exact failures: $s=0$ erases phase and $s=1$ restores rank one. The open gate is deriving $s$ and the sixth-order coupling from one Furey/environment interaction. See [document 1012](../1000-1099/1012_partial_face_dephasing_is_the_exact_compatibility_window_between_the_jordan_determinant_barrier_and_the_hopf_cubic_phase.md).

There is now an exact conditional parameter match to the strict-loss response. Equating the dephasing distinguishability $1-s^2$ with the established Schur defect $1-|f|^2$ fixes $s(\omega)=|f(\omega)|=(\omega^2+\alpha^2)/(\omega^2+\beta^2)$. The phase-locking coefficient is then suppressed by this ratio to the sixth power; at zero frequency in the hyperbolic parametrization it is $e^{-24\ell}$. Strict loss lies in $0<s<1$ at every finite frequency. This does not identify the channels: amplitude loss changes observed norm while phase damping preserves populations. A joint typed dilation remains the decisive gate. See [document 1013](../1000-1099/1013_the_strict_loss_schur_defect_fixes_the_partial_dephasing_overlap_conditionally_but_does_not_identify_the_two_channels.md).

The joint-dilation gate now closes pointwise for a common scalar transfer. Three normalized environment rays can be chosen with reference overlaps $f$ and mutual face-record overlap $|f|$. Compressing the same controlled isometry onto the reference ray gives $fI$, while tracing the environment gives the phase-damping channel with $s=|f|$. For strict loss the Gram determinant is $(1-|f|)^2(1+2|f|)>0$, so three environment dimensions are minimal: one reference plus exactly two auxiliary directions, matching the outgoing-port count. Causal rational globalization and unequal face transfers remain open; pointwise Gram positivity alone does not supply a time-local scattering model. See [document 1014](../1000-1099/1014_a_minimal_three_state_environment_jointly_realizes_scalar_loss_and_partial_dephasing_pointwise_but_causal_globalization_remains_open.md).

Unequal transfers are now classified pointwise. For reference amplitudes $f_0,f_1$, the allowed environment overlap is exactly the disk $|s-\bar f_1f_0|\le\sqrt{(1-|f_0|^2)(1-|f_1|^2)}$. Its interior has Gram rank three and its boundary rank two. A nonnegative real overlap exists only if this disk intersects $[0,1]$; opposite transfer phases can exclude it even when both channels are passive. Complex overlap rotates the sixfold marking by its phase, while the geometric-mean prescription is not universally admissible. The next decisive calculation is insertion of the actual normal/tangent transfer functions across the protected band. See [document 1015](../1000-1099/1015_unequal_face_transfers_admit_a_joint_environment_exactly_inside_a_gram_overlap_disk_and_relative_phase_can_exclude_real_dephasing.md).

That physical insertion now closes exactly at the unit-frequency silver checkpoint. Reciprocal scales $\gamma_n=1+\sqrt2$ and $\gamma_t=\sqrt2-1$ imply $|f_n||f_t|=e^{-4\ell}$. The distinguished Real overlap is the negative signed geometric mean $s_*=-e^{-2\ell}$. Its Gram determinant factors into manifestly positive terms for every strict loss and vanishes only at $\ell=0$, where $s_*=-1$ is the graded reflection endpoint. Thus strict loss moves the environment into rank three while preserving a real continuation of the grading. The sixth-order locking factor is $e^{-12\ell}$ at this checkpoint. Full-band analyticity remains open. See [document 1016](../1000-1099/1016_the_physical_silver_channels_select_a_negative_real_overlap_inside_the_gram_disk_at_the_unit_frequency_checkpoint.md).

The full-frequency test now supplies a no-go: no pointwise-real overlap exists on the entire real axis. The real-intersection margin is positive at zero frequency and at the silver checkpoint, but has exact tail $H(\omega,\ell)=-64\cosh^2\ell/\omega^2+O(\omega^{-4})$. Hence every strict-loss branch crosses a finite terminal boundary, and weak loss can even split the admissible set into disconnected bands. The Real theory is necessarily band-limited. A global causal continuation must instead allow complex $s(\omega)$ with $s(-\omega)=\bar s(\omega)$, turning its phase into a dispersive rotation of the sixfold marking. See [document 1017](../1000-1099/1017_the_physical_silver_real_overlap_is_necessarily_band_limited_and_must_complexify_or_stop_at_a_finite_cutoff.md).

The complex repair has a unique causal silver point. Choosing the Gram-disk centre $s_c=\bar f_tf_n$ maximizes the Gram determinant and makes the two residual environment directions orthogonal, but generically continues as the two-chart function $f_t(-z)f_n(z)$ with an upper-half-plane pole. Exactly at $\ell_*=log(1+\sqrt2)=\operatorname{arsinh}(1)$, the normal zero and reflected tangent pole coincide at unit scale, $a_n=b_t=1$, and cancel. The result $s_c(z)=((z+i(\sqrt2-1)^2)/(z+i(1+\sqrt2)^2))^2$ is a same-half-plane rational Schur overlap. This is an exact unique tuning theorem; no microscopic principle yet selects the tuned loss. See [document 1018](../1000-1099/1018_the_unique_silver_loss_asinh1_cancels_the_two_chart_pole_and_turns_the_complex_gram_center_into_a_causal_schur_overlap.md).

The selection audit now prevents that last sentence from being blurred. The positive-rate dilation contains no loss potential; the diagonal Jordan barrier is flat in log coordinates; the constrained silver action fixes the reactance amplitude $k$, not the attenuation rapidity $\ell$; and natural uncoupled positive rate costs minimize at zero loss. None of the existing actions selects $\operatorname{arsinh}(1)$. What does select it, conditionally, is the joint principle “choose the Gram-determinant maximizer and require that maximizer to be one-chart causal.” In rate language the resulting unit seam is $\kappa_{\rm obs,n}-\kappa_{\rm env,n}=\kappa_{\rm obs,t}+\kappa_{\rm env,t}=1$, with common observed/environment ratio $\sqrt2$. The exact obstruction penalty $(a_n-b_t)^2$ has unique minimum and curvature eight there, but using it as an action remains explanatory only after its physical origin is derived. See [document 1019](../1000-1099/1019_the_existing_jordan_and_rate_actions_do_not_select_asinh1_but_causal_gram_extremality_does_conditionally.md).

The first full-matrix lift attempt now fails for an exact reason. For the canonical minimal two-port Darlington completions, the relative matrix $U_t^\sharp U_n$ has determinant $((z+ib_t)/(z-ib_t))^2((z-ib_n)/(z+ib_n))^2$. At the tuned seam $b_t=a_n=1$, the scalar observed product cancels, but the full normal completion has its determinant zero at $b_n=(1+\sqrt2)^2$, not at $a_n$. A double upper-half-plane determinant pole therefore survives. Even inserting the common reference projector leaves poles outside the doubly compressed observed entry. Constant port gauges cannot repair a determinant pole. The viable architecture is consequently typed partial sewing: share the reference ray while keeping normal and tangent residual ports distinct, or add a genuinely physical nonminimal compensator. See [document 1020](../1000-1099/1020_the_unique_silver_pole_zero_seam_is_compression_specific_and_does_not_lift_to_the_minimal_darlington_relative_matrix.md).

The typed partial sewing now exists explicitly. Take a common three-state environment $E=\mathbf1_{\rm ref}\oplus A_2$ and analytic defect factors $g_j$ with $|f_j|^2+|g_j|^2=1$. The rays $e_n=(f_n,g_n,0)$ and $e_t=(f_t,0,g_t)$ define a rational-inner controlled isometry on $\mathbb C^2\otimes E\cong\mathbb C^6$. Reference compression returns $\operatorname{diag}(f_n,f_t)$, while environment trace multiplies coherence by $\bar f_tf_n$. At the tuned loss this is precisely the causal Gram-centre overlap. The three Gram rays have rank three under strict loss, proving the environment count is minimal for this data. One anisotropic face preserves only its reflection; conjugating it through the three placements makes the residual pair an exact $A_2$ orbit whose defect densities sum isotropically. See [document 1021](../1000-1099/1021_a_six_dimensional_typed_partial_sewing_realizes_the_causal_gram_centre_with_one_reference_ray_and_an_a2_residual_doublet.md).

The Gram-centre selection principle is now statistically precise. With the observed defects fixed, the conditioned residual covariance is $K_{\rm res}=\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}A&\bar\delta\\\delta&B\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$, where $\delta=s-\bar f_tf_n$. Orthogonality, maximum determinant and maximum conditional Gaussian entropy all select $\delta=0$ uniquely. Other plausible slogans do not: minimum Stinespring dimension selects the disk boundary, while normalized-Gram Rényi-2 entropy selects the disk point closest to zero, and even von Neumann entropy has explicit counterexamples. At the physical silver checkpoint the origin lies inside the disk, so quadratic entropy would choose $s=0$, not the causal centre. The viable conditional chain is therefore fixed loss variances $\to$ maximum Gaussian residual entropy $\to$ Gram centre $\to$ causality $\to\ell=\operatorname{arsinh}(1)$. See [document 1022](../1000-1099/1022_the_gram_centre_is_a_maximum_volume_gaussian_bath_not_a_minimal_environment_or_generic_quantum_entropy_state.md).

The Gaussian selector now closes directly onto the programme's Jordan spine. On the Real residual slice, $K_{\rm res}=\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}A&x\\x&B\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$ lies in $H_2(\mathbb R)_+$ and the influence functional $-\nu\log\det K_{\rm res}$ has the unique centre saddle $x=0$. In Lorentz coordinates its determinant is $u^2-v^2-x^2$, and its TKK conformal algebra is $\mathfrak{sp}(4,\mathbb R)\cong\mathfrak{so}(2,3)$. The same barrier is exactly the rank-three cubic norm restricted to $\operatorname{diag}(1,K_{\rm res})$, while scalar Schur elimination reproduces the inverse-middle Jordan triple. This unifies the Gaussian bath, cubic norm and recursion branches. The real-form gate remains decisive: an independent imaginary residual covariance enlarges the cone to $H_2(\mathbb C)$ and the conformal parent to $Spin(2,4)$. See [document 1023](../1000-1099/1023_the_gaussian_gram_selector_is_the_h2r_jordan_logdet_barrier_and_a_peirce_slice_of_the_rank_three_cubic.md).

Conjugate-frequency reality does not by itself close that real-form gate. Writing $\delta=x+iy$, the condition $\delta(-\omega)=\bar\delta(\omega)$ makes $x$ even and $y$ odd, but leaves both as independent positive-frequency quadratures. A simple upper-half-plane analytic rational response already provides a counterexample to “causal plus Real implies pointwise real.” The $H_2(\mathbb R)$ reduction is exact at zero frequency, for static/equal-time covariance, or when an extra reciprocal/evenness relation forces $y=0$. The credible architecture therefore separates an $H_2(\mathbb C)$ retarded-response parent from an $H_2(\mathbb R)$ reciprocal noise sector. A Keldysh or antiunitary derivation of that split is now the active gate. See [document 1024](../1000-1099/1024_conjugate_frequency_reality_pairs_but_does_not_remove_the_imaginary_residual_mode_so_spin23_is_static_or_reciprocal.md).

The minimal Keldysh split now exists at quadratic effective level. In $S_{\rm SK}=\phi_q^\dagger K^R\phi_c+\phi_c^\dagger K^A\phi_q+i\phi_q^\dagger N\phi_q$, a reciprocal complex retarded kernel has a real symmetric positive spectral matrix $\rho=(K^R-K^A)/(2i)$. A common-temperature KMS multiplier is real and scalar, so $N_\beta=\coth(\beta\omega/2)\rho$ lies in $H_2(\mathbb R)_+$ while $K^R$ remains complex and causal. The Jordan log determinant belongs to this positive noise block, not the full Keldysh or retarded kernel. Generic nonreciprocity makes the cross-spectral density complex and restores the $H_2(\mathbb C)$ parent. This closes the effective two-kernel architecture while leaving its Furey/Standard-Model derivation open. See [document 1025](../1000-1099/1025_a_reciprocal_keldysh_kernel_places_noise_in_h2r_while_retarded_response_remains_complex_and_fdt_preserves_the_split.md).

The Furey antiunitary audit now separates the two needed symmetries exactly. The ungraded source exchange $F_CK$ descends to $\Theta=XK$, squares to $+I$, and fixes a three-dimensional positive Hermitian cone unitarily equivalent to $H_2(\mathbb R)_+$; it is the viable noise-reciprocity candidate. The graded antiunitary $F_CpK$ descends to $\Gamma=\varepsilon K$, squares to $-I$, and fixes only scalar Hermitian matrices, so it cannot preserve a generic anisotropic silver noise cone. It remains the coherent quaternionic sewing. The two antiunitaries anticommute and their relative product is precisely the $h\chi$ grading. No new operator was invented, but physical invariance of the bath under $F_CK$ is still unproved. See [document 1026](../1000-1099/1026_the_ungraded_furey_exchange_antiunitary_not_the_graded_quaternionic_one_is_the_candidate_noise_reciprocity_selector.md).

The residual-current construction now exposes a representation fork. If the bath variables are the linear aggregate ports $a=(L,Q)$, document 1026 applies directly and $XK$ is the required real structure. If instead the physical currents are the quadratic relationships $J_x=a^\dagger\sigma_xa$ and $J_y=a^\dagger\sigma_ya$, both have equal parity—both even under $XK$ and both odd under $\varepsilon K$—so either antiunitary permits a symmetric Onsager covariance. There is no contradiction: one statement concerns Hermitian kernels on the fundamental port space, the other a covariance on the adjoint coordinate plane. The quadratic covariance is a connected four-point object and is not the minimal Gram kernel without a Wick or influence-functional derivation. The linear route remains the direct dilation candidate; the relationship route remains a separate microscopic observable branch. See [document 1027](../1000-1099/1027_the_furey_residual_current_gate_splits_into_linear_port_and_quadratic_relationship_typings_with_different_antiunitary_tests.md).

The Gaussian Wick bridge now closes kinematically and surjectively. For a proper Gaussian aggregate with $XK$-fixed covariance $C=pI+x\sigma_x+y\sigma_y$, the connected relationship-current covariance is $M_J=2\det(C)I+4rr^T$, $r=(x,y)$. Every positive real symmetric $2\times2$ kernel arises from this formula: if its eigenvalues are $\lambda_+\ge\lambda_-$, choose $p^2=(\lambda_++\lambda_-)/4$ and $|r|^2=(\lambda_+-\lambda_-)/4$. Thus every residual $H_2(\mathbb R)$ Gram kernel has a proper-Gaussian Furey-root realization. The generic root is two-to-one under $r\mapsto-r$, and the map is not a Jordan, inverse or Schur homomorphism; it does not transport the log-determinant dynamics automatically. See [document 1028](../1000-1099/1028_the_proper_gaussian_wick_map_from_the_xk_fixed_furey_root_surjects_onto_every_positive_residual_h2r_covariance.md).

Applying that inverse Wick map to the tuned silver defect reveals a new regularity gate. The normal defect exceeds the tangent defect at every finite nonzero real frequency, but their anisotropy vanishes quadratically at zero. Hence the ordinary even coherence root behaves as $|\omega|$ and has a cusp. The real-axis analytic root instead changes sign, $r(-\omega)=-r(\omega)$, and its covariance obeys the graded sewing law $C(-\omega)=D C(\omega)D$. The smooth even relationship kernel therefore descends from a sign-sensitive frequency double cover. This does **not** yet construct an upper-half-plane matrix spectral factor; that is now the causal gate. See [document 1029](../1000-1099/1029_the_tuned_silver_wick_root_is_a_graded_frequency_double_cover_because_the_defect_anisotropy_has_a_quadratic_zero.md).

That causal gate now splits cleanly. A constant Hadamard basis reduces the root covariance to positive scalar spectra $c_\pm=p\pm r$ with $c_+(-\omega)=c_-(\omega)$; their logarithmic integrability constructs scalar outer factors and hence a graded upper-half-plane matrix factor satisfying $H(-\omega)=D\overline{H(\omega)}D$. But the exact factor cannot be rational or finite-state: $C_{11}\sim P/|\omega|$, and $r^2$ contains the nonsquare quartic $\omega^4+18\omega^2+1$.
