Worse, $\int\operatorname{tr}C\,d\omega$ diverges logarithmically. An even causal source filter makes the root finite-variance and preserves graded sewing, but its Wick image acquires the square of the filter. Thus generalized causality closes positively, while exact rationality closes negatively and physical finite variance moves to a genuine UV-completion gate. See [document 1030](../1000-1099/1030_the_tuned_wick_root_has_a_graded_outer_factor_but_no_rational_or_finite_variance_continuum_realization_without_uv_completion.md).

The anomalous-covariance audit opens a second exact Wick route. For commuting Gaussian response amplitudes fixed simultaneously by $XK$ and $\varepsilon K$, the complete common covariance sector is $C=pI$, $S=sI+iv\sigma_z$ with $p^2\ge s^2+v^2$. Its relationship-current covariance is surjective onto every positive real $2\times2$ kernel, with target orientation encoded by the square $(s+iv)^2$ and the usual root fiber $(s,v)\sim(-s,-v)$. This unifies the two antiunitaries algebraically but does not cure the $1/|\omega|$ UV tail. For raw two-component **equal-time/even-frequency** CAR pairing, antisymmetry makes the two fixed conditions incompatible; the odd-frequency correction is treated below. See [document 1031](../1000-1099/1031_anomalous_gaussian_pairing_gives_a_two_antiunitary_wick_surjection_but_only_for_a_neutral_commuting_or_nambu_root.md).

The minimal enlarged **static/even-frequency** CAR symmetry sector now exists. Doubling the response doublet and letting both antiunitaries act on the channel factor produces an exact two-real-dimensional common pairing plane $F=\varepsilon_{\rm copy}\otimes(sI+iv\sigma_z)$. Exhaustive real-linear classification gives dimension zero before doubling and two afterward; four complex modes are minimal within that repeated-fundamental static branch. Assigning opposite charges to the copies makes the pair neutral, and bounded local quasifree states can support it. But this closes only symmetry, charge and local positivity. The fermionic Wick covariance has not yet been shown to reproduce the positive Jordan cone; the Pfaffian sees only $s^2+v^2$. See [document 1032](../1000-1099/1032_doubling_the_carrier_is_the_minimal_two_antiunitary_car_pairing_repair_and_opposite_copy_charge_makes_it_neutral.md).

Frequency-resolved CAR exchange reopens the undoubled branch. The exact law $F(-\omega)=-F(\omega)^T$ makes even-frequency pairing internally antisymmetric but odd-frequency pairing internally symmetric. The unrestricted two-component common fixed space of $XK$ and $\varepsilon K$ is precisely the symmetric plane $sI+iv\sigma_z$, so it is a valid odd-frequency CAR candidate. The tuned analytic root already has the required odd sign. This corrects the earlier minimality claim: doubling is necessary only for static/even-frequency pairing. It does not yet close the physics, because fermionic current noise is a frequency convolution rather than a pointwise Wick square, gauge/Lorentz typing remains open, and the $1/\omega$ tail is only principal-value cancelling, not absolutely UV integrable. See [document 1033](../1000-1099/1033_odd_frequency_car_pairing_reopens_the_single_doublet_and_limits_the_doubled_car_minimality_to_the_static_even_frequency_branch.md).

The first convolution calculation is now exact. At the tuned loss the tangent defect is simply a frequency-dilated normal defect, $B(\Omega)=A(\sqrt q\,\Omega)$. The inverse-time prototype changes sign, excluding any single positive scalar autocorrelation. Yet two rational odd kernels at the half pole scale, $\phi_0=\nu/(\nu^2+b^2)$ and $\phi_1=\nu/(\nu^2+b^2)^2$ with $b=\sqrt q/2$, reconstruct $A$ exactly through one positive autocorrelation and one negative cross-correlation. The resulting two-kernel coefficient matrix has forced signature $(1,1)$. This makes the microscopic target explicit: a fermionic/Keldysh vertex must generate that indefinite form while the final observable spectrum remains positive. The construction is not yet a causal loop because the symmetric boundary kernels are not individually retarded propagators. See [document 1034](../1000-1099/1034_the_tuned_defects_have_an_exact_signed_two_kernel_odd_frequency_convolution_but_no_single_positive_scalar_autocorrelation.md).

The boundary-loop causality concern now closes structurally. Each odd kernel is an exact combination of upper-half-plane analytic retarded poles and advanced conjugates; contour closure kills the $RR$ and $AA$ loops, leaving only mixed $RA/AR$ sectors. The signed coefficient form also has the explicit factorization $G=L^TDL$ with $D=\operatorname{diag}(1,-1)$, the literal grading already supplied by $h\chi$ and by the relative product of the two Furey antiunitaries. Thus the causal sector pattern and required Lorentzian sign both match existing programme data. The remaining non-circular gate is severe but precise: derive the tuned residue matrix $L$, the $D$ insertion, and the occupation/KMS factors from one source action. Congruence alone is not a microscopic identification. See [document 1035](../1000-1099/1035_the_signed_odd_frequency_convolution_is_purely_mixed_ra_ar_and_its_lorentzian_metric_reduces_exactly_to_the_furey_grading.md).

Canonical pole-jet normalization removes most of the apparent residue freedom. The repeated-pole kernel obeys $b^2\phi_1=-\tfrac12\partial_{\log b}\phi_0$, so $(\phi_0,b^2\phi_1)$ is a dimension-matched mode/scale-jet pair. In that basis $G=\alpha\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}1&-\rho_*\\-\rho_*&0\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$ with $\rho_*=(6\sqrt2-4)/7=2\tanh(2\ell_*)/(2+\tanh(2\ell_*))$. The residue map factors as $L=\sqrt\alpha N_-\operatorname{diag}(1,\rho_*)$, where $N_-=\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}1&-1\\0&1\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$ is an exact unit shear. Thus no new continuous shape parameter remains beyond the tuned loss. The live typing gate is whether the pole-order jet intertwines with the already established Jordan/TKK carrier plane; identical matrices alone do not prove it. See [document 1036](../1000-1099/1036_after_log_scale_normalization_the_convolution_residue_map_is_a_unit_shear_times_one_scale_fixed_by_the_tuned_loss.md).

The pole-jet comparison now reaches exact Lie closure. Log-scale translation on the first primal jet is generated by $F=E_{21}$; its dual residue action is $-F^T=-E_{12}$, whose unit exponential is exactly $N_-$. With $E=E_{12}$ and $D=\operatorname{diag}(1,-1)$, the identities $[E,F]=D$, $[D,E]=2E$ and $[D,F]=-2F$ close $\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb R)$. The canonical block embedding $A\mapsto\operatorname{diag}(A,-A^T)$ places this triple in $\mathfrak{sp}_4(\mathbb R)\cong\mathfrak{so}(2,3)$. This is an exact response-to-Lie-algebra bridge, but not yet the physical TKK identification: a specific Jordan idempotent, Peirce typing and intertwiner to the established carrier pair are still required. See [document 1037](../1000-1099/1037_the_pole_jet_residue_shear_and_furey_grading_close_an_exact_sl2_triple_inside_sp4_but_the_physical_jordan_idempotent_is_still_untyped.md).

The real TKK orbit is now classified, correcting the primitive-idempotent expectation. The pole-jet nilpotent has rank two, so it cannot be conjugate to a primitive rank-one projector triple. It is also distinct from the positive identity orbit: the induced symmetric form has inertia $(1,1)$ rather than $(2,0)$. An explicit symplectic coordinate exchange followed by an orthogonal channel rotation sends the triple to $E_D=\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}0&D\\0&0\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$, $F_D=\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}0&0\\D&0\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)$ and $H_0=\operatorname{diag}(I,-I)$. The Jordan object is therefore the split involution/tripotent $D$, with $D=e_+-e_-$ packaging the complementary normal/tangent primitive projectors. The remaining physical gate is no longer which Jordan object, but why the source implements the required canonical coordinate/momentum exchange. See [document 1038](../1000-1099/1038_the_pole_jet_sl2_is_the_tkk_orbit_of_the_split_jordan_involution_D_not_a_primitive_positive_idempotent.md).

That canonical exchange is now expressed in established variables. It is the channel-controlled Fourier quarter-turn $S_-=I_{\rm phase}\otimes e_++J_{\rm phase}\otimes e_-$, so $S_-^2=I\otimes D$. It is not the realified antiunitary $\Gamma_{\mathbb R}=K_{\rm phase}\otimes\varepsilon_{\rm channel}$, whose square is $-I$. Instead $\Gamma_{\mathbb R}$ conjugates $S_-$ to the complementary inverse turn $S_+^{-1}$, and their cascade is the graded complex structure $-J\otimes D$. Thus the sideband/Pfaffian operator supplies the correct exchange-and-orientation action on the two controlled Fourier faces, but the existing local interface does not itself mix $q_2$ with $p_2$. The remaining gate is a boundary action generating the channel-selective quarter-turn. See [document 1039](../1000-1099/1039_the_split_tkk_intertwiner_is_a_channel_controlled_fourier_quarter_turn_whose_square_is_D_not_the_realified_furey_antiunitary.md).

That effective boundary-action gate now closes. The positive-semidefinite quadratic Hamiltonian $H_-=(\omega_0/2)(q^Te_-q+p^Te_-p)$ generates $J\otimes e_-$, and its flow at $T=\pi/(2\omega_0)$ is exactly $S_-$. Because the generator is both Hamiltonian and Euclidean-antisymmetric, the transformation is symplectic, orthogonal and passive: the split Jordan orbit does not require an unstable or negative-energy microscopic mode. The realified Furey antiunitary transports this generator to the complementary inverse oscillator, preserving the face-exchange interpretation. Effective existence is therefore established; what remains open is why the microscopic Furey/Higgs system selects the projector $e_-$ and the integrated quarter-turn angle. See [document 1040](../1000-1099/1040_a_positive_projected_oscillator_generates_the_controlled_fourier_tkk_intertwiner_as_a_passive_quarter_period_boundary_flow.md).

The integrated angle is now fixed conditionally rather than fitted. For a face-controlled passive canonical flow $S(\Theta)=I\otimes e_++R(\Theta)\otimes e_-$, the carrier condition $S(\Theta)^2=I\otimes D$ is equivalent to $R(2\Theta)=-I$, hence $\Theta=\pi/2\pmod\pi$. The only passive roots are the two orientations $\pm J$ on the negative face, and the least positive action is the quarter-turn. This does not fix frequency and duration separately. Passivity is essential: dropping orthogonality leaves the continuous squeezed family $gJg^{-1}$. The live microscopic questions are now projector selection, orientation selection and scale allocation. See [document 1041](../1000-1099/1041_passivity_and_the_square_root_of_D_quantize_the_projected_oscillator_action_to_a_quarter_turn_modulo_orientation.md).

The first attempt to combine this turn with the older local sewing produces a sharp no-go. If every one of the six real sewing edges also carries one $\pm\pi/2$ controlled turn, each transported face accumulates three odd quarter-turns. All $2^6$ orientation assignments are therefore noncentral after six edges, in either operator ordering. For a uniform edge $M=ES_-$ or $S_-E$, $M^2=-J\otimes I$, $M^4=-I$, $M^6=J\otimes I$ and $M^8=I$; twelve applications give $-I$ and twenty-four give $+I$. The TKK turn cannot simply be appended to each placement edge without changing the cover. The live repairs are a distinguished seam, paired insertions, a genuinely semilinear orientation reversal or an enlarged 24-step cover. See [document 1042](../1000-1099/1042_a_quarter_turn_on_every_sewn_edge_obstructs_the_six_cycle_central_holonomy_and_generically_doubles_the_coherent_cover.md).

The sparse and semilinear classification now narrows those repairs. Replacing bare sewing by the realified antiunitary does not make an all-edge turn central: all 64 signed schedules still fail. A single distinguished turn also fails. The minimal nonzero compatible architecture is instead a twist–antitwist pair on two edges of the same parity, hence on the same transported face; the two orientations must be opposite. Exactly 12 signed two-seam schedules preserve the original six-edge $-I$ holonomy. Four insertions produce 36 $+I$ and 36 $-I$ central schedules, but lack a selector. The leading architecture is therefore a localized defect pair rather than a uniform edge decoration. See [document 1043](../1000-1099/1043_semilinearity_does_not_repair_an_all_edge_quarter_turn_but_a_minimal_same_face_twist_antitwist_pair_preserves_the_six_cycle.md).

Placement symmetry reduces those 12 minimal pairs to one binary datum. Cyclic rotations give two size-six orbits, according to whether the antitwist lies $+2$ or $-2$ edges from the twist. Bare reflection exchanges them, but the physical Real reflection also reverses the quarter-turn sign, so its spatial and internal reversals cancel and each orbit is preserved. The minimal interface therefore has no continuous position or separation modulus; conditionally it carries one relational chirality $\chi=\pm1$. A retarded/advanced prescription is the leading candidate selector, but the microscopic action on seam operators must first confirm the sign-reversing Real typing. See [document 1044](../1000-1099/1044_the_minimal_twist_antitwist_pair_has_two_chiral_placement_orbits_preserved_by_the_physical_real_reflection.md).

The interval is globally holonomy-trivial but locally relationship-active. Relative to undefected sewing, its transfer alternates $S_+$ and $S_-$ until the antitwist restores identity. Isotropic phase covariance is blind to this, whereas traceless quadrature tensors reverse sign. More sharply, writing the carrier columns as $u,v\in\mathbb R^2$, the turn $v\mapsto Jv$ rotates the relationship doublet $(c,a)=(u^Tv,u^TJv)$ as $(c,a)\mapsto(a,-c)$. Since $\det(\Psi^T\Psi)=a^2$, the twisted Jordan Gram exchanges its off-diagonal square $c^2$ with its determinant. The defect is therefore a local converter between symmetric correlation and oriented-area/Pfaffian data. The remaining gate is whether both signed quantities survive gauge completion as physical currents. See [document 1045](../1000-1099/1045_the_paired_twist_interval_is_locally_visible_as_a_quarter_rotation_between_jordan_correlation_and_oriented_area.md).

The Pfaffian comparison now closes exactly at response-root level. For the $2\times2$ carrier matrix $\Psi=(u\;v)$, $\Psi^TJ_{\rm phase}\Psi=(\det\Psi)\varepsilon_{LQ}$, so $a=u^TJv=\det\Psi$ is precisely the Pfaffian of the phase symplectic form pulled back to the lepton–colour response doublet. Its square is the Jordan Gram determinant, and its sign is exactly what the Gram map forgets. It is odd under $h\chi$ and exchange, matching the weak Pfaffian orientation representation; the controlled turn rotates correlation into this coefficient. What remains open is not algebraic Pfaffian status but the microscopic intertwiner identifying the response-root alternating form with the weak/Higgs bilinear, especially in the presence of complex three-generation CP phases. See [document 1046](../1000-1099/1046_the_oriented_area_is_exactly_the_pfaffian_line_of_the_response_root_but_its_microscopic_weak_higgs_identification_remains_open.md).

The sign becomes a genuine conditional order parameter once the phase symplectic orientation is retained. For fixed $Q>0$, the complete Gram fiber is $O(2)$; quotienting by it erases orientation. But transformations preserving both the phase metric and fixed $J_{\rm phase}$ form only $SO(2)$. On that oriented root bundle, $\eta=\det\Psi/\sqrt{\det Q}=\pm1$ is gauge invariant, and every continuous sheet change forces $\det\Psi=\det Q=0$, hence a rank-one Jordan crossing. The physical fork is precise: if reflections are gauge, $\eta$ is unphysical; if they are global or antiunitary symmetries, the sheets are distinct and symmetry-related. A bound zero mode does not follow until a CAR-complete operator index is constructed. See [document 1047](../1000-1099/1047_fixing_the_phase_symplectic_orientation_reduces_the_gram_root_gauge_to_SO2_and_turns_the_pfaffian_sign_into_a_rank_one_separated_Z2_sheet.md).

A minimal conditional index witness now exists. Taking the raw response Pfaffian $a(x)=\det\Psi(x)$ as the real mass in $H=-i\sigma_2\partial_x+a(x)\sigma_1$ gives class BDI with $T=K$, $C=\sigma_3K$ and chiral symmetry $\sigma_3$. For $a(x)=\tanh x$, the unique normalized zero mode is $\psi_0=(\operatorname{sech}x/\sqrt2,0)^T$. Its mass zero is exactly the rank-one Jordan crossing. This proves that the oriented-root wall can support a protected fermionic mode under a concrete CAR operator, while sharply isolating the unresolved step: no Furey/Higgs or Standard-Model action yet derives $a$ as that real mass, and a complex second mass can evade the crossing. See [document 1048](../1000-1099/1048_if_the_response_pfaffian_is_a_BDI_dirac_mass_then_its_rank_one_wall_binds_one_exact_zero_mode_but_the_coupling_is_not_yet_derived.md).

The holonomy-compatible paired architecture removes any automatic exact-zero prediction. Its kink and antikink carry opposite BDI indices, so the total index is zero. Their endpoint modes have opposite chirality and admit the symmetry-preserving coupling $H_{\rm end}=t\sigma_1$, producing energies $\pm|t|$; locality can make $t$ exponentially small with seam separation but does not force it to vanish. Moreover a second mass $b\sigma_3$ permits $(a,b)$ to rotate from $(+1,0)$ to $(-1,0)$ at fixed gap, breaking the real chiral protection exactly as a complex Pfaffian phase evades a sign crossing. The paired seam can therefore yield a near-zero doublet only conditionally; exact zeros need an additional charge or selection rule. See [document 1049](../1000-1099/1049_the_minimal_paired_seam_has_zero_total_BDI_index_so_its_endpoint_modes_can_hybridize_and_a_complex_second_mass_removes_sign_protection.md).

The two established Furey antiunitaries supply exactly such a conditional selection fork, but their dynamical roles are not yet typed. On the endpoint doublet, $\Theta=XK$ has square $+1$, $\Gamma=\varepsilon K$ has square $-1$, and their relative product is the chiral grading $\pm D$. If $\Theta$ is time reversal and $\Gamma$ particle-hole, the CI-pattern constraints allow both $X$ and $Y$ mixing, so splitting and complex rotation remain. If the roles reverse, the DIII-pattern constraints force the entire minimal endpoint Hamiltonian to zero: the pair is Kramers-degenerate and particle-hole pinned. Exact protection therefore depends on the commutation signs $\Theta H\Theta^{-1}$ and $\Gamma H\Gamma^{-1}$ supplied by the microscopic boundary action, not on antiunitary squares alone. See [document 1050](../1000-1099/1050_the_two_furey_antiunitaries_protect_or_split_the_paired_wall_endpoints_according_to_their_time_reversal_particle_hole_role_assignment.md).

The existing Keldysh typing now supplies a minimal CI witness but not a selection theorem. For the reciprocal endpoint kernel $H_{\rm end}=tX$, $\Theta H\Theta^{-1}=+H$ while $\Gamma H\Gamma^{-1}=-H$, exactly the CI role assignment and the full splitting plane of document 1050. This coheres with $\Theta$ as the established reciprocity real structure and $\Gamma$ as coherent orientation-reversing sewing. Yet scalar noise $N=nI$ is fixed by both antiunitaries, so covariance invariance alone cannot determine Hamiltonian parity or exclude DIII. The fork can only be closed by projecting the microscopic coherent kernel onto the two endpoint wavefunctions while retaining retarded/advanced and frequency-reflection data. See [document 1051](../1000-1099/1051_the_minimal_reciprocal_endpoint_kernel_realizes_the_CI_furey_role_assignment_but_noise_reciprocity_alone_cannot_exclude_DIII.md).

The independent four-seam branch has now been fully quotiented and remains underselected. Its 72 central schedules form 14 cyclic orbits and 11 orbits under either bare or sign-reversing Real dihedral symmetry, though the partitions differ. Under the physical Real action, the 36 $+I$ schedules occupy five orbits and the 36 $-I$ schedules six; the only size-three high-symmetry orbits lie in the original $-I$ sector. No unique or exceptionally symmetric holonomy-flipping architecture emerges. This pins four seams behind an additional action/charge selector and strengthens the two-seam pair as the minimal live branch. See [document 1052](../1000-1099/1052_the_four_seam_central_sector_remains_eleven_real_dihedral_orbits_so_placement_symmetry_does_not_select_a_sign_changing_architecture.md).

The paired-wall endpoint kernel is now realized by an explicit continuum calculation rather than only posited algebraically. For the piecewise Pfaffian mass \(-m_0,+m_0,-m_0\) across a length-\(L\) interval, the normalized isolated kink and antikink modes have the exact projected coupling \(t=-m_0e^{-m_0L}\). Midpoint reflection completes the Furey operations into \(PXK\), which commutes and squares to \(+1\), and \(P\varepsilon K\), which anticommutes and squares to \(-1\). This concrete minimal wall therefore selects CI and makes the residual endpoint scale a function of \(L/\xi=m_0L\). The statement is an exact projection theorem, not an exact finite-wall spectral formula, and the Pfaffian mass coupling remains to be derived from the microscopic source. See [document 1053](../1000-1099/1053_the_explicit_piecewise_pfaffian_kink_antikink_wall_has_exact_projected_overlap_and_realizes_the_CI_furey_role_assignment.md).

The full piecewise spectrum now calibrates that projection exactly. Matching the decaying exterior spinors through the positive-mass interval gives \(|E|=m_0\exp[-L\sqrt{m_0^2-E^2}]\), with precisely one physical chiral pair for every finite \(L>0\). Its large-separation expansion begins \(E/m_0=e^{-\ell}+(\ell/2)e^{-3\ell}+\cdots\), \(\ell=m_0L\), so the projected overlap is the exact leading term and underestimates the true energy slightly. The correction falls below \(0.4\%\) by \(\ell=3\). This closes the model-level spectrum while leaving the common scale bridge—from discrete seam distance and odd-frequency pole decay to \(m_0L\)—open. See [document 1054](../1000-1099/1054_the_exact_piecewise_pfaffian_kink_antikink_spectrum_obeys_an_implicit_exponential_law_and_calibrates_the_endpoint_projection.md).

The first scale-bridge audit shows exactly why that last gap matters. If the internal half-poles \(1/2\) and \((3+2\sqrt2)/2\) are provisionally used as two wall masses and the two-edge separation is written honestly as \(L=2a\), then the exact wall levels form a one-parameter family. Their ordering reverses at \(a\approx0.338565\) in pole units. Hence neither a numerical splitting nor even a robust hierarchy follows from the current algebra. A single source action must type frequency as spatial decay and derive the physical edge length before this branch can become predictive. See [document 1055](../1000-1099/1055_the_odd_frequency_half_poles_give_a_conditional_wall_spectrum_but_the_underived_edge_length_controls_even_the_level_ordering.md).

The stronger common-clock proposal is now excluded exactly. If both half-poles were passive oscillator frequencies acting for one edge duration \(T\), the square-root condition would require their ratio to be an odd-over-odd rational number. The actual ratio \(3+2\sqrt2\) is irrational. Pell-type odd rational approximants give arbitrarily close multi-turn recurrences, but no exact duration and no minimal clock. The live repair is to separate the common angular quarter-turn from the silver radial/scale-jet dilation, or to derive channel-dependent velocities or durations. See [document 1056](../1000-1099/1056_the_irrational_silver_half_pole_ratio_forbids_a_common_exact_passive_quarter_turn_edge_clock_but_has_pell_near_recurrences.md).

The angular/radial repair now has an exact local algebra. The controlled compact turn \(J\otimes e_-\) and split dilation \(K\otimes D\) generate a squeeze \(X\otimes e_-\), closing \(\mathfrak{sl}_2(\mathbb R)_-\oplus\mathbb R\mathcal H_+\). Dilation conjugates the passive Fourier root into the previously classified continuous squeezed square-root family. This supplies a concrete curvature mechanism but not full Spin(2,3): the closure is four-dimensional and contains no cross-face mixing. The next bounded test is whether one already motivated Furey/Jordan exchange generator expands it to all ten dimensions. See [document 1057](../1000-1099/1057_the_compact_controlled_turn_and_split_tkk_dilation_generate_a_one_face_sl2_plus_a_commuting_dilation_not_full_sp4.md).

That bounded test succeeds after correcting the finite-swap/infinitesimal-generator type. Adjoining the Hamiltonian channel rotation \(I\otimes\varepsilon\) to the controlled turn and split dilation generates the canonical ten-dimensional basis \(I\otimes\varepsilon\oplus\{J,X,K\}\otimes\{I,X,D\}\), exactly \(\mathfrak{sp}_4(\mathbb R)\). Removing any one seed leaves dimension three or four. This is the clearest exact Jordan–Furey recursion to the Spin(2,3) Lie algebra yet obtained. Its sole sharp physical gate is whether the source-native Furey \(\varepsilon\), presently established inside an antiunitary, is also generated by a continuous coherent flow on the same carrier. See [document 1058](../1000-1099/1058_a_three_generator_jordan_furey_recursion_closes_all_of_sp4_but_requires_the_furey_channel_rotation_as_a_continuous_flow.md).

The response algebra supplies that continuous flow exactly. The real skew generator \(G_{\rm src}=F_Cp\) squares to \(-I\), preserves the aggregate subspace and obeys \(Ae^{tG_{\rm src}}=e^{t\varepsilon}A\). Its realification is precisely \(I_{\rm phase}\otimes\varepsilon_{\rm channel}\), so the three-generator route is now source-complete at response-representation level. The remaining gate is an action, not a matrix: generic diagonal response coefficients break this rotation unless \(r_u=r_e\) and \(r_\nu=r_d\), and a microscopic continuous lepton–quark rotation needs a gauge- and charge-compatible mediator. See [document 1059](../1000-1099/1059_the_linear_furey_product_FCp_exponentiates_exactly_to_the_missing_continuous_sp4_channel_rotation_but_its_action_term_is_open.md).

At effective response level, even the action gate now has a minimal positive realization. Since \(iF_Cp\) is Hermitian with eigenvalues \(\pm1\), the pulse Hamiltonian \(H=\mu I+i gF_Cp\) generates the exact flow and is positive semidefinite whenever \(\mu\ge|g|\); the shift contributes only a common phase. This does not require the crossed degeneracies of an exact bulk symmetry. It converts the remaining problem into a sharply microscopic one: construct the gauge-, charge-, Lorentz- and CAR-complete operator or mediator whose response reduction is this pulse. See [document 1060](../1000-1099/1060_a_positive_first_order_response_pulse_generates_the_full_furey_channel_flow_without_bulk_degeneracy_but_needs_microscopic_charge_completion.md).

The minimal microscopic lane is now classified. No Standard-Model-only dimension-five operator can neutralize the colored crossed bilinears. At dimension six, \((QL)(u^ce^c)\) and \((QL)(d^c\nu^c)\) are gauge-singlet, Lorentz-scalar and separately \(B,L\)-conserving, and one \(S_1=(\bar3,1)_{1/3}\) mediator generates both at tree level. This exactly reuses the proton-safer colored branch of the doubled-Furey graph. However, matching \(iF_Cp\) requires equal Wilson magnitudes and opposite oriented phases, which gauge symmetry does not select. The next calculation is the explicit response matching/Fierz map and the source of that relative phase. See [document 1061](../1000-1099/1061_the_minimal_sm_field_completion_of_the_furey_channel_pulse_is_a_pair_of_dimension_six_S1_exchange_operators_with_an_underived_relative_phase.md).

Tree matching adds a decisive rank constraint. One \(S_1\) copy produces a rank-one Wilson Gram matrix, so nonzero \(QL\!-\!u^ce^c\) and \(QL\!-\!d^c\nu^c\) edges force an additional \((u^ce^c)^\dagger(d^c\nu^c)\) edge. A pure sparse \(F_Cp\) pulse therefore needs at least two mediator directions. Two copies suffice: orthogonal right-bilinear coupling vectors and a shared left vector realize coefficients \(+i,-i,0\) exactly. The open gate is now whether the doubled Furey geometry derives this mass-weighted orthogonality and phase, rather than merely suggesting two links. See [document 1062](../1000-1099/1062_one_S1_mediator_cannot_generate_a_pure_FCp_pulse_but_two_orthogonal_copies_realize_the_exact_oriented_star.md).

That source gate now closes negatively for the minimal endpoint construction. Its three colored links count coupling slots, while one complex \(S_1\) and its conjugate can support all three; the conjugate is not a second propagating copy, and no scalar kinetic multiplicity or mass operator was constructed. Adding two copies by hand still gives the exact tree witness, but gauge symmetry and separate \(B,L\) allow a general Hermitian copy-space mass matrix. Worse, the shared \(QL\) coupling contributes δ\(M^2\propto\lambda_L\lambda_L^\dagger\), which is off diagonal for the witness and regenerates \(C_{ud}=\kappa/[m^2(m^2+2\kappa)]\). The pure-star ±\(i\) phases have no invariant cycle phase either; they become oriented data only relative to an independently fixed Furey/Yukawa frame. The live branch is therefore a protected enlarged multiplet, a boundary kernel, or an accepted triangular third edge—not the minimal doubled-arrow model alone. See [document 1063](../1000-1099/1063_the_doubled_furey_links_do_not_supply_two_S1_copies_and_the_shared_QL_leg_radiatively_breaks_the_naive_orthogonality.md).

Flavor amplification now makes the microscopic cost explicit. For coupling matrices into an \(N\)-dimensional mediator space, the pure-star condition implies the exact bound \(N\ge\operatorname{rank}C_{Lu}+\operatorname{rank}C_{Ld}\). A literal generationwise \(F_Cp\otimes I_3\) has two rank-three arms and therefore needs at least six \(S_1\) directions; six algebraically suffice, but the shared-left loop regenerates a full forbidden \(3\times3\) block. Two copies remain possible only for one rank-one collective flavor mode. The programme must therefore decide whether its four-channel carrier is a generation aggregate or a universal action on three independent modes; those are no longer interchangeable readings. See [document 1064](../1000-1099/1064_full_rank_generationwise_FCp_requires_at_least_six_S1_copies_and_the_shared_leg_regenerates_a_three_by_three_forbidden_block.md).

Accepting the third edge reveals a new but narrower bridge. Every one-mediator rank-one Gram triangle has positive Bargmann product \(C_{Lu}C_{ud}C_{dL}\), so its loop phase is exactly zero despite complex-looking edges. The shared-leg correction to the rank-two star instead generates a positive \(u-d\) edge between the two imaginary arms, making the invariant product negative and its phase exactly \(\pi\). The obstruction therefore creates a genuine central \(\mathbb Z_2\) flux. It still cannot supply chirality: conjugation fixes a negative real loop, so twist and antitwist remain indistinguishable. The next gate is an explicit intertwiner from this Wilson-loop sign to the response Pfaffian sheet plus an independent orientation-odd datum. See [document 1065](../1000-1099/1065_the_one_S1_triangle_has_trivial_Bargmann_holonomy_but_shared_leg_mixing_generates_a_rephasing_invariant_pi_flux.md).

The explicit topology now sharpens that gate into a no-go and a surviving bridge. After real rephasing and inverse-mass whitening, the three mediator directions form an odd loop in \(\mathbb{RP}^1\); its unit-vector lift returns at the antipode, exactly reproducing the negative Bargmann sign. Completing a ray canonically to the oriented frame \((r,Jr)\) sends this deck action to \(\Psi\mapsto-\Psi\). In two dimensions that is central \(-I\in SO(2)\) and preserves \(\det\Psi\), so it cannot exchange the response-Pfaffian sheets. The Wilson flux naturally matches the coherent central-sign branch, not the Pfaffian orientation branch. A one-column reflection or reversal of \(J\) remains an independent required datum. See [document 1066](../1000-1099/1066_the_Wilson_pi_flux_is_the_nontrivial_RP1_line_holonomy_but_its_canonical_frame_lift_is_central_minus_I_not_a_Pfaffian_sheet_flip.md).

The metric comparison with placement now succeeds at one unique point. Writing \(r=\kappa/m^2\), the scale-free inverse-mass metric is \(\left(\begin{smallmatrix}1+r&-r\\-r&1+r\end{smallmatrix}\right)\). The three Wilson rays have \(A_2\) overlap magnitudes \(1/2\), and their two-face reflection product has order three, iff \(r=1\). Then \(K=G_{A_2}/(3m^2)\), the rays are \(e_1,e_2,e_1+e_2\), and reflections normal to \(d\) and \(l\) are exactly the established placement matrices \(F\) and \(FA\), with product \(A\). The central sign is topologically robust for every \(r>0\); exact Coxeter geometry is metrically tuned. The new stop-everything question is whether a gap equation, boundary criticality or RG condition derives \(\kappa=m^2\). See [document 1067](../1000-1099/1067_the_Wilson_rays_become_exactly_the_A2_placement_root_system_at_the_unique_shared_leg_strength_kappa_equals_m_squared.md).

The first selector audit gives both a route and a warning. Within this metric family, exact placement covariance has defect \(\|A^TKA-K\|_F^2=3(r-1)^2\), while the variance of the three marked ray norms is \(2(r-1)^2/3\); either principle uniquely selects \(r=1\). But a trace-normalized determinant is \((1+2r)/[4(1+r)^2]\) and decreases for \(r>0\), so the generic Jordan log-det/isotropy barrier selects the unmixed point \(r=0\). The \(A_2\) metric is selected only when the three relationships are marked and symmetry-related. This is a concrete separation of carrier stability from relational organization, and it prevents the programme from crediting the cubic/determinant sector with a selection it does not make. See [document 1068](../1000-1099/1068_placement_C3_covariance_selects_the_Wilson_A2_metric_but_the_generic_Jordan_logdet_barrier_selects_the_unmixed_point_instead.md).

Counterterm closure now specifies the required symmetry more precisely. The three coupling rays admit the exact intertwiner \(A\Lambda=\Lambda T\), with \(T^3=I\) and fixed relation \(l-u-d=0\). But the general complex Hermitian \(C_3\)-invariant quadratic form is \(aG_{A_2}/2+d\,i\varepsilon\); cyclic symmetry alone leaves an orientation-odd deformation. Complex conjugation sends \(d\mapsto-d\), so \(C_3+\)Real uniquely protects the positive \(G_{A_2}\) ray. The physical source triplet complicates this promising result: \(u,d\) use \(S_1^\dagger\), while \(l\) uses \(S_1\), and the signed cycle does not commute with that grading. The needed symmetry must therefore be graded/antiunitary or act only after a neutral response quotient; it cannot be an ordinary gauge-linear flavor rotation. See [document 1069](../1000-1099/1069_C3_plus_a_Real_structure_protects_the_Wilson_A2_metric_but_the_gauge_conjugate_source_triplet_requires_a_graded_lift.md).

The first graded-lift test closes a tempting shortcut. Encoding the physical source orientations as \(o=(0,0,1)\), the induced conjugation voltages are the coboundary \(a_i=o_i+o_{i+1}=(0,1,1)\), whose total flux is zero. This is general: vertex-grading differences always telescope to even flux around a closed cycle. The physical three-link section therefore gives two disconnected triangles, not the placement \(C_6\). Assigning one antiunitary to every edge gives the required odd voltage \((1,1,1)\), but its orbit necessarily visits both conjugation orientations of all three labels. The minimal source carrier for the placement cover is consequently a six-state orientation lift, not six families; a coherent root must retain the sheet above the neutral observable quotient. See [document 1070](../1000-1099/1070_the_physical_S1_orientation_pattern_is_a_zero_flux_coboundary_so_the_placement_six_cycle_requires_the_full_conjugation_doubled_source_triplet.md).

Prakash–Ramya's punctured anomaly code now separates the two \(S_1\) arms more strongly than representation theory alone. All six components of \((QL)(u^ce^c)\) are four-physical-field relations in \(W^\perp\), hence logical-neutral \(Z\)-stabilizers. All six components of \((QL)(d^c\nu^c)\) become weight-three logical-\(Z\) operators after the \(\nu^c\) puncture, split as three down-type Dirac and three scalar charged-current relations. Thus a puncture-fixing symmetry acting trivially on the logical quotient cannot exchange the two arms or protect the coefficient equality required by \(F_Cp\). The equality must be unpunctured, logical-changing or genuinely dynamical. The conjugation sheet and code logical grade are separate fibres and must both survive in the next coherent lift. See [document 1071](../1000-1099/1071_the_standard_model_code_separates_the_two_S1_FCp_channels_into_stabilizer_and_logical_sectors.md).

The unpunctured path is now classified completely. Exactly twelve elements of \(W(D_5)\) exchange the two six-component operator families: the coset \((S_3^{\rm colour}\times S_2^{\rm weak})r_{e_4+e_5}\). The canonical root reflection fixes \(Q,L\), swaps \(u_i^c\leftrightarrow d_i^c\) and \(e^c\leftrightarrow\nu^c\), and is the \(SU(2)_R\) Weyl reflection. Every exchanger swaps the \(\nu^c\) and \(e^c\) punctures, so there is no puncture-fixing exception. This gives coefficient equality a credible ultraviolet home while making its breaking mechanism exact. The relative sign still requires the lifted \(16_F16_F10_H\) Clebsch tensor, and the same full multiplet threatens proton safety. See [document 1072](../1000-1099/1072_exactly_twelve_D5_Weyl_elements_exchange_the_two_S1_channels_and_all_swap_the_nu_c_and_e_c_punctures.md).

The spin lift now fixes the missing orientation. An exact five-mode Clifford construction verifies the full Clifford relations, the symmetry of all ten \(C\Gamma_A\), and the vector-channel coefficients. For every colour, \(\mathcal B_{10}(d_i^c,\nu^c)/\mathcal B_{10}(u_i^c,e^c)=-1\), with the parallel left contraction \(d_i\nu/(u_ie)=-1\). Thus the unique \(16_F16_F10_H\) tensor contains \((ue-d\nu)(u^ce^c-d^c\nu^c)\) and supplies the exact equal-and-opposite \(F_Cp\) arm pattern in the declared response frame. The result does not evade the one-mediator Gram obstruction and does not preserve the isolated model's proton-safe \(B,L\) assignment. The remaining gate is a broken-phase Schur complement that retains the Clebsch residue while removing the third edge and diquark partners. See [document 1073](../1000-1099/1073_the_spin10_16_16_10_Clebsch_tensor_supplies_the_FCp_relative_minus_but_also_restores_the_third_edge_and_diquark_channels.md).

The first Schur-complement test is exact and restrictive. If every positive-metric mediator copy preserves the unified relation \(\lambda_d=-\lambda_u\), then \(C_{ud}=-\|\lambda_u\|_K^2\); no number of identical complete \(\mathbf{10}\) copies can remove the third edge. The minimal open-star repair needs a two-copy split involution, \(\lambda_d=-D\lambda_u\), with the up vector balanced between the two eigenspaces and the common leg in the positive face. This reproduces \((C_{Lu},C_{Ld},C_{ud})=(1,-1,0)\), but a shared-leg correction \(K=I+\alpha P_+\) immediately regenerates \(C_{ud}=-\alpha/2\). The missing sparsity is necessarily a symmetry-breaking, radiatively protected datum; merely adding unified copies cannot supply it. See [document 1074](../1000-1099/1074_positive_Spin10_Clebsch_preserving_copies_can_never_remove_the_third_edge_but_a_twisted_two_copy_involution_can.md).

The proton-safety cost is now completely counted at charge-support level. The unified vector triplet has three \(T\)-currents \(QQ,u^ce^c,d^c\nu^c\) and two \(\bar T\)-currents \(QL,u^cd^c\), giving six exchanges: two desired \(F_Cp\) arms, one additional \(B,L\)-conserving four-quark channel, and three \(\Delta B=\Delta L\) proton-decay channels. Every one has \(B-L=0\), so neither continuous \(B-L\) nor matter parity can select the safe pair. Meanwhile the current doubled-Furey endpoint model supplies only incidence and representation slots, not the represented one-form kinetic space needed to count an \(S_1\) multiplicity-two module. That dependency is pinned at \(\Omega_D^1(\mathcal A_F)\); the two-copy \(D\)-twist cannot be credited to the finite geometry before that calculation. See [document 1075](../1000-1099/1075_the_unified_vector_triplet_gives_two_FCp_arms_one_safe_extra_and_three_proton_decay_channels_all_with_BminusL_zero.md).

The safe puncture itself is now exact. On \(\Lambda^2\mathbb C^4\), projection from colour–colour to colour–lepton bivectors keeps \(QL\) and \(u^ce^c-d^c\nu^c\) while deleting \(QQ\) and \(u^cd^c\). Its stabilizer inside Pati–Salam colour–lepton \(SU(4)\) is exactly \(S(U(3)\times U(1))\): nine generators commute and the six colour–lepton roots do not. Thus the projector is Standard-Model and left–right covariant but necessarily breaks \(SU(4)\) and \(Spin(10)\). It cannot be achieved by a reality-compatible mass projection on one mediator six, because the desired arms require both conjugate triplet orientations; it must act on source incidence or use extra interference. Separate baryon number can protect the zeros at classical vertex level, whereas \(B-L\) is precisely the exceptional linear combination that cannot. An anomaly-consistent ultraviolet selector remains open. See [document 1076](../1000-1099/1076_the_proton_safe_current_projector_exists_only_after_breaking_the_pati_salam_colour_lepton_roots.md).

The finite-one-form pin is now a theorem rather than a caution. Three countermodels hold \(\mathcal A=\mathbb C_L^3\oplus\mathbb C_R^3\), \(\mathcal H=\mathbb C_L^3\oplus\mathbb C_R^3\), the diagonal representation and the same single coarse nonzero edge fixed, yet Dirac blocks of ranks one, two and three produce represented left–right one-form dimensions one, two and three. Tensoring with one fixed gauge intertwiner makes these repeated copies of the same gauge type. Even after multiplicity is known, incidence does not fix the positive kinetic/mass metric. Thus the current Furey arrows neither derive nor exclude the two-copy \(D\)-twisted repair. The branch reopens only after explicit \((\mathcal A_F,\mathcal H_F,\pi,D_F,\gamma,J)\), represented calculus, gauge-isotypic decomposition and kinetic form are supplied. See [document 1077](../1000-1099/1077_coarse_Furey_arrow_incidence_cannot_determine_the_represented_one_form_or_S1_copy_number.md).

The obvious charge-based protection route is now closed in its minimal continuous form. For family-universal \(SM+3\nu^c\) charges, ordinary Dirac Yukawas and no extra chiral anomaly-cancelling matter, the full anomaly-free \(U(1)_X\) space is \(\operatorname{span}\{Y,B-L\}\). Choosing the scalar charge to allow \(S,QL\), \(S^\dagger u^ce^c\) and \(S^\dagger d^c\nu^c\) then also makes \(S^\dagger QQ\) and \(S,u^cd^c\) exactly neutral for every point in that plane. Separate baryon number worked only as an anomalous classical selector. The surviving exits are family dependence, extra chiral matter, a genuinely discrete gauge symmetry or a finite differential calculus that omits the source maps. A published NCG Pati–Salam model provides a concrete precedent for the last mechanism, though not yet a derivation for this Furey system. See [document 1078](../1000-1099/1078_no_minimal_family_universal_anomaly_free_U1_can_protect_the_proton_safe_S1_source_mask.md).

That NCG precedent has now been crossed against the exact Wilson target. Model C contains separate complex \(H_L(6,1,1)\) and \(H_R(6,1,1)\) multiplets. Its \(H_{3L/R}\) components carry only the alternating leptoquark currents, while \(H_{\bar3L/R}\) companions carry only diquark currents. This is a concrete finite-geometry realization of source-level proton-safe separation and evades the single-real-six obstruction. Yet a nonzero \(H_{3L}\)–\(H_{3R}\) inverse-propagator entry gives the desired equal-and-opposite crossed arms while positivity forces \(C_{ud}=-K_{RR}<0\). The minimal pair closes the Wilson triangle. Proton safety independently requires a zero or suppressed leptoquark–diquark mass block, precisely the mixing assumption acknowledged in the paper. The next calculation is therefore the actual NCG scalar potential/one-form metric, not another charge census. See [document 1079](../1000-1099/1079_NCG_Pati_Salam_Model_C_realizes_the_safe_source_split_but_its_minimal_left_right_pair_still_closes_the_Wilson_triangle.md).

That mass-block question now has an exact invariant-theory form. On the complex Pati–Salam six, the Hermitian contraction \(H_L^\dagger H_R\) preserves the leptoquark/diquark split, but the equally gauge-invariant epsilon contraction \(H_L^TJH_R\) is purely off-block and pairs every colour–lepton bivector with a colour–colour bivector. All fifteen \(\mathfrak{sl}_4\) invariance conditions vanish exactly, so gauge symmetry cannot protect the safe block. A common phase grading does precisely the wanted job: it permits Hermitian left–right communication and forbids all holomorphic Pfaffians. The frontier has therefore narrowed to a decisive finite-geometry test—derive that phase/grading from the complete represented calculus and spectral action, or accept quantitative companion decoupling rather than exact proton safety. See [document 1080](../1000-1099/1080_the_complex_Pati_Salam_six_has_a_gauge_invariant_Pfaffian_that_reconnects_leptoquark_and_diquark_sources_unless_a_phase_grading_forbids_it.md).

The first vacuum-spurion audit qualifies that phase route without killing it. The six and ten are components of the same same-chirality spectral Higgs tensors, so the Pati–Salam-breaking ten-plet VEV carries the common phase and breaks it. A neutral \(6\,6\,\overline{10}\,\overline{10}\) quartic is therefore allowed by charge counting. However, the physical right-neutrino VEV is rank one in colour–lepton space. Exhausting all 24 delta-contraction patterns proves that every direct induced six-Pfaffian bilinear then vanishes by antisymmetry, while a generic higher-rank symmetric spurion gives nonzero values. The leading candidate protection is now the conjunction of Hermitian spectral traces and a rank-one breaking vacuum, not an unbroken scalar phase by itself. Mixed spurions and loops remain the stop-everything audit. See [document 1081](../1000-1099/1081_the_common_H_phase_is_broken_by_the_Pati_Salam_ten_vev_but_a_rank_one_neutrino_spurion_annihilates_the_direct_six_Pfaffian_quartic.md).

The first closed-path audit is positive. The published nonzero finite-Dirac blocks form the four-cycle \(L-R-R^c-L^c-L\). Of 32 length-four closed walks, exactly eight traverse all four sectors and contain both \(H_L\) and \(H_R\); every one contains one daggered and one undaggered \(H\). Hence the primitive spectral quartic generates Hermitian cross communication, never a holomorphic \(H_LH_R\) Pfaffian. Moreover, a colour-preserving diagonal \(\Sigma\) vacuum acts diagonally on the colour–colour and colour–lepton halves of \(\Lambda^2\mathbb C^4\). At quartic order the finite graph therefore permits the wanted left/right leptoquark relation while preserving the safe source split. Higher closed paths and broken-phase loops are now the exact remaining gate. See [document 1082](../1000-1099/1082_the_minimal_spectral_Pati_Salam_block_graph_forces_cross_six_mixing_to_be_Hermitian_at_quartic_order.md).

Higher spectral order finds the first escape and then kills it componentwise. There are no VEV-reduced holomorphic cross-six candidates at lengths two or four. At length six a unique cyclic class appears, \(H_L\bar\Sigma H_R^\dagger H_RH_R^\dagger\Sigma^\dagger\), corresponding to an \(a_6/\Lambda^2\) operator. Yet replacing the two daggered right tensors by the rank-one neutrino VEV produces the sandwich \(S^*A_RS^*=0\). Exhaustion of 512 length-eight walks leaves five candidate classes, all of which vanish for the same rank-one/diagonal vacuum and become nonzero for a generic symmetric spurion. The live frontier is now an all-orders normal-form proof that every candidate traps the antisymmetric right six between transported rank-one projectors, followed independently by a broken-phase quantum-counterterm audit. See [document 1083](../1000-1099/1083_the_first_higher_spectral_holomorphic_cross_six_path_occurs_at_length_six_but_rank_one_breaking_kills_every_candidate_through_length_eight.md).

That all-orders normal form is now proved. Cutting a candidate trace at its unique \(H_L\) edge turns the remaining graph into the tree \(L^c-R^c-R-L\). The right-Higgs edge is then a bridge, so its crossings must alternate \(H_R^\dagger,H_R,\ldots,H_R^\dagger\). Every retained antisymmetric right six is bracketed by two rank-one right-ten projectors; all intervening \(\Sigma\) excursions preserve the neutrino line on the physical diagonal vacuum. The local factor is therefore \(P_vM_LA_RM_RP_v=0\). This proves the holomorphic cross-six coefficient vanishes at every bare spectral word order under the minimal-graph, one-line, no-left-VEV contract, while leaving Hermitian communication intact. A length-sixteen exhaustion checks 15,264 retained-six placements. The branch now moves cleanly to quantum counterterms and verification of the exact vacuum contract. See [document 1084](../1000-1099/1084_the_minimal_spectral_graph_has_an_all_orders_rank_one_bridge_normal_form_that_forbids_holomorphic_cross_six_mixing.md).

The all-orders zero now has a symmetry face. The colour–lepton reflection \(P=\operatorname{diag}(1,1,1,-1)\) is even on the colour–colour half of the six, odd on its colour–lepton half, even on the neutrino \(e_4e_4\) breaking direction and invariant on diagonal \(\Sigma\) vacua. It reverses the epsilon form, so Pfaffian companion mixing is odd while Hermitian like-sector communication is even. On sources it is lepton parity: all three leptoquark currents are odd and both diquark currents even. This supplies the perturbative Ward selection rule missing after 1084. The discomfort moves to topology: with three generations the weak 't Hooft vertex contains three lepton doublets and is odd, so the parity is nonperturbatively anomalous unless ultraviolet data repair it. See [document 1085](../1000-1099/1085_the_rank_one_spectral_zero_is_the_unbroken_colour_lepton_reflection_Ward_sector_but_three_generation_weak_instantons_make_it_nonperturbatively_anomalous.md).

The anomaly cannot be removed by choosing a different universal sign convention. For each family, invariance of the quark–lepton and quark–quark source vertices gives \(q_T-q_D=q_Q+q_L\), while the weak instanton coefficient is \(3q_Q+q_L=q_Q+q_L\pmod2\). The physical triplet split and the mixed weak anomaly are the same class. Hence an odd number of universally treated families is necessarily anomalous, and multiplying by weak, \(SU(4)\), or fermion-parity centres changes neither side. Two protected families are mod-two anomaly free, but a family-dependent action must then confront the observed non-block-diagonal Yukawa matrices. The remaining exact exits require flavor structure, a larger discrete group, additional chiral/topological data or anomaly inflow. See [document 1086](../1000-1099/1086_for_an_odd_number_of_families_any_universal_Z2_that_splits_leptoquark_from_diquark_triplets_has_the_same_mod_two_weak_anomaly.md).

Generic flavor connectivity closes the minimal two-family exit. A diagonal sign symmetry of a Yukawa matrix is constant on every connected component of its bipartite support graph. Equivalently, for nondegenerate masses and connected CKM/PMNS mixing, the common diagonal-sign commutant is only \(\{+I_3,-I_3\}\) in each quark and lepton family space. The triplet-split vector is consequently either \((0,0,0)\) or \((1,1,1)\); anomaly freedom selects only the unprotected first case. A \((1,1,0)\) repair exists exactly on a disconnected \(2+1\) texture, so any realistic version must derive its mixing entries as controlled parity-breaking spurions and calculate the first induced companion-mixing operator. See [document 1087](../1000-1099/1087_connected_CKM_and_PMNS_Yukawa_graphs_force_any_exact_diagonal_family_Z2_back_to_the_universal_anomalous_class.md).












## 18 October 2026 — the higher-cyclic exit first opens at \(\mathbb Z_9\)

Poincaré duality produces the next unavoidable refinement. The orientable three-character KO-6 model has an antisymmetric \(3\times3\) intersection form and is therefore singular. Adding one \(J\)-paired spectator sector raises the form to four characters and gives Pfaffian \(-1\), determinant one. But with no additional Dirac edge the left Dirac commutant remains three-dimensional, so the repaired topology is metrically disconnected. The next search is now concrete: find existing Standard-Model/Pati–Salam characters that supply the anchor and spectator, then ask whether the existing Higgs/Yukawa/Majorana edges restore connectedness. See [the Poincaré-duality and connectedness audit](../1100-1199/1103_the_orientable_three_character_family_model_fails_Poincare_duality_while_a_fourth_spectator_repairs_the_intersection_form_but_not_Dirac_connectedness.md).

An explicit KO-6 matrix model exposes why the pure two-point family quartet is still insufficient. It can satisfy \(J^2=1\), \(JD=DJ\), \(J\gamma=-\gamma J\), oddness, order zero and first order, but \(J\)-closure duplicates a self-conjugate bimodule label with opposite gradings. Every Hochschild zero-cycle acts identically on those copies, so orientability fails. A third, distinct anchor character removes the repeated label and admits an explicit orienting cycle while preserving the \(\pm3\) arrow charges. This opens an existing-summand embedding branch and a less attractive new-summand branch. See [the KO-6 orientability and anchor theorem](../1100-1199/1102_the_minimal_two_point_KO6_family_quartet_fails_orientability_but_one_anchor_character_gives_an_explicit_real_even_first_order_completion.md).

The first-order condition does not close the two-point family branch, but it fixes its diagram. For bimodule labels \((i,j)\), an edge to \((k,\ell)\) passes first order iff \(i=k\) or \(j=\ell\). The charge-three edge can therefore run horizontally from \((0,r)\) to \((1,r)\), sharing its opposite-algebra label. Reality maps it to the vertical conjugate edge from \((r,0)\) to \((r,1)\), carrying the opposite charge. Diagonal shortcuts changing both labels are excluded. This establishes a minimal Krajewski-level real pair while leaving KO signs, grading, full multiplicities, physical unimodularity and the junk quotient open. See [the first-order real-pair theorem](../1100-1199/1101_the_first_order_condition_allows_the_charge_three_family_edge_only_as_a_horizontal_vertical_real_pair_but_does_not_complete_the_standard_model_finite_triple.md).

The smallest constructive family extension is now explicit. For a chosen charged-lepton projector \(P_\alpha\), the two-point algebra \(\mathbb C\oplus\mathbb C\) acts as \(zP_\alpha+w(I-P_\alpha)\). Its determinant-one condition on the \(1+2\) family representation forces the primitive generator \(I-3P_\alpha\), exactly the family \(A_2\) vertex. Any nonzero off-block finite-Dirac edge \(P_\alpha D_F(I-P_\alpha)\) then has commutator charge \(-3\), with its adjoint carrying \(+3\). This supplies a minimal algebraic origin for the flavon charge and insertion graph. It remains a family-factor theorem, not a completed NCG embedding: the full real structure, grading, first-order condition, physical unimodularity, junk quotient, scalar multiplicity and potential must still be computed. See [the minimal two-point family-algebra theorem](../1100-1199/1100_the_minimal_two_point_family_algebra_produces_the_A2_vertex_by_unimodularity_and_a_charge_three_one_form_but_its_full_NCG_embedding_remains_open.md).

The finite-algebra no-go must now be separated from the full finite-Dirac data. A generation-blind representation \(a\otimes I_F\) cannot label families, but the nondegenerate charged-lepton operator \(H_e=Y_eY_e^\dagger\) canonically supplies \(P_e,P_\mu,P_\tau\). The exact family augmentations are \(\Delta_\alpha=I-3P_\alpha(H_e)\), with the same \(A_2\) Gram matrix as before. This removes continuous flavor-frame ambiguity and physically labels all three candidate vertices. It still does not select which one is gauged. The required flavor edges occupy charge sectors \(0,\pm3,6\), while a family coefficient in \(D_F\) acquires no new charge-three gauge representation from the unchanged generation-blind algebra. The next gate is therefore an explicit extended finite triple and represented one-form calculation. See [the charged-lepton projector and flavon-boundary theorem](../1000-1099/1099_the_charged_lepton_Dirac_spectrum_canonically_labels_all_three_family_projectors_but_neither_selects_one_B_minus_3Li_vertex_nor_generates_its_charge_three_flavon.md).

The requested typed \(A_2\) comparison can now be made exactly at representation level. If \(P_0=I-J/3\) is the trace-zero projector on amplitudes over the three lattice placements, their centered vertices are \(p_i=P_0e_i\). The family augmentations obey \(\delta_i=\mathbf1-3e_i=-3p_i\), so their Gram matrix is exactly nine times the placement Gram matrix and the cycle/reflection actions intertwine. This removes any continuous \(O(2)\) fitting freedom. It does not make the physical identification canonical: the six bijections between \(\{L_0,L_1,L_2\}\) and \(\{e,\mu,\tau\}\) form a label torsor, and generation-blind NCG chooses none. See [the family-to-placement \(A_2\) intertwiner and label-torsor theorem](../1000-1099/1098_the_family_B_minus_3Li_orbit_is_exactly_the_trace_zero_placement_A2_module_but_the_intertwiner_is_a_six_choice_label_torsor.md).

The minimal continuous lift has now been identified with known model-building territory. If family \(i\) is exceptional, its generator is exactly \(X_i=B-2Y-3L_i\), so modulo the already gauged hypercharge direction it is \(B-3L_i\). The three family choices are not three unrelated solutions: their centroid is \(B-L-2Y\), their traceless deviations are \((-2,1,1)\), \((1,-2,1)\), and \((1,1,-2)\), and their Gram matrix has diagonal six, off-diagonal minus three and spectrum \((9,9,0)\). They therefore form an exact scaled \(A_2\) weight orbit under family permutations. This sharpens the frontier while reducing the novelty claim: the symmetry is established in the literature; the programme-specific result is its exact descent from the Majorana-compatible triality representative and the location of the family-selection problem. A generation-blind finite algebra acts as \(a\otimes I_F\), commutes with \(U(3)_F\), and cannot select one orbit vertex. See [the \(B-3L_i\), \(A_2\)-orbit and NCG-obstruction theorem](../1000-1099/1097_the_minimal_continuous_lift_is_hypercharge_dressed_B_minus_3Li_and_its_three_family_choices_form_an_A2_orbit_that_generation_blind_NCG_cannot_select.md).

The parallel continuous-completion branch is not empty. With one common Higgs and diagonal Dirac Yukawas, every continuous gauge, gravitational and cubic anomaly collapses to \(\sum_a(3q_{Q_a}+q_{L_a})=0\). The baryon-triality residues then make an integer lift possible only when the family count is divisible by three. For three families, exhaustive minimization gives a unique pattern up to family permutation: quark charges are universal, while one lepton family is shifted relative to a pair. Quark Yukawas remain fully connected; both lepton Dirac matrices have \(1\oplus2\) support. A charge-three flavon preserves baryon triality, fills every missing lepton cross-block at first order, and completes the exceptional Majorana entry at second order. This is an exact anomaly-free Froggatt–Nielsen candidate, not yet an NCG or phenomenological derivation. See [the minimal continuous-lift and flavon-texture theorem](../1000-1099/1096_three_families_admit_a_minimal_anomaly_free_continuous_baryon_triality_lift_but_it_forces_a_one_plus_two_lepton_texture_repaired_by_a_charge_three_flavon.md).

The perturbative anomaly descent has now reached a sharp boundary. In the parent, the two weak mixed residues are \(+1\) and \(-1\pmod3\); after breaking, baryon triality has \(SU(2)^2\mathbb Z_3\) residue one. Both demand \(N_f=0\pmod3\). The Dai–Freed invariant is stronger: its signed charge sum is \(3N_f\pmod9\). These conditions share a zero locus but are not the same class. A real degree-six anomaly polynomial cannot evaluate torsion characteristic data of a flat \(\mathbb Z_3\) bundle, and three identical continuous \(U(1)\) lifts still have exact linear and cubic sums nine rather than zero. The remaining bridge therefore requires an \(\eta\)-invariant/bordism computation or a fully specified generation-dependent continuous completion. See [the perturbative-shadow and torsion-boundary theorem](../1000-1099/1095_the_anomaly_polynomial_tracks_the_mod3_shadow_of_the_Pati_Salam_descent_but_the_baryon_triality_mod9_class_is_genuinely_torsional.md).

The Majorana-vacuum stabilizer is now known globally, not merely at Lie-algebra level. With integer hypercharge \(y=6Y\), its connected component is \((SU(3)_C\times SU(2)_L\times U(1)_y)/\mathbb Z_6\); the kernel is generated by \((\zeta_3I_3,-I_2,e^{i\pi/3})\). The order-nine lift commutes with this component and cubes to the colour centre. Multiplying it by the hypercharge transformation \(z=\omega^2\) gives an exact order-three complement, so the complete representation-level stabilizer is \(G_{\rm SM}\times\mathbb Z_3\). Its elementary charges differ from \(B-2Y\) only by colour centre and hence agree on observables. This closes the global stabilizer branch while leaving the induced spin-bordism map open. See [the exact Majorana-vacuum stabilizer theorem](../1000-1099/1094_the_Majorana_vacuum_stabilizer_is_the_Z6_quotiented_standard_model_times_a_split_baryon_triality_with_the_Z9_lift_cubing_to_colour_centre.md).

The unbroken parent group can now be identified exactly. If \(U=\operatorname{diag}(\omega,\omega,\omega,1)\), then \(\langle SU(4),U\rangle=\{h\in U(4):\det h\in\mu_3\}\cong SU(4)\times\mathbb Z_3\). The order-nine \(U\) is a gauge-dressed representative of the split component generator, not a nonsplit abstract extension. On one Pati–Salam generation \((\mathbf4,\mathbf2,\mathbf1)\oplus(\bar{\mathbf4},\mathbf1,\mathbf2)\), the bare component has zero pure, gravitational and \(SU(4)\)-mixed anomaly sums, but the independent weak mixed residues are \(+1\) and \(-1\pmod3\). Both vanish exactly for a family count divisible by three. Because the bare central representative does not preserve the Majorana direction whereas \(U\) does, the next gate is an anomaly-transgression calculation through the gauge-dressed Higgs stabilizer. See [the parent-group and mixed-anomaly theorem](../1000-1099/1093_the_order_nine_operator_is_a_gauge_dressed_component_of_SU4_times_Z3_and_the_three_family_obstruction_moves_into_mixed_weak_anomalies.md).

The quotient and anomaly statements must distinguish elementary-field charges from gauge-equivalence classes. Componentwise reduction gives \(3B\equiv6Y\pmod3\), hence a hypercharge gauge transformation on Standard-Model fields—not baryon triality itself. The nontrivial \(\mathbb Z_3\) is instead the quotient of the order-nine extension by its gauge cube; on gauge-invariant operators it acts by \(B\bmod3\), with \(B-2Y\) a fieldwise representative (up to inversion conventions). Its linear and cubic Dai–Freed residues are both \(3\pmod9\) per generation and therefore vanish for three generations. This independently matches the weak-instanton and particle-determinant conditions, but it does not yet compute the bordism class of the full Pati–Salam extension. See [the gauge-quotient and Dai–Freed audit](../1000-1099/1092_the_Z9_extension_not_its_naive_mod3_reduction_realizes_baryon_triality_and_matches_the_Dai_Freed_mod9_anomaly.md).

The failed universal \(\mathbb Z_2\) and connected-family repairs do not close exact discrete protection. Under three family-universal (SM+\nu^c) copies, neutral Higgs and Majorana-breaking VEVs, and ordinary Dirac Yukawas, write (x=3q_Q+q_L). The unwanted holomorphic companion mixing has charge \(-x\), whereas the three-family weak-instanton vertex has charge \(3x\). Protection and nonperturbative invariance therefore require (x\ne0\) but (3x=0\pmod N). Exhaustive anomaly-congruence enumeration finds no solution for (2\le N\le8); the first is \(\mathbb Z_9\), represented by (q_Q=1,q_L=0). Its companion bilinear has charge six, while the three-family instanton has charge nine and is neutral. On color-singlet observables this discrete quark-number lift acts as baryon triality. This is a low-energy classification, not yet a derivation from the full Pati–Salam finite geometry or its Dai–Freed data. See [the minimal \(\mathbb Z_9\) protector](../1000-1099/1088_Z9_is_the_minimal_family_universal_cyclic_lift_that_protects_triplet_companion_mixing_and_its_three_family_quotient_is_baryon_triality.md).

The first ultraviolet embedding obstruction is now removed exactly. With \(\omega=e^{2\pi i/9}\), the action \(\operatorname{diag}(\omega I_3,1)\) factors as the module phase \(e^{i\pi/6}\) times \(\exp[(i\pi/6)(B-L)]\in SU(4)\). Its cube lies in \(SU(4)\), so quotienting gauge transformations forces the faithful \(\mathbb Z_3\) baryon-triality action. The inverse exterior square gives the required diquark/leptoquark charges (7,8\pmod9), while the neutrino rank-one symmetric-square direction is neutral. The remaining gate is whether the complete real finite spectral triple admits this opposite particle/conjugate module phase. See [the Pati–Salam factorization theorem](../1000-1099/1089_the_Z9_quark_number_lift_factors_exactly_into_a_chiral_module_phase_times_Pati_Salam_BminusL_and_its_gauge_quotient_is_baryon_triality.md).

The supplied Prakash–Ramya paper now meets this branch at a precise fork. Its integral Cartan row \(q_1+q_2+q_3=3(B-L)\) reduces modulo three to the same word as \(3B\), namely \(6Y\bmod3\). This common row is a hypercharge-gauge stabilizer—not baryon triality itself—and because it vanishes on the \(\nu^c\) puncture it is a code stabilizer rather than the logical coordinate. Modulo nine the lifts separate by \(3L\): \(3(B-L)\) is weak-anomaly free per family but gives the Majorana bilinear charge six, whereas \(3B\) makes the Majorana vacuum neutral but carries weak charge three per family, canceled only by three generations. The paper's logical gate and the field symmetry share \((1,\zeta_9,\zeta_9^{-1})\) but act on different spaces. See [the inequivalent ninth-root lift theorem](../1000-1099/1090_the_Prakash_Ramya_qutrit_gate_and_Z9_baryon_triality_share_a_ternary_shadow_but_are_inequivalent_ninth_root_lifts.md).

The lift ambiguity is now closed inside the declared minimal finite-sector model. Enumerating (X_k=3(B-L)+3kL\pmod9) shows that only (k=1), namely (3B), both preserves the rank-one \(\nu^c\nu^c\) vacuum and charges the companion bilinear. Its operator (U\oplus U\oplus U^*\oplus U^*\) on (L,R,L^c,R^c) commutes with the antiunitary real structure and sector grading, preserves diagonal \(\Sigma\) transport and the neutrino line, and normalizes all edge representation spaces. The full doubled determinant cancels; the particle-half determinant is one exactly for a family count divisible by three. This is not yet a claim that the chosen NCG unimodularity convention applies to the global normalizer. See [the four-sector admissibility and unique-lift theorem](../1000-1099/1091_the_rank_one_Majorana_vacuum_uniquely_selects_the_3B_mod9_lift_and_its_four_sector_operator_closes_only_for_three_family_unimodularity.md).

The source-level Pati–Salam audit now prevents a false completion of the family toy model. In the published geometry, \(\mathbb H_R\oplus\mathbb H_L\oplus M_4(\mathbb C)\) acts as \(\pi_{\rm PS}(a)\otimes I_3\), so the complete family unitary group lies in its commutant; its connected centre has only the single phase of \(M_4(\mathbb C)\). It therefore supplies neither the family \(\mathbb C\oplus\mathbb C\) selector nor the anchor/spectator by itself. The calculation also exposes a productive fork: strict first order restricts toward the Standard-Model algebra, whereas unrestricted spectral Pati–Salam uses a quadratic fluctuation \(A_{(2)}\). If a family extension puts a charge \(-3\) singlet in \(A_{(1)}\), its quadratic sector has charge \(-6\), exactly neutralizing the exceptional Majorana bilinear of charge \(+6\). This is exact charge arithmetic but not yet a nonzero tensor component; the next stop-everything test is the explicit family-extended \(A_{(2)}\) calculation. See [document 1104](../1100-1199/1104_the_spectral_Pati_Salam_algebra_does_not_supply_the_family_anchor_by_itself_but_its_non_first_order_quadratic_fluctuation_is_the_natural_charge_six_completion.md).

That stop-everything test now rejects the naïve identification. The source formula is \(A_{(2)}=\sum_r\widehat a_r[A_{(1)},\widehat b_r]\), not \(A_{(1)}^2\). On an edge \((i,j)\to(k,\ell)\), a normalized perturbation kernel gives \(A_{(2)}=D_{ts}(K_{ki}-1)(K_{\ell j}-1)\). Hence \(A_{(2)}\) preserves existing Dirac/linear-fluctuation support and vanishes on every horizontal or vertical edge satisfying first order. Exhausting the two-point bimodule gives eight first-order edges with zero \(A_{(2)}\) and four diagonal defects with nonzero \(A_{(2)}\). The strict charge-three family arrow therefore cannot square itself through the generalized fluctuation. The non-first-order branch survives only if the physical Majorana support itself changes both left and opposite labels; otherwise the charge-six composite must arise through ordinary scalar insertions, mediators or an enlarged path. See [document 1105](../1100-1199/1105_the_generalized_quadratic_inner_fluctuation_is_a_first_order_defect_dressing_not_the_square_of_the_charge_three_family_edge.md).

The published Majorana support passes that diagonal-defect test. An unprimed Pati–Salam fermion has bimodule type \((X,Y)\), while its primed conjugate has type \((Y,X)\); the \(\nu_R\)-Majorana block therefore changes both labels. The source computes its nonlinear component explicitly as \(A_{(2)}=k^{*\nu_R}\Delta_{\dot aJ}\Delta_{\dot bI}\). Representation theory gives \(\operatorname{Sym}^2(2,4)=(3,10)\oplus(1,6)\), exactly the listed Majorana Higgs sectors. But this is gauge structure: \(\Delta\sim(2_R,1_L,4)\) has no generation index, while \(k^{\nu_R}\) is a fixed generation-space coefficient. The direct nonlinear mechanism is real without yet being the family \(B-3L_i\) mechanism. The next mixed branch must put a family character on the fundamental \(\Delta\) factor, not merely on its Yukawa coefficient. See [document 1106](../1100-1199/1106_the_Pati_Salam_Majorana_block_is_a_genuine_diagonal_A2_defect_but_its_Delta_square_is_gauge_not_family_structure.md).

At charge-support level that mixed branch has a unique minimal architecture. Since \(q(\nu^c)=(3,0,0)\), the symmetric Majorana matrix needs scalar sectors \(-6,-3,0\) with multiplicities \(1,2,3\). A single fundamental \(\Delta\) charge has only one self-sum. Exhausting integral two-charge alphabets shows that \(\{-3,0\}\) is the unique minimum: \(\Delta_{-3}^2\), \(\Delta_{-3}\Delta_0\) and \(\Delta_0^2\) generate exactly the three required sectors and cover all six symmetric entries. This realizes “one carrier, two faces, three relationships” precisely. It is not yet an NCG result: the published calculus contains one generation-blind \((2_R,4)\) field, and coarse arrows do not determine whether a family extension supplies two gauge-isotypic one-form copies. The branch is pinned at that multiplicity computation and at the noncommuting relation between \(B-3L_i\) and unbroken \(SU(4)\). See [document 1107](../1100-1199/1107_two_Pati_Salam_Delta_faces_with_charges_zero_and_minus_three_are_the_unique_minimal_quadratic_alphabet_for_the_full_family_Majorana_texture.md).

The first multiplicity test rejects the naïve associative tensor product. Family idempotents \(P,Q\) isolate \(PKP,PKQ,QKP,QKQ\); for full Majorana support the two diagonal neutral blocks remain independent while reality pairs only the charged cross orientations. Thus \(\mathcal A_{\rm PS}\otimes(\mathbb C\oplus\mathbb C)\) generically gives two neutral \(\Delta\) copies plus a charged complex copy, not the desired common neutral plus charged pair. Algebra closure prevents simply retaining the separate generators while forbidding their products. The viable exits are a proved junk relation, dynamical decoupling or a normalizer/linking construction. See [document 1108](../1100-1199/1108_the_naive_Pati_Salam_tensor_family_algebra_generically_gives_three_Delta_directions_not_the_two_face_carrier.md).

A cross-branch Jordan result supplies the exact algebraic selector that the associative extension lacks. On the coarse two-copy Hermitian space, exchange-conjugation fixes \(J_z=\{\bigl(\begin{smallmatrix}t&z\\\bar z&t\end{smallmatrix}\bigr)\}\): one common neutral population plus both quadratures of the charged relationship. This space is Jordan closed, while its associative commutator regenerates the excluded \(\sigma_z\) population difference. It is Jordan-isomorphic to \(H_2(\mathbb R)\), so its conformal closure is \(Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)\). The bridge is exact but typed: \(\sigma_xK\) cannot exchange the raw rank-one and rank-two family Hilbert sectors; it must act on equal-dimensional scalar-copy or response faces with a compatible kinetic Gram metric. Also \(E_{\pm}\circ E_{\pm}=0\), so charge six lives in the external Pati–Salam symmetric tensor \(\Delta\odot\Delta\), not an internal Jordan square. See [document 1109](../1100-1199/1109_the_population_normal_Jordan_selector_removes_the_extra_neutral_Delta_and_recovers_Spin23_but_charge_six_lives_in_an_external_symmetric_square.md).

The first spectral-metric test exposes the cost of making that selector dynamical. The neutral family blocks have a diagonal kinetic Gram matrix with weights \(Z_P=\operatorname{Tr}(C_P^\dagger C_P)\) and \(Z_Q=\operatorname{Tr}(C_Q^\dagger C_Q)\), so canonical normalization always creates a quadratic exchange isometry. But the normalized quartic purity \(\rho(C)=\operatorname{Tr}[(C^\dagger C)^2]/\operatorname{Tr}(C^\dagger C)^2\) is one for the rank-one \(P\) block and strictly below one for a full-rank \(2\times2\) \(Q\) block. Exact exchange through the minimal single-trace quartic therefore forces \(\operatorname{rank}(QKQ)\le1\). For squared singular-value ratio \(r\), the exchange defect is \(2r/(1+r)^2\), giving a controlled approximate branch when the complement block is hierarchical. Additional spectral paths could compensate this invariant, but simple field rescaling cannot. See [document 1110](../1100-1199/1110_the_minimal_spectral_kinetic_metric_can_be_normalized_to_the_Jordan_face_but_quartic_exchange_forces_the_Q_Majorana_block_to_rank_one.md).

## 18 October 2026: the Jordan quartic gate reaches a low-energy neutrino texture

The population-normal \(H_2(\mathbb R)\) copy-space proposal now has a precise conditional phenomenological consequence. ## 18 October 2026: the unchanged Pati–Salam triple cannot protect the null line

The finite-geometry audit closes the simplest microscopic protection route. ## 18 October 2026: the minimal family refinement is ‎‎‎‎(mathbb C^3), with unavoidable costs

Separating the selected line, the image of the rank-one complementary Majorana block and its null line requires the full diagonal algebra (mathbb C^3); every two-dimensional diagonal subalgebra identifies a pair. ## 18 October 2026: bilinear scalar generation preserves the kernel and splits the ultraviolet completion

Kernel-compatible Weyl fields obey \(X+Z\equiv1+w_L\pmod2\); every scalar sourced by their bilinear therefore obeys \(X_\Phi+Z_\Phi\equiv w_\Phi\pmod2\). The three elementary family edges and the Higgs pass explicitly, and polynomial composites preserve the congruence. By contrast, the primitive weak-singlet repair \((X,Z)=(0,1)\) fails it.
