## 18 October 2026: the unreduced Pati–Salam algebra has no character capable of serving as \(S\)

The first physical embedding test closes the literal existing-character route in the unreduced theory. Neither quaternionic factor nor (M_4(\mathbb C)) admits a unital one-dimensional complex representation, so (mathbb H_R\oplus\mathbb H_L\oplus M_4(\mathbb C)) contains no candidate (S). The null-mask programme therefore splits. The strict-first-order branch must work after reduction to (mathbb C\oplus\mathbb H\oplus M_3(\mathbb C)), using its unique complex character together with an explicit family extension. The unreduced Pati–Salam branch relaxes first order and must re-evaluate the dangerous support in the full generalized (A_{(1)}+A_{(2)}) calculus; a Krajewski-diagonal label no longer proves absence. See [1122](../1100-1199/1122_the_unreduced_Pati_Salam_algebra_has_no_character_for_S_so_the_null_mask_splits_into_reduced_first_order_and_generalized_branches.md).

The reduced branch now has its own construction-level fork. The direct sum \(\mathcal A_{\rm SM}\oplus\mathbb C^3\) has exactly four complex characters and is the only natural combination considered here whose label set can literally be \(\{S,P,I,N\}\). Real-selector replication \(\mathcal A_{\rm SM}^3\) has only three characters, while the literal real tensor product \(\mathcal A_{\rm SM}\otimes_{\mathbb R}\mathbb C^3\) has six because \(\mathbb C\otimes_{\mathbb R}\mathbb C\cong\mathbb C\oplus\mathbb C\). Thus the viable exact-four carrier is a genuine direct-sum extension with crossed bimodules still to construct, not an automatic family product. Its naive connected central unitary torus has rank five before the \(J\)-gauge kernel and unimodularity are computed. See [1123](../1100-1199/1123_only_the_direct_sum_reduced_SM_plus_C3_has_the_four_characters_but_it_makes_S_a_genuine_algebra_extension.md).

The crossed-bimodule test then closes the simplest physical reading of that exact-four carrier. A simple bimodule over a direct sum has one left and one opposite-factor support. Physical weak/color sectors already occupy those two slots, so a third direct-sum family character cannot decorate the same state without replacing one gauge support. The added \(P,I,N\) summands create new Krajewski rows and columns, not three labelled copies of every old physical cell. Self-pairs can mimic isolated singlet pieces, but they do not furnish a spectrum-wide selector. The reduced branch must therefore move to a commuting/tensor action, a normalizer/Jordan selector or genuinely new matter; otherwise the unreduced generalized-fluctuation branch becomes primary. See [1124](../1100-1199/1124_the_exact_four_direct_sum_has_a_two_sided_slot_obstruction_to_decorating_physical_SM_fermions.md).

The “wheels within wheels” branch gives the first constructive escape. Keep the physical internal bimodule \(H_{ij}\) and let \(\mathbb C^3\) act on its multiplicity commutant: \(H_{ij}\otimes F\). The family action commutes with both internal sides, so it consumes no gauge-support slot, while \([D_F,\rho_F(f)]\) can remain nonzero and generate family-sensitive relations. If promoted to the coordinate algebra this returns to the tensor-product gauge problem; if retained as commutant data it requires a generalized spectral/Jordan axiom system. Operator relations then carry the natural Jordan triple \(\{x,y,z\}=(xy^*z+zy^*x)/2\), giving a precise home for relational-carrier composition. The next test is whether the null-mask relation space contains the established \(H_2(\mathbb R)\) Jordan slice with conformal \(Spin_0(2,3)\). See [1125](../1100-1199/1125_ophanim_wheels_within_wheels_as_a_nested_commutant_and_Jordan_triple_research_branch.md).

That closure test gives a sharp local/global split. The safe Dirac relation \(X_{PI}\) and Majorana null relation \(X_{PN}\) each generate a principal \(H_2(\mathbb R)\) corner and hence a local \(Spin_0(2,3)\) conformal wheel. But their mixed Jordan product forces \(X_{IN}\), their squares generate all populations, and the unital closure is \(H_3(\mathbb R)\) with conformal algebra \(Sp(6,\mathbb R)\). Thus “one carrier, two faces, three relationships” is exact: the third relationship is generated, not assumed. A global \(Spin(2,3)\) now requires sector-typed triple composition, a conditional expectation, junk or dynamics that prevents the entire \(Sp(6)\) envelope from becoming physical. See [1126](../1100-1199/1126_two_overlapping_H2R_null_mask_wheels_generate_H3R_and_raise_Spin23_to_Sp6.md).

The sector typing is already present in the Krajewski anchors. The Dirac and Majorana copies of the visible label \(P\) are actually \((P,N)\) and \((P,I)\), hence orthogonal relationship states. On the full four-state carrier, their relation operators occupy disjoint blocks and all mixed products vanish. The typed closure is \(H_2(\mathbb R)_D\oplus H_2(\mathbb R)_M\), with two local \(Spin_0(2,3)\) conformal algebras. The \(H_3/Sp(6)\) envelope appears precisely when the opposite anchor is forgotten. The physical gate is now exact: represented one-forms, junk and symmetry breaking must preserve the anchor projectors. See [1127](../1100-1199/1127_opposite_anchor_typing_splits_the_Sp6_envelope_into_two_local_Spin23_relational_wheels.md).

The original Möbius-gear inspiration supplies a compatible parity law. An ordinary loop of \(n\) external gear contacts has holonomy \((-1)^n\) and therefore requires even \(n\). One Möbius orientation reversal contributes another minus sign, so twisted closure requires odd \(n\); three is the minimal nontrivial case. This maps cleanly but conditionally onto \(P,I,N\): two primary relations \(P-I\) and \(P-N\), one induced closing relation \(I-N\), and a hidden type/orientation transport controlling whether the cycle frustrates or closes. The two interlocking wheels remain the Ophanim image; three belongs to the minimal closed relational dynamics. See [1128](../1100-1199/1128_three_is_the_minimal_Mobius_gear_cycle_and_the_third_relation_of_the_typed_twin_wheels.md).

The discrete-cover audit then separates capability from realization. The existing six KO-6 sectors form two oriented \(C_3\) sheets, not the connected \(C_6\) Möbius cover; a sheet-switching seam is still required. More strongly, KO-6 reality supplies the sheet involution but does not determine the \(\mathbb Z_2\) connection: all eight edge-lift assignments commute with \(J\), with four trivial and four Möbius holonomies. The Möbius sub-branch is therefore indeterminate from the current axioms. It becomes decidable only after specifying the physical particle/conjugate representation and computing the sheet-switching parity in the junk-quotiented one-form module. See [1129](../1100-1199/1129_the_current_KO6_triangle_is_two_oriented_C3_sheets_not_yet_a_Mobius_C6_cover.md) and [1130](../1100-1199/1130_KO6_reality_supplies_the_sheet_involution_but_does_not_determine_Mobius_holonomy.md).

Keeping both classes live produces a much sharper filter. Vertex-sheet gauge equivalence reduces the eight assignments to two classes, and strict cyclic covariance gives them the canonical representatives \(000\) and \(111\). Homogeneous even transport selects the trivial \(C_3\sqcup C_3\) cover; homogeneous odd transport selects the connected \(C_6\) cover. The unresolved datum is therefore not an arbitrary seam location but the degree of physical relationship transport. See [1131](../1100-1199/1131_C3_covariance_and_homogeneous_grading_reduce_eight_holonomies_to_two_canonical_live_branches.md).

The graded Jordan calculation then links this fork to the central algebraic choice. For grading-odd relations, the binary Jordan product is even while the Jordan triple product remains odd. If degree is the sheet cocycle, binary response composition selects the trivial cover and typed triple recursion selects the Möbius cover. Both may coexist at different levels: an odd Möbius relational carrier can underlie an even untwisted observable algebra. Arbitrary linear seams and unmotivated edge-asymmetric transports are filtered out; typed odd one-forms, \(J\)-assisted Real transport and even response transport remain live pending the finite one-form/junk calculation. See [1132](../1100-1199/1132_binary_Jordan_composition_selects_the_trivial_cover_while_odd_Jordan_triple_recursion_selects_the_Mobius_cover.md).

The first typed TRO calculation makes that layered claim precise and also limits it. The two-spoke space \(R=\operatorname{span}\{E_{PI},E_{PN}\}\) is closed under Jordan triples, so the null mask is preserved. Its third relationship is \(E_{PI}^*E_{PN}=E_{IN}\), but this lies in the even linking algebra \(R^*R\), not in the odd carrier \(R\). Binary self-adjoint closure promotes it and yields the coarse \(H_3/Sp(6)\) envelope. Thus two odd spokes cannot generate the third odd Möbius edge by triple closure alone. The connected branch now requires an independent typed odd edge, a \(J\)/groupoid lift, or a spectator-mediated path. See [1133](../1100-1199/1133_the_typed_TRO_preserves_the_null_mask_but_cannot_generate_the_third_odd_Mobius_edge.md).

The first surviving lift is now explicit. Let \(\rho\) advance the cyclic label \(P\to I\to N\to P\) and define the semilinear transport \(T=J\rho\). Then \(T^3=J\) and \(T^6=1\), producing the connected six-state orbit without adding a complex-linear map between \(P_D\) and \(P_M\). This is a genuine Real/groupoid Möbius construction, not an ordinary one-form: its physical gate is derivation of \(\rho\) as a typed commutant symmetry or correspondence that preserves the anchor mask. See [1134](../1100-1199/1134_a_minimal_Real_groupoid_realizes_the_Mobius_C6_without_a_linear_anchor_mixing_seam.md).

The oriented-sector audit immediately filters that construction. For the actual compositional cycles, reality reverses the rotation: \(JrJ=r^{-1}\). Hence \(r\) and \(J\) generate \(D_3\), and every \(Jr^k\) has order two; none produces \(C_6\). The order-six construction requires a different sheet-parallel rotation \(c\) which runs opposite to natural composition on the conjugate sheet. The Real branch therefore survives only if \(c\) is derived as covariant frame transport, an opposite-category normalizer or an extended correspondence—not as the existing composition map. See [1135](../1100-1199/1135_the_natural_oriented_relation_rotation_is_dihedral_and_blocks_the_naive_Jrho_Mobius_C6.md).

The complete parallel-deck census then finds exactly three commuting involutions \(\delta_k\), each making \(\delta_kr\) an order-six cycle. Yet every \(\delta_k\) contains precisely one reversed relation pair \((i,j)\leftrightarrow(j,i)\), which changes both Krajewski labels and is forbidden as a strict first-order complex-linear edge. Thus no all-linear first-order Möbius deck exists on the six sectors. The branch survives only structurally, through generalized non-first-order support, or through a mediator resolving the diagonal pair into allowed steps. See [1136](../1100-1199/1136_every_parallel_deck_involution_gives_C6_but_contains_one_first_order_forbidden_relation_reversal.md).

The existing fourth-character spectator does not supply that mediator. Its retained edges attach \(SP\) to one orientation component and \(PS\) to the other, leaving two four-vertex components. Exhausting all grading-odd first-order bridges finds only \(PN\leftrightarrow PI\) and its conjugate \(IP\leftrightarrow NP\), precisely the dangerous maps between the Dirac and Majorana \(P\)-lifts. Hence the minimal spectator repairs duality and left-metric connectedness but cannot realize anchor-safe Möbius holonomy. See [1137](../1100-1199/1137_the_minimal_spectator_completion_cannot_realize_Mobius_holonomy_without_erasing_the_anchor_mask.md).

## 19 October 2026: the natural Spin(2,3) return exists, but its transport does not yet adapt

The generative-dynamics audit locates a genuine return map already present in the corpus. A symplectic loop \(g=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}A&B\\C&D\end{smallmatrix}\right)\) acts on the Lagrangian/Jordan chart by \(T_g(X)=(C+DX)(A+BX)^{-1}\), the discrete form of the established matrix Riccati flow. This is the correct Spin(2,3)-specific alternative to importing a logistic map. But fixed-loop iteration satisfies \(T_g^n=T_{g^n}\): the returned state does not change the next transport. On the scalar symplectic slice the derivative is strictly positive, so a multiplier cannot cross \(-1\); the minimal normalized TRO self-triple is projectively the identity; Jordan closure is idempotent; Schur recursion terminates; and the \(C_3/C_6\) constructions remain finite covering holonomies. A genuine bifurcation now requires a derived covariant state-to-Hessian law \(X\mapsto\mathcal S_\lambda(X)\), giving \(g_\lambda[X]=\exp(J\mathcal S_\lambda(X))\). No present stress, Furey, TRO or feedback construction supplies that law. See [1138](../1100-1199/1138_the_natural_spin23_riccati_return_is_fixed_transport_not_yet_a_feedback_bifurcation.md).

The state-to-Hessian question now has an exact structural answer. Since the Lagrangian carrier is the homogeneous space \(\mathrm{Lag}(2,\mathbb R)=Sp(4,\mathbb R)/P\), a fully equivariant generator map \(\mathcal K:\mathrm{Lag}(2,\mathbb R)\to\mathfrak{sp}_4\) would be determined by a vector fixed by the parabolic stabilizer \(P\). Direct block algebra and a rank-ten executable certificate show that this fixed subspace is zero. Therefore no nonzero fully \(Sp(4)\)-covariant feedback generator can depend on one Lagrangian/Jordan state alone. The obvious positive penalty \(\lVert p-Xq\rVert^2\) does not evade the result: its Hamiltonian generator annihilates the graph \(p=Xq\) and leaves the state fixed. The minimum viable adaptive return must contain an independently transported second face, memory/defect variable, external stress/response field or an explicit symmetry reduction. This turns “relational carrier” from a metaphor into a type requirement for generative dynamics. See [1139](../1100-1199/1139_full_spin23_covariance_forbids_a_nonzero_state_only_feedback_generator.md).

The next arity classification makes “three” mathematically substantive. An ordered transverse pair of Lagrangian faces has a unique fully equivariant generator up to scale, the grading $H_{M,N}=\Pi_M-\Pi_N$, but it fixes both defining faces and has an indefinite Hessian. A third Lagrangian $L_X=\operatorname{graph}(X)$, positive relative to the pair, canonically supplies $\mathcal S_X=\operatorname{diag}(X,X^{-1})>0$ and the elliptic generator $K_X=J\mathcal S_X$ with $K_X^2=-I$. Its exact Riccati action is $X\mapsto[(\cos\theta-\sin\theta)/(\cos\theta+\sin\theta)]X$. Thus one face gives no generator, two give a relation that fixes its endpoints, and three positive faces first give healthy covariant transport of a carrier. The minimal update remains radial and integrable, so nonlinear branching still requires evolving faces, noncommuting cycles or a transported step law. See [1140](../1100-1199/1140_three_positive_lagrangian_faces_are_minimal_for_healthy_covariant_relational_transport.md).

The first physical-typing test rejects a literal identification of the $P,I,N$ triangle with those three Lagrangians. Forgetting opposite anchors produces the six-dimensional $H_3/Sp(6)$ envelope; preserving them gives two orthogonal $H_2/Sp(4)$ blocks with no cross-block symplectic pairing. Locally, the natural two population matrices are singular and the coherence matrix is indefinite. The constructive replacement comes from the stress/defect relationship rays. For $X=\sum_iw_ib_ib_i^T$, Cauchy--Binet gives $\det X=\sum_{i<j}w_iw_j\det(b_i,b_j)^2$, so positive weights on two nonparallel rays produce exactly the interior graph needed by the three-face theorem. For the established thermal tensor/vector/scalar rays, $\rho=\left(\begin{smallmatrix}a+c&c\\c&b+c\end{smallmatrix}\right)$ and $\det\rho=ab+ac+bc$. Whenever at least two channels are active, this gives the explicit positive completion $\mathcal S[\rho]=\operatorname{diag}(\rho,\rho^{-1})$. The remaining physical gates are derivation of the inverse-response kinetic block and a causal update law for the channel weights. See [1141](../1100-1199/1141_PIN_is_not_a_positive_lagrangian_triple_but_stress_rays_build_the_missing_third_face.md).

The inverse-response gate is now resolved at the static mathematical level. If $W(q)=q^T\rho q/2$ is a positive quadratic response functional, its Legendre dual is $W^*(p)=p^T\rho^{-1}p/2$, with Fenchel gap $[p-\rho q]^T\rho^{-1}[p-\rho q]/2$. Thus the inverse block is derived rather than inserted. Promoting $q,p$ to an independent canonical pair and taking $H=W+W^*$ gives the positive self-dual Hessian and $K_\rho^2=-I$. That promotion remains an extra physical assertion: positivity alone permits any positive kinetic block, an overall clock scale remains free, and a frequency-dependent or lossy stress response requires a conservative state-space dilation before it can serve as a local Hamiltonian. The existing selector dilations prove feasibility for their rational response family but are not yet a realization of the Standard-Model stress kernel. See [1142](../1100-1199/1142_legendre_duality_derives_the_inverse_response_block_but_not_the_physical_clock_or_feedback.md).

Specializing the earlier general Feshbach realization theorem to the three thermal relationship rays gives an exact minimal model. The weight-three determinant-line factor contributes the same \(-1\) at the standard Fourier fixed point and cancels the finite Weil signature phase in the automorphic section.

Literal comparison with the physical full-Fourier phase \(\pm i\) is nevertheless invalid without another map. The two-channel physical Schrödinger representation acts on two configuration variables, while the genus-two \(A_2\) theta realization acts on \((A_2\otimes\mathbb R)^2\cong\mathbb R^4\). Their Maslov indices therefore differ dimensionally. The common nine-state/two-qutrit shadow does not itself identify the continuous representation spaces.

The leading home-stretch gate is now an explicit theta or reduction kernel \(\mathcal K\) satisfying \(\mathcal K U_{\rm phys}(\widetilde g)=\rho_{A_2,2}(\widetilde g)\mathcal K\) for channel exchange, controlled Fourier and the mutual gate. A successful kernel would unify the continuous carrier, finite qutrit module, genus-two topology and automorphic sum. Failure of every positivity- and gate-preserving reduction would stop the identification. ## 19 October 2026 — exhaustion verdict

The rank-split programme has reached its declared terminal condition. The generator \(I-3P\), charge-three Peirce relation, anomaly-free family lift, Yukawa/Majorana insertion graph, fieldwise \(\mathbb Z_9\), colour-centre quotient to physical \(\mathbb Z_3\), nine-state finite symplectic reduction and untwisted orbifold carrier are exact under their stated contracts. Ordinary associative finite-algebra embeddings are excluded; the bare Furey TRO cannot select the genus-one orientation; outer/Jordan gauging and a massive gapped defect are viable generalized constructions; family selection, localized spectrum, coherent wall orientation and stress/BF operator coupling are indeterminate external data.

The resulting narrative is strong but conditional: a selected family line can support an externally gauged or centrally lifted \(B-3L_i\) sector whose global quotient and untwisted defect realize the finite \(Sp(4,\mathbb F_3)\) shadow of the continuous \(Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)\) relational carrier. The Standard Model and ordinary NCG do not presently predict the selection or defect. See the [complete exhaustion audit](../1100-1199/1184_exhaustion_audit_of_the_rank_split_spin23_standard_model_programme.md).

## 19 October 2026 — reflexive closure sharpens the terminal boundary

Two-Yukawa trace invariants can support non-alignment, while CP-sensitive orientation first appears at higher order. The four target moments form a double Jordan frame, but ordered-frame equivariance returns every frame identically rather than choosing one.

The terminal obstruction is now exact in a useful class: integrating out one, or any finite number of, fully isotropic cross-Peirce links sees only the selected-frame moments and remains blind to the complementary (\mathbb{CP}^1). Multiplicity does not repair the missing information. Absolute flavour selection therefore requires independently derived anisotropy—such as a nondegenerate complementary-plane operator—not another isotropic copy. ## 19 October 2026 — Planckian transport becomes an external response-geometry test

The Planckian (t)-(J) calculation supplies an experimentally relevant but deliberately narrower bridge. Its reported conductivity is a scalar detailed-balance ray. A full particle/energy spectral matrix would instead lie in the positive cone (H_2(\mathbb R)_+); its determinant is the (2+1)-dimensional Lorentz quadratic form, and its conformal completion is (Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)). This is exact kinematics of positive two-current response, not a microscopic symmetry claim.

The public code currently lacks the physical lattice energy current. A diagnostic matrix-FTLM extension exists, but its energy-weighted proxy cannot decide the physics. The next discriminating calculation is to derive (J_E) from a local continuity equation, compute the three independent spectra, and test positivity and normalized determinant across temperature, doping, size and broadening. See [document 1216](../1200-1299/1216_planckian_tj_transport_is_a_scalar_detailed_balance_ray_and_a_testable_two_current_spin23_response_extension.md).

## 19 October 2026 — Leech pair geometry reaches the same cone but not the missing selector

Ordered pairs of Leech vectors supply a canonical arithmetic entrance to the same positive cone: their \(2\times2\) Gram matrices lie in \(H_2(\mathbb R)_+\), minimal pairs form determinant strata \(0,12,15,16\), and the degree-two theta series is naturally \(Sp(4,\mathbb Z)\)-modular. This is an exact lattice/Jordan/arithmetic bridge to the continuous \(Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)\) response spine, not an identification of Conway symmetry with \(Spin(2,3)\).

The selector boundary persists. Two positive determinant-fifteen copies have the wrong ternary discriminant type; a sign-reversed second summand produces the required hyperbolic module, but the orientation twist and affine theta characteristic are not selected by Leech data alone. The decisive gate is a microscopic seam whose reversal supplies both structures and intertwines the existing Golay sewing module. See [document 1217](../1200-1299/1217_leech_pair_gram_geometry_reaches_spin23_but_needs_an_orientation_twist_and_affine_theta_shift.md).

## 19 October 2026 — Outcome Dynamics closes one numerical artifact, not the physical bridge

The adjacent Outcome Dynamics programme has progressed from idealized source rows to a bonded GaAs active/support calculation with eight independent mechanical patterns, nine frequencies and stress, charge and optical outputs. The coarse 72-cell transfer retained rank eight and reciprocity, but large lateral-refinement changes correctly blocked physical admission. Fourth-order load integration and stress recovery were separately converged and did not remove the odd/even through-thickness response.

A genuine same-node element-order control now changes the active layer from linear to quadratic through-thickness interpolation. On the preregistered 1 MHz normal direction, the quadratic 7-to-9-node changes are 0.0331% in projected stress direction, 0.0949% in charge, 0.0852% in optical displacement and 0.1816% in generalized displacement. The linear comparison remains unstable at 7.29%, 7.33% and 13.24% for the three observables. The prior parity is therefore supported as an element-discretization artifact on this direction.

This advances the action-to-observable middle and supplies a useful numerical-admission rule for every response branch. It does not select an \(H_2(\mathbb R)\) compression or derive \(Spin(2,3)\). Mass formulation, consistent lateral/support refinement, the full eight-pattern frequency rerun, measured inputs and physical observation remain open. See [document 1218](../1200-1299/1218_outcome_dynamics_turns_the_stress_bridge_into_a_converged_numerical_gate_not_evidence_for_fundamental_spin23.md).

## 19 October 2026 — consistent mass converges locally but keeps the numerical gate open

Checkpoint 194 holds the quadratic element and physical model fixed while replacing row-sum-lumped mass with a full consistent mass matrix. The consistent-mass 7-to-9-node sequence passes the declared local convergence screens: 0.0965% in projected stress direction, 0.0635% in charge, 0.0504% in optical displacement and 0.2220% in generalized displacement.

That internal convergence does not close the representation gate. At nine nodes, lumped and consistent mass still differ by 8.54% in stress direction, 69.22% in charge, 72.21% in optical displacement and 64.92% in generalized displacement. The next valid calculation is therefore consistent-mass lateral and support refinement, not the full 72-cell rerun. Physical admission and every fundamental \(Spin(2,3)\) inference remain false. See [document 1219](../1200-1299/1219_outcome_dynamics_consistent_mass_converges_but_disagrees_with_lumped_mass_so_the_gate_remains_open.md).

## 19 October 2026 — the candidate-action blocker round separates wrong ontology, missing particularity and a real experiment

The A4 physical-state audit gives a split verdict. The displayed four-dimensional effective action remains blocked because its stationary vacuum, numerical Hessian, constraint cohomology, boundary domains and physical pole residues are not specified strongly enough to choose between healthy and ghostly completions. The unconstrained literal two-time subbranch already fails: nonzero second-time momentum gives \(m_{\rm eff}^2=m^2-\omega_2^2\) and destroys the ordinary one-time initial-value interpretation. The \(H_2(\mathbb R)\) response-geometry reading adds no second spacetime time and remains the recommended live ontology. See [document 1220](../1200-1299/1220_the_a4_physical_state_gate_blocks_the_typed_action_but_rejects_only_the_naive_two_time_reading.md).

The A5 selector question has an exact general boundary. If a selector is \(G\)-equivariant and its input is fixed by \(G\), its output must also be fixed. It therefore cannot select a point in a fixed-point-free orbit such as the complementary \(\mathbb{CP}^1\). Finite isotropic link multiplicity, symmetric probability and inner block signs do not repair this. A viable selector requires covariant anisotropy, a sector-limited or twisted structure, spontaneous breaking plus history, boundary data, or an anomaly/index. This proves a missing-asymmetry requirement, not a theological cause. See [document 1221](../1200-1299/1221_symmetric_data_cannot_select_a_nonfixed_orientation_and_a5_requires_a_covariant_selector_input.md).

The A11 target is now operational. Three controlled \(\mathbb Z_3\) sectors are compared using a first-harmonic amplitude transverse to a response plane frozen before labels are revealed. Synthetic controls verify zero transverse amplitude for rank-two null families, nonzero amplitude for a positive rank-three family, cancellation of sector-independent contact terms and preservation of rank and relative invariants under one common probe recalibration. The protocol does not yet predict an effect: without a microscopic defect-to-stress coupling and effect window, a null closes this realization but not every possible \(Spin(2,3)\) theory. See [document 1222](../1200-1299/1222_a_preregistered_z3_holonomy_response_test_can_separate_a_third_physical_direction_from_ambient_spin23_geometry.md).

## 19 October 2026 — 691 reaches the arithmetic carrier through the Klingen-Siegel tower

Nagaoka's congruence between the degree-three Klingen lift of Δ and the degree-three Siegel Eisenstein series modulo 691 descends under the Siegel operator to

\[
[\Delta]\equiv\mathcal G_{12}^{(2)}\pmod{691}.
\]

The middle stage is a modular form for (Sp(4,\mathbb Z)), so it lies directly on the arithmetic subgroup of the established (Sp(4,\mathbb R)\cong Spin_0(2,3)) carrier. The same Klingen-Siegel tower carries distinct mod-23 determinant-divisibility statements and meets the genus-two Leech theta series. This is an exact automorphic linkage, but it does not turn either prime into a physical constant or select the outstanding orientation seam. The next gate is a normalized genus-two coefficient/Hecke comparison on the Leech determinant strata, not further integer pattern matching. See [document 1223](../1200-1299/1223_the_prime_691_reaches_the_spin23_arithmetic_carrier_through_the_weight_twelve_klingen_siegel_tower.md).
